# Frontend subsystems

Reference detail for 6 subsystems. Entry point and cross-cutting analysis: [`../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md`](../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md).

Anchors are `file:line` as of 2026-09-22 and will drift; re-grep rather than assume a contradiction.

**Contents:** [`fe-core`](#1-fe-core---frontend-transport-routing-auth-state-and-build-config) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`fe-chat`](#2-fe-chat---frontend-chat-experience-and-landing-demo) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`fe-upload-archive`](#3-fe-upload-archive---frontend-upload-batch-upload-archive-and-novelty-pages) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`fe-admin-auth`](#4-fe-admin-auth---frontend-admin-console-settings-and-auth-flows) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`fe-design`](#5-fe-design---frontend-design-system-ui-primitives-and-3d-scenes) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`fe-quality`](#6-fe-quality---frontend-teste2e-harness-and-quality-gates)

## 1. `fe-core` - Frontend transport, routing, auth state and build config

This subsystem is the entire client-side contract with the backend and with Supabase: one axios instance that is the only transport to FastAPI, a React Router 8 route table split into a full-bleed tier (`/`, `/login`) and a shell tier (everything else), an AuthContext that turns a Supabase session plus the server-owned role-feature matrix into four boolean capabilities, a cross-tab idle-session state machine, and the Vite/nginx build plumbing that makes the dev proxy, the deterministic E2E mode and the production CSP work. It deliberately keeps the browser holding only the Supabase anon key: all role and department enforcement lives in Python, and everything here is a UX affordance layered on top of a backend that stays authoritative.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js` (450 lines) - The ONLY backend transport. Axios instance, request interceptor (JWT vs X-Guest-ID, dev backend-ready wait, E2E fixture branch), response interceptor (502/504 GET retry, single silent 401 refresh + sign-out, privileged-MFA announcement), and ~45 exported endpoint wrappers.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/App.jsx` (96 lines) - Two-tier route table. Outer <Routes> for Landing/Login + catch-all ShellRoutes; inner <Routes> inside <AppShell> with a per-route <RouteSuspense> and <ProtectedRoute>. Mounts <IdleSessionGuard/> once.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/main.jsx` (78 lines) - Provider tree and QueryClient defaults (staleTime 30s, gcTime 30min, the retry predicate that de-duplicates the interceptor's retries, refetchOnWindowFocus off) plus the ['departments'] select default and the E2E skipAnimations opt-in.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx` (303 lines) - Session sync, profile read, feature-matrix fetch, MFA assurance-level tracking, per-identity React Query cache eviction, realtime feature broadcast channel, and the derived can*/isAdmin surface.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx` (92 lines) - Guard with five ordered gates: loading skeleton, guest/allowGuest, needsMfa redirect, profileError/pending/rejected interstitials, then roles[] and isAllowed===false redirects to /dashboard.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/IdleSessionGuard.jsx` (204 lines) - Tiered idle logout state machine: 1s tick, localStorage-shared activity stamp, 60s warning modal with countdown ring, 12h absolute cap, ?next= preservation.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/idleSession.js` (63 lines) - Pure idle policy: IDLE_LIMIT_MS {privileged 15m, standard 30m}, IDLE_WARNING_MS 60s, ABSOLUTE_SESSION_MS 12h, the two localStorage keys, LOGIN_NEXT_PARAM='next', safeNextPath, loginPathWithNext, formatCountdown.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/permissions.js` (28 lines) - canUseFeature + DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES, the frozen mirror of routers/settings.py::DEFAULT_FEATURES.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.js` (89 lines) - Recognises the backend's aal2 refusal by lowercase substring 'multi-factor authentication is required' on a 403; a module-level listener Set; shouldPromptForPrivilegedMfa and requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn predicates.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/apiErrors.js` (41 lines) - apiErrorMessage / apiErrorStatus / isRetryableFailure. Re-exported through api.js so screens import error rules from one place.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/routePrefetch.js` (55 lines) - Hover/focus warm-up: per-segment chunk loaders duplicating App.jsx's lazy() import expressions, plus a per-segment list of query prefetches. Module-level `warmed` Set.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/vite.config.js` (130 lines) - backendReadiness() dev middleware, e2eApiGuard() (dev + preview), the spaAwareApiProxy HTML bypass, eleven proxy entries, the e2e define block, three dedupe, and the manualChunks policy.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/nginx/default.conf.template` (36 lines) - Production security headers including the envsubst-templated CSP, /healthz, SPA history fallback, and a 7d static-asset location that deliberately declares no add_header.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx` (106 lines) - Theme/palette/motion/effects/contrast persisted under 'isu-thesis-preferences-v2'; drives document.documentElement dataset + Material dynamic colour; PreferenceMotion wraps framer-motion MotionConfig.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/AppShell.jsx` (320 lines) - Three responsive navs built from one useNavItems() list, Ctrl/Cmd-K command palette, health poll, and the single mount point for <PrivilegedMfaGate/>.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/supabaseClient.js` (14 lines) - createClient from VITE_SUPABASE_URL/ANON_KEY; throws at module-evaluation time when either is missing.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/testing/e2eSession.js` (21 lines) - isE2ETestMode (MODE==='e2e') and the localStorage fixture reader/clearer keyed 'isu_e2e_auth_fixture'.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ErrorBoundary.jsx` (40 lines) - Single app-wide class boundary, outermost in main.jsx. No reset path except window.location.reload().
- `rag-thesis-frontend/Dockerfile` (25 lines) - Two-stage, digest-pinned node:24.18.0-alpine3.24 build -> nginx:1.31.3-alpine3.24; four VITE_ build args baked into the bundle; /healthz HEALTHCHECK on 8080.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/eslint.config.js` (61 lines) - Flat config. files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}'] only; react/jsx-no-undef as the JSX ReferenceError catcher; complexity and no-console are warnings.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` (153 lines) - Parses dist/index.html for module script + modulepreload + stylesheet hrefs, gzips level 9, enforces 330 kB eager budget, per-chunk caps, and that useSceneRuntime/OverviewCharts are absent from the eager set.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/vite-server.mjs` (45 lines) - Builds --mode e2e into dist-e2e and previews on 127.0.0.1:4173 strictPort; closes on the literal stdin line 'close'.

### Control flow

Every HTTP call leaves through one axios instance. The request interceptor (api.js:72-90) runs three branches in order. First, when `SHOULD_WAIT_FOR_DEV_BACKEND` — `import.meta.env.DEV && !import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL` (api.js:20) — it awaits `waitForBackend()`, which polls Vite's own `/__backend-ready` middleware (vite.config.js:17-31, which proxies a 1 s-timeout fetch to `http://localhost:8000/health` and always answers 200 `{ready}`) up to 40 times at 350 ms apart and then throws `'The backend did not become ready within 14 seconds.'`; concurrent callers collapse onto one `backendReadyPromise`, nulled in `.finally` (api.js:46-69). Second, in E2E mode it short-circuits entirely: a fixture with a `user` gets the literal `Authorization: Bearer deterministic-e2e-token`, otherwise `X-Guest-ID` (api.js:74-82) — no Supabase call at all. Third, the real path calls `supabase.auth.getSession()` on every single request and sets `Authorization: Bearer <access_token>`, or falls back to `X-Guest-ID: guestRateId()`. `guestRateId()` (api.js:25-40) mints a `crypto.randomUUID()` (or a 16-byte hex fallback, or throws a TypeError if neither exists) into `sessionStorage['iskai_guest_rate_id']`, so guest rate-limit identity is per browser *tab*, unlike the idle clocks which are localStorage and therefore per browser.

The response interceptor (api.js:111-158) is a fixed cascade. (1) Transient-gateway GET retry: method is GET, status ∈ {502, 504}, and `config.__transientGetRetries < 2`; it sets `backendReady = false`, increments the counter on the config object, sleeps `attempt * 350` ms (350 then 700), and re-issues via `api.request(config)` — which re-enters the request interceptor, so in dev it also re-waits on `/__backend-ready`, and so the Authorization header is re-stamped from the live session. 503 is deliberately excluded (api.js:13-17) because it means an explicit readiness/configuration failure. (2) 401: if `config.__authRetried` is unset and not E2E, it marks the flag and calls `refreshSessionOnce()` (api.js:100-108), a single collapsed `supabase.auth.refreshSession()` shared across every 401 in flight; on success the identical request is replayed once with the fresh token. If the refresh fails — or the request was already retried — it calls `supabase.auth.signOut()` and performs a **hard** `window.location.href = loginPathWithNext(pathname + search)`, discarding all React state. (3) `isPrivilegedMfaRefusal(error)` (403 whose `detail` lowercases to contain `'multi-factor authentication is required'`) fires `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()`, which walks a copy of a module-level listener Set; the error is still rejected so each caller keeps its own handling. Note the cascade is not exclusive: a 401 falls through the `window.location.href` assignment into the privileged-MFA check and then `Promise.reject(error)`.

React Query sits above that and is configured to not duplicate it. `main.jsx:131-149`: `staleTime` 30 s, `gcTime` 30 min, `refetchOnWindowFocus: false`, and a retry predicate that returns false for any status `< 500` or exactly 503 (authn/authz/validation/readiness need human action), false for 502/504 (`INTERCEPTOR_RETRIED_STATUSES`, because the interceptor already burned two attempts and retrying here turned one failed GET into up to six requests), and otherwise `failureCount < 1` — so a 500 or a reply-less network error gets exactly one React Query retry. `queryClient.setQueryDefaults(['departments'], {select: normalizeDepartments})` (main.jsx:153) tolerates both the legacy bare array and the versioned `{departments: []}` shape.

Auth state resolves in `AuthProvider`. On mount (AuthContext.jsx:178-199) it calls `supabase.auth.getSession()` once and registers `onAuthStateChange`, explicitly dropping the `INITIAL_SESSION` event because `getSession()` already covered it — without that, every page load ran the profile read and the feature fetch twice. `syncSession` (AuthContext.jsx:139-159) is strictly ordered: `forgetPreviousAccount(nextUserId)` first (AuthContext.jsx:72-80 — it compares against `identityRef`, skips the `undefined` first resolution so a fresh tab's landing prefetches survive, and otherwise calls `queryClient.clear()`, which also cancels in-flight requests so they cannot land in the new identity's cache); then `await checkMfa(currentUser)`; then `setUser`; then `await Promise.all([fetchProfile(id), loadFeatures()])` — awaited on purpose, because fetching features without awaiting left `features === null` on the first render after sign-in, every `can*` false, and `ProtectedRoute` bounced students and faculty off `/chat`, `/archive`, `/novelty`, `/upload` to `/dashboard` on any hard refresh; only then `setLoading(false)`. `fetchProfile` reads `profiles` directly through the anon-key client (role, full_name, email, department, status, avatar_url) and sets `profileError` on any failure. `checkMfa` reads `mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel()` and sets `needsMfa = nextLevel === 'aal2' && nextLevel !== currentLevel`; reaching `aal2` also zeroes `privilegedMfaRefusals`. `signOut()` clears the cache twice — before the network round trip and again after — because a still-mounted query observer refetches with the old token the moment its data is removed.

Capabilities are derived at AuthContext.jsx:262-265 as `canUseFeature(role, features, name)`: admin/superadmin are unconditionally true; otherwise the server policy is consulted, and a `null` policy (fetch failed, or signed out) falls back to `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` rather than denying — an empty `{}` is treated as a real deny-everything policy. A second, realtime path keeps this current: while a user exists, a Supabase Broadcast channel `global_feature_updates` listens for `features_updated` and re-runs `loadFeatures()` (AuthContext.jsx:202-220); superadmins publish through `broadcastFeatureUpdate()`.

`ProtectedRoute` evaluates in a fixed order (ProtectedRoute.jsx:118-197): `loading` -> `<RouteSkeleton/>` (which itself reads `useLocation()` and picks a per-segment skeleton shape, PageSkeleton.jsx:290-294); no `user` -> children if `allowGuest`, else `<Navigate to="/login" replace/>`; `needsMfa` -> `/login`; `profileError` / `isPending` / `isRejected` -> full interstitials with a Reload or Sign out button; then `roles && !roles.includes(role)` -> `/dashboard`; then the strict `isAllowed === false` -> `/dashboard`. The strictness matters: `isAllowed={undefined}` passes.

The idle machine (IdleSessionGuard.jsx) is `active = Boolean(user) && !needsMfa && !isE2ETestMode`. When inactive its effect removes both localStorage keys and resets `expiringRef`. When active it seeds `isu-session-started-at` only if absent (so the 12 h cap is measured from the first login, not this tab's mount), calls `recordActivity()` once, resolves `limitMs` from `idleLimitForRole(isAdmin)` (15 min privileged / 30 min standard), and installs a 1 s `setInterval` plus passive listeners for `pointerdown`, `keydown`, `wheel`, `touchstart` — mousemove is deliberately excluded — and a `visibilitychange` handler that ticks immediately on return to the tab. Each tick reads the freshest of the in-memory ref and the shared `isu-idle-last-activity` stamp, checks the absolute cap first, then computes `remaining = lastActivity + limitMs - now`: `<= 0` expires with reason `'idle'`, `<= 60_000` sets `secondsLeft` (driving the modal and the depleting SVG ring), otherwise clears it. `recordActivity` throttles writes to once per second and always clears the warning. `expire` is idempotent via `expiringRef`, removes both keys, awaits `signOut()`, then `navigate(loginPathWithNext(pathname+search), {replace:true})` reading the route through `locationRef` — a ref specifically so `expire` keeps a stable identity, because listing `location` as a dependency previously rebuilt `expire` on every navigation, which tore down and reinstalled the interval and all four listeners and reset the idle clock with no real input. Reasons `'absolute'` and `'idle'` raise distinct toasts; `'manual'` (the modal's Sign out now) raises none. `Login.jsx:105` reads the destination back through `safeNextPath(searchParams.get(LOGIN_NEXT_PARAM))` and navigates there at `Login.jsx:202` — the single-source-of-truth for the `next` key exists because the 401 handler once wrote `returnTo` while Login read `next`, silently dropping every return trip.

In dev, `spaAwareApiProxy` (vite.config.js:40-48) resolves the three paths that are simultaneously React routes and API prefixes: its `bypass(req)` returns `/index.html` when `req.method === 'GET' && req.headers.accept?.includes('text/html')`, so a browser navigation to `/chat`, `/upload`, `/upload/batch` or `/settings` stays inside Vite and hits the SPA history fallback, while axios (whose default Accept is `application/json, text/plain, */*`) and every multipart POST proxy through to :8000. In E2E, `--mode e2e` rewrites `VITE_API_URL` to `/__e2e_api`, points Supabase at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/__e2e_supabase`, blanks the Turnstile key, and installs `e2eApiGuard()` on both the dev and the **preview** server so an unmocked call answers a clean 501 instead of falling through to the SPA fallback and returning 200 index.html as a confusing JSON parse error. `e2e/vite-server.mjs` builds into `dist-e2e` (never `dist`, which the bundle budget measures) and previews on 127.0.0.1:4173 with `strictPort`.

In production the app is static files behind nginx. `nginx/default.conf.template:13` emits the CSP through envsubst with `${API_ORIGIN}`, `${SUPABASE_ORIGIN}`, `${SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN}` and `${DOLLAR}` supplied by `docker-compose.operations.yml:58-61` (where `DOLLAR: $$` becomes a literal `$` so nginx's own `$uri` survives templating). The directives are: `default-src 'self'`; `base-uri 'self'`; `object-src 'none'`; `frame-ancestors 'none'`; `form-action 'self'`; `script-src 'self' https://challenges.cloudflare.com`; `style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline' https://fonts.googleapis.com`; `img-src 'self' data: blob: ${SUPABASE_ORIGIN}`; `font-src 'self' data: https://fonts.gstatic.com`; `connect-src 'self' ${API_ORIGIN} ${SUPABASE_ORIGIN} ${SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN} https://challenges.cloudflare.com`; `frame-src https://challenges.cloudflare.com`; `upgrade-insecure-requests`. Alongside it: `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `Permissions-Policy: camera=(), microphone=(), geolocation=(), payment=(), usb=()`, `Strict-Transport-Security: max-age=31536000; includeSubDomains`, all with `always`.

### Public surface

- src/api.js — `const api = axios.create({ baseURL: import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL || '', timeout: 180000 })` (api.js:8-11). 180 s default because embedding-heavy calls run inline.
- src/api.js:161 `healthCheck()` -> GET /health -> `{status, checks:{api,database}, version}` (main.py:278).
- src/api.js:167 `chatQuery(query, session_id=null, department_filter=null, guest_history=[], guest_source_ids=[], signal=undefined, turnstileToken=null, thesis_category_filter=null, edit_from_turn=null, conversation_replies=[])` -> POST /chat. Ten positional parameters; `turnstileToken` is sent as the `X-Turnstile-Token` header only when truthy.
- src/api.js:197-212 sessions: `getSessions()` GET /sessions; `renameSession(id,title)` PUT /sessions/{id}; `deleteSession(id)` DELETE /sessions/{id}; `getSessionMessages(id)` GET /sessions/{id}/messages.
- src/api.js:215 `listPapers(department=null, thesisCategory=null)` -> GET /papers[?department=&thesis_category=]; src/api.js:224 `deletePaper(id)` DELETE /papers/{id}.
- src/api.js:228 `getPublicSettings()` GET /settings/public (unauthenticated-safe: returns only `{evaluation_department}`); src/api.js:232 `getTracks()` GET /upload/tracks, returns `data.tracks`.
- src/api.js:238 `uploadPaper({file,title,authors,year,abstract,track,department,program_id,specialization_id,thesis_category,idempotencyKey})` -> POST /upload/paper multipart; department defaults to 'CCSICT', thesis_category to 'student'; sends `Idempotency-Key` when supplied.
- src/api.js:261 `getUploadStatus(jobId)` GET /upload/status/{id}; :265 `cancelUploadJob(jobId, reason='')` POST /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel `{reason: reason||null}`; :313 `getUploadJobs(ids)` GET /upload/jobs?ids=<comma-joined>.
- src/api.js:269 `extractMetadata(file)` POST /upload/extract-metadata; :282 `extractMetadataBatch(files)` POST /upload/batch/extract-metadata with a 10-minute timeout; :298 `uploadBatch({files, rows, defaults, onUploadProgress})` POST /upload/batch with a 15-minute timeout, `rows` JSON-stringified into the form.
- src/api.js:319-341 duplication: `scanDuplication(file, department=null)` POST /duplication/scan; `getScanHistory()`; `deleteScanHistory(scanId)`; `clearScanHistory()` DELETE /duplication/history; `scanDuplicationChat(scanId, question)` POST /duplication/chat.
- src/api.js:344-382 analytics: `getPublicSummary()` GET /analytics/summary; `getAnalyticsOverview()`; `getRecentActivity(limit=25)`; `updateMyProfile(payload)` PUT /analytics/me; `listUsers()` GET /analytics/users; `updateUserRole(userId, payload)` PUT /analytics/users/{id}/role — accepts a bare string and wraps it as `{role}`; `updateUserDetails(userId,payload)`; `deleteUser(userId)`; `getSystemLogs(limit=200)`.
- src/api.js:395-418 maintenance (superadmin): `getOperationsSummary()`, `getIngestionWorkers()` (asserts `workers` array), `getOperationalJobs(limit=100)` (asserts `jobs`), `getOperationalAlerts(limit=100)` (asserts `alerts`), `acknowledgeOperationalAlert(alertId)`, `getRetentionReport()`. `requireOperationsObject` (api.js:385) throws TypeError on a non-object body or a missing array field.
- src/api.js:421-436 catalog/departments: `getDepartments()` GET /catalog/departments/legacy then `normalizeDepartments(data)`; `createDepartment` POST /departments/ (note the trailing slash); `updateDepartment`/`deleteDepartment` on /departments/{id}.
- src/api.js:439-445 `getFeaturePermissions()` GET /settings/features; `updateFeaturePermissions(payload)` PUT /settings/features.
- src/api.js:450 re-exports `apiErrorMessage`, `apiErrorStatus`, `isRetryableFailure` from ./lib/apiErrors.js.
- src/context/AuthContext.jsx:238-294 `useAuth()` value: `{user, profile, role, department, status, loading, needsMfa, profileError, isPending, isRejected, privilegedMfaRequired, privilegedMfaRefusals, refreshMfa(), satisfyMfa(), refreshProfile(), reloadSession(), isAdmin, isSuperadmin, isFaculty, isStudent, features, canChat, canArchive, canScan, canUpload, displayName, avatarUrl, signOut(), broadcastFeatureUpdate()}`.
- src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx:118 `<ProtectedRoute roles?={string[]} isAllowed?={boolean} allowGuest?={boolean}>`.
- src/lib/permissions.js:10 `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` (frozen, two roles only: student {chat,archive}=true, faculty adds novelty); :24 `canUseFeature(role, features, feature)` — admin/superadmin short-circuit true.
- src/lib/idleSession.js: `IDLE_LIMIT_MS`, `IDLE_WARNING_MS`, `ABSOLUTE_SESSION_MS`, `IDLE_LAST_ACTIVITY_KEY='isu-idle-last-activity'`, `IDLE_SESSION_START_KEY='isu-session-started-at'`, `idleLimitForRole(isPrivileged)`, `safeNextPath(path, fallback='/dashboard')`, `LOGIN_NEXT_PARAM='next'`, `loginPathWithNext(here)`, `formatCountdown(ms)`.
- src/lib/privilegedMfa.js: `isPrivilegedMfaRefusal(error)`, `onPrivilegedMfaRequired(listener) -> unsubscribe`, `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()`, `resetPrivilegedMfaListeners()` (test seam), `shouldPromptForPrivilegedMfa({refusals,dismissedAt,factorsLoading,enrolling})`, `requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn({isPrivileged,totpEnrolled})`.
- src/lib/keys.js: `contentKeys(values, prefix='item')`, `slotKeys(count, prefix)`.
- src/lib/routePrefetch.js:236 `prefetchRoute(pathname, queryClient, {signedIn=true})`.
- src/testing/e2eSession.js: `isE2ETestMode`, `readE2EAuthFixture()`, `clearE2EAuthFixture()`, `E2E_AUTH_STORAGE_KEY='isu_e2e_auth_fixture'`.
- Route table (App.jsx). Tier 1, no shell, lazy behind one shared `<Suspense fallback={<BootFallback/>}>`: `/` -> Landing, `/login` -> Login. Tier 2, `*` -> ShellRoutes, each element wrapped in its own `<RouteSuspense fallback={<RouteSkeleton/>}>`: `/dashboard` ProtectedRoute (any signed-in); `/archive` isAllowed=canArchive; `/chat` isAllowed=canChat + allowGuest; `/novelty` isAllowed=canScan; `/upload` isAllowed=canUpload; `/upload/batch` isAllowed=canUpload; `/admin` roles=['admin','superadmin']; `/settings` ProtectedRoute (any signed-in); `*` -> NotFound with NO guard (guests reach it).
- Vite dev proxy (vite.config.js:93-105), all to http://localhost:8000: `/upload` and `/chat` and `/settings` via `spaAwareApiProxy` (changeOrigin + HTML bypass); `/papers`, `/health`, `/sessions`, `/duplication`, `/analytics`, `/catalog`, `/maintenance`, `/departments` as plain string targets.
- npm scripts (package.json:177-188): `dev`, `build`, `lint` (`eslint .`), `test` (`node --test`), `test:coverage` (lines 85 / functions 85 / branches 80, lcov to coverage/lcov.info), `test:e2e` (`node e2e/run-playwright.mjs`), `test:e2e:install`, `preview`, `bundle:budget`, `security:headers <url>`.

### Invariants

- src/api.js is the ONLY module that talks to the FastAPI backend. Every page imports its wrappers; nothing else constructs an axios instance or calls fetch against the API.
- A request carries EITHER `Authorization: Bearer <supabase jwt>` OR `X-Guest-ID`, never both, decided per-request from the live session (api.js:83-88). The backend CORS allow-list must contain both header names plus `Idempotency-Key` and `X-Turnstile-Token` (main.py:196) or the preflight fails.
- Automatic transport-level retry is GET-only and limited to 502/504, at most twice. 503 is explicitly NOT retried. React Query must never retry a status the interceptor already handled — `INTERCEPTOR_RETRIED_STATUSES` (main.jsx:129) has to stay equal to `TRANSIENT_GATEWAY_STATUSES` (api.js:17).
- At most ONE silent token refresh per request, enforced by the `config.__authRetried` flag, and at most one refresh in flight globally, enforced by the `sessionRefresh` promise (api.js:92-108).
- The post-login destination key is `next` and is owned by src/lib/idleSession.js. Both writers (api.js:146 and IdleSessionGuard.jsx:90) call `loginPathWithNext`; the sole reader (Login.jsx:105) calls `safeNextPath`. idleSession.test.js:66 pins that all three go through this module.
- `safeNextPath` accepts only paths starting with a single `/`; `loginPathWithNext` additionally refuses `/` and anything starting with `/login` so re-login cannot loop.
- Permissions must resolve BEFORE `loading` clears — `syncSession` awaits `Promise.all([fetchProfile, loadFeatures])` (AuthContext.jsx:152) — or ProtectedRoute bounces legitimate users to /dashboard on every hard refresh.
- `queryClient.clear()` must run before a different `user.id` is published to any consumer. `forgetPreviousAccount` is called as the first statement of `syncSession` (AuthContext.jsx:143) and again at the top of `signOut` (AuthContext.jsx:281), with a third clear after the sign-out round trip (AuthContext.jsx:289).
- `canUseFeature` treats `features === null` as 'policy unresolved -> apply the documented server defaults', and `{}` as a real deny-all policy (permissions.js:24-28). `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` is `Object.freeze`d at both levels and pinned by permissions.test.js:45.
- `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` must stay byte-equivalent to `rag-thesis-backend/routers/settings.py:19` `DEFAULT_FEATURES`. Verified: both are student {chat:T, archive:T, novelty:F, upload:F} and faculty {chat:T, archive:T, novelty:T, upload:F}.
- The privileged-MFA refusal is matched as a lowercase SUBSTRING `'multi-factor authentication is required'` on a 403 only (privilegedMfa.js:44-51). The backend wording (`PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL`) is pinned by tests/test_auth_authorization.py; rewording it silently breaks the in-place recovery.
- An aal1 privileged session is NEVER recovered by routing back to /login — that mints another aal1 session and loops. `mfaBypass` is deliberately left alone by the refusal handler (AuthContext.jsx:226-236) and PrivilegedMfaGate collects the factor in place. Pinned by privilegedMfa.test.js:137.
- One session = one idle clock, shared across tabs via localStorage. Every tick trusts `Math.max(in-memory ref, shared stamp)` (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:138-139).
- `ABSOLUTE_SESSION_MS` (12 h) must exceed every `IDLE_LIMIT_MS` value; pinned by idleSession.test.js:24.
- `expire` must keep a stable function identity — the current route is read through `locationRef`, never a dependency (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:68-73, 89).
- A new backend router prefix needs a matching `server.proxy` entry in vite.config.js or the dev server 404s it. Current backend prefixes are /upload /chat /papers /sessions /duplication /analytics /departments /catalog /settings /maintenance plus app-level /health, /health/worker, /ready — all except /ready are proxied.
- Any path that is BOTH a React route and an API prefix must use `spaAwareApiProxy`, not a bare target string. Today that is exactly /chat, /upload and /settings.
- E2E builds go to `dist-e2e`; `dist` is reserved for the production build the bundle budget measures (e2e/vite-server.mjs:68).
- `useSceneRuntime` and `OverviewCharts` must never appear in the eager payload, and the eager gzipped total must stay <= 330 kB (check-bundle-budget.mjs:37-49). The budget script fails hard if it parses zero eager assets, so a build-output format change cannot silently pass.
- The nginx template must declare no `add_header` inside a `location` block — doing so suppresses inheritance of every server-scope security header (documented at nginx/default.conf.template:32-33). `tests/test_operations_security.py:282` asserts `${API_ORIGIN}` and `${SUPABASE_ORIGIN}` are still present in the template.
- index.html's Google Fonts `<link>` origins (fonts.googleapis.com for the stylesheet, fonts.gstatic.com for the woff2) must stay in step with the CSP's `style-src`/`font-src` — otherwise faces are blocked only behind the production proxy while every dev and e2e run looks correct.

### Data model

- Supabase `profiles` row, read directly by the browser with the anon key (AuthContext.jsx:112-116): `{role, full_name, email, department, status, avatar_url}`. This is the ONLY table the frontend touches directly; everything else goes through FastAPI.
- Role-feature matrix: `{ student: {chat,archive,novelty,upload}, faculty: {…} }` booleans, from GET /settings/features. Mirrored client-side as `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` (permissions.js:10) against `routers/settings.py:19` `DEFAULT_FEATURES`; the server validates PUT payloads to exactly those two roles and four feature names (routers/settings.py:27-36).
- AuthContext state: `user`, `profile`, `loading`, `features`, `profileError`, `needsMfa`, `mfaBypass` (never persisted), `privilegedMfaRefusals` (a counter, not a flag, so a dismissed prompt can be re-raised), plus `identityRef` (undefined = unresolved, null = signed out) and `broadcastChannelRef`.
- Derived context fields: `role = profile?.role ?? null`; `department = profile?.department ?? 'CCSICT'`; `status = profile?.status ?? (user ? 'unavailable' : 'approved')`; `needsMfa` exposed as `needsMfa && !mfaBypass`; `privilegedMfaRequired = privilegedMfaRefusals > 0`.
- Browser storage keys: localStorage `isu-idle-last-activity`, `isu-session-started-at`, `isu-thesis-preferences-v2`, `isu_e2e_auth_fixture` (e2e only), `isu_e2e_skip_animations` (e2e only), `isu_e2e_turnstile` (e2e only); sessionStorage `iskai_guest_rate_id`, `iskai_guest_gate_ok`.
- Preferences shape (PreferencesContext.jsx:7-13): `{theme:'system'|'light'|'dark', palette:'isu', motion:'system'|'reduced', effects:'balanced', contrast:'standard'|'high'}`, projected onto `documentElement.dataset.{theme,palette,motion,effects,contrast}` + the `dark` class + `style.colorScheme`.
- React Query keys touched here: `['health']` (AppShell, 30 s refetchInterval, `retry:false`), `['departments']` (has a global `select`), `['papers']`, `['tracks']`, `['public-settings']`, `['sessions']`, `['mfa-factors']`. All unscoped by identity — which is precisely why `forgetPreviousAccount` calls `queryClient.clear()`.
- Vite build-time constants: `VITE_API_URL`, `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`, `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` (the last resolved by a two-directory `loadEnv`: frontend-local always, root-level only for `mode === 'production'`, vite.config.js:73-76).

### Config knobs

- VITE_API_URL — axios baseURL; also disables the dev /__backend-ready wait when set (api.js:20)
- VITE_SUPABASE_URL / VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY — required; missing either throws at module load (supabaseClient.js:6)
- VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY — resolved by a two-directory loadEnv: frontend-local always, root-level only for mode==='production'; forced to '' in e2e (vite.config.js:73-83)
- mode — 'e2e' swaps VITE_API_URL to /__e2e_api, Supabase to http://127.0.0.1:4173/__e2e_supabase with a fixed anon key, and installs e2eApiGuard on dev + preview
- import.meta.env.DEV — half of SHOULD_WAIT_FOR_DEV_BACKEND
- localStorage isu_e2e_skip_animations='1' — e2e-only, jumps framer-motion to final frames for the accessibility matrix (main.jsx:122)
- localStorage isu_e2e_turnstile='1' — e2e-only, fabricates a Turnstile site key (security/turnstileConfig.js:4)
- localStorage isu_e2e_auth_fixture — the deterministic {user, profile, features, needsMfa} E2E session
- nginx envsubst: API_ORIGIN, SUPABASE_ORIGIN, SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN, DOLLAR
- Docker build args: VITE_API_URL, VITE_SUPABASE_URL, VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY
- PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER=1 — set by e2e/run-playwright.mjs so Playwright reuses the already-started preview server
- CI env var — flips Playwright to 2 workers, forbidOnly, and 2 retries (playwright.config.js:14-16)
- Bundle budget constants in scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs: EAGER_BUDGET_KB=330, LAZY_CHUNK_BUDGETS_KB {useSceneRuntime:250, OverviewCharts:115}, DEFAULT_CHUNK_BUDGET_KB=150
- Coverage thresholds in package.json test:coverage: lines 85 / functions 85 / branches 80

### Error handling

- 502/504 on a GET: silently retried twice by the interceptor at 350 ms then 700 ms, then surfaced. React Query does not retry them again.
- 503: never retried anywhere — treated as an explicit readiness/configuration failure requiring human action (api.js:13-17, main.jsx:142).
- 401: one silent `supabase.auth.refreshSession()` + replay; on failure `signOut()` and a hard `window.location.href = '/login?next=<path>'`.
- 403 whose detail contains 'multi-factor authentication is required': `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` notifies every subscriber, AuthContext increments `privilegedMfaRefusals`, PrivilegedMfaGate opens a modal offering either the TOTP challenge (if a verified factor exists) or in-place enrolment; the original promise still rejects so callers keep their own handling.
- Other 4xx: rejected to the caller. `isRetryableFailure` returns false, so screens do not offer a Retry button that cannot work (apiErrors.js:155-158).
- 5xx and reply-less transport failures: `isRetryableFailure` true; React Query retries once.
- `apiErrorMessage` unwraps FastAPI's `detail` (string verbatim, or a validation list flattened to `loc.join('.'): msg`), then `data.message`, then `error.message` when no response arrived, then the caller's fallback.
- Operations endpoints additionally throw a TypeError client-side when the body is not an object or the expected array field is missing (api.js:385-393).
- Supabase profile read failure -> `profileError` -> ProtectedRoute renders a 'Profile temporarily unavailable' interstitial with a Reload button; access is paused rather than degraded.
- Feature-matrix fetch failure -> `features` stays null -> `canUseFeature` falls back to DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES so a transient error cannot lock a student out of chat; the server still enforces the real gate.
- `profile.status === 'pending'` / `'rejected'` -> dedicated interstitials with a Sign out button (ProtectedRoute.jsx:153, :177).
- Any uncaught render error -> the app-wide ErrorBoundary card; `componentDidCatch` logs to console.error and there is no telemetry sink.
- Dev backend unavailable -> `waitForBackend()` throws a plain Error after 14 s; the message reaches the user through `apiErrorMessage`'s no-response branch.
- e2e: an unmocked call hits the `/__e2e_api` middleware and returns 501 `{detail:'Unmocked E2E API request'}` rather than falling through to index.html.

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/idleSession.test.js — 7 tests: privileged < standard limits, the 12 h cap outliving every idle limit, safeNextPath rejecting off-origin and protocol-relative paths, loginPathWithNext keeping/omitting destinations, formatCountdown never going negative, and (line 66) a source-level assertion that api.js, IdleSessionGuard.jsx and Login.jsx all route the `next` key through this module.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/permissions.test.js — 5 tests: admins bypass the policy, a null policy falls back to the documented server defaults, a real policy always wins, unknown/absent roles are denied, and DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES is frozen.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.test.js — 13 tests: the refusal is recognised across casing and an added clause, ordinary 403s and non-403s are left alone, the listener Set survives a self-unsubscribing and a throwing subscriber, dismissal silences only that refusal, the prompt waits for the factor lookup and yields to enrolment, requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn's two-condition truth table, and (line 137) a source-level assertion that no screen recovers an aal1 session by routing to /login.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/apiErrors.test.js — 6 tests pinning the detail-unwrapping order and that Retry is offered only for status 0 or >= 500.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/catalog.test.js — normalizeDepartments across the legacy array and versioned object shapes.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js — 11 Playwright tests over the built dist-e2e bundle: signed-out redirect to /login, guest appearance controls not redirecting, guest RAG answer surviving a hard refresh, guest retry-after-failure and stop-without-losing-the-question, archive filtering, faculty novelty metrics, the admin durable-upload resume across a refresh, batch upload polling, and the superadmin operations view. Each test declares its own page.route handlers; anything unhandled returns 501 and is collected as `unexpected`.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js and visual-quality.spec.js — the axe matrix (the consumer of the isu_e2e_skip_animations flag) and Material surface contrast.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_operations_security.py:282 — asserts the nginx template still contains ${API_ORIGIN} and ${SUPABASE_ORIGIN}.
- CI gates (.github/workflows/quality.yml:175-207): npm run lint, npm run test:coverage (85/85/80), npm run build, npm run bundle:budget, then npm run test:e2e with a cached Chromium.
- NOT covered by any test: src/api.js's interceptor chain itself (no unit test mounts axios), AuthContext's syncSession ordering and cache-eviction logic, ProtectedRoute's gate ordering, and IdleSessionGuard's tick/expire machine — only the pure helpers underneath them are pinned.

### Gotchas

- A guest who trips ANY 401 is hard-navigated to /login. `routers/chat.py:2117` returns 401 with 'Guest conversations do not have saved sessions.' — a request-shape error. In the interceptor a guest has no session, so `refreshSessionOnce()` resolves false, `supabase.auth.signOut()` is a no-op, and `window.location.href = '/login?next=/chat'` fires (api.js:141-146). A guest is sent to a sign-in page for a bug they cannot act on, losing the whole conversation. The 401 branch has no `if (session)` precondition.
- `window.location.href` on 401 is a full document navigation, so everything React held is lost — the exact state the silent-refresh comment (api.js:128-132) was written to protect. It only protects the refreshable case.
- AuthProvider's context `value` is a plain object literal rebuilt on every render (AuthContext.jsx:238) with an inline `signOut` arrow. That makes `signOut` unstable, which makes `expire` unstable (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:81-102, deps `[signOut, navigate]`), which makes the main idle effect re-run — tearing down the 1 s interval and all four activity listeners and calling `recordActivity()`, resetting the idle clock with no real input. This is the *same* class of bug the `locationRef` comment says was fixed for `location`; the `signOut` half is still open. The realtime `features_updated` broadcast calls `setFeatures` (AuthContext.jsx:209), so any admin toggling permissions resets the idle timer for every signed-in user in the deployment.
- Because the context value is never memoized, every `useAuth()` consumer (AppShell, every nav item, ProtectedRoute, IdleSessionGuard, PrivilegedMfaGate) re-renders on every AuthProvider render.
- `normalizeDepartments` runs twice on the same payload: once inside `getDepartments()` (api.js:423) and again via the `['departments']` query default `select` (main.jsx:153). Idempotent, so harmless, but one of the two is redundant.
- The manual `'Content-Type': 'multipart/form-data'` headers on `uploadPaper`, `extractMetadata`, `extractMetadataBatch` and `uploadBatch` are dead. axios 1.18.1 unsets Content-Type for browser FormData at `lib/helpers/resolveConfig.js:85` so the browser can attach the boundary. `scanDuplication` (api.js:319) omits the header and behaves identically — the inconsistency is cosmetic but misleading.
- `backendReady = false` is assigned on every 502/504 GET (api.js:121) even in production, where `SHOULD_WAIT_FOR_DEV_BACKEND` is false and nothing ever reads the flag. Dead state mutation outside dev.
- When the dev backend is down, `waitForBackend()` rejects after 14 s with a no-`response` Error; React Query's predicate sees `status === undefined` and retries once, and the interceptor waits another full 14 s. A single query therefore takes ~28 s to finally fail in dev with FastAPI stopped.
- `routePrefetch`'s module-level `warmed` Set adds the segment BEFORE the `signedIn` check (routePrefetch.js:239-241). A signed-out visitor hovering the /chat nav item (guests see it) permanently marks 'chat' warmed, so after they sign in the `['public-settings']` and `['sessions']` prefetches never run. The Set is also never cleared on identity change, so a second account in the same tab gets no prefetching at all.
- `routePrefetch.chunkLoaders` duplicates the dynamic-import expressions from App.jsx by hand (routePrefetch.js:197-205). It has no entry for `/upload/batch`, and any App.jsx path rename silently degrades the prefetch to a no-op rather than erroring.
- The nav-visibility idiom `canChat !== false` (AppShell.jsx:25-28) is vestigial: `canUseFeature` returns `Boolean(...)`, never undefined, so `!== false` is exactly `=== true`. Reads as if a tri-state existed.
- `<Routes location={location} key={location.pathname}>` (App.jsx:39) is leftover from the removed AnimatePresence wrapper described in the comment above it. The explicit `location` prop and the `key` now only force a remount of the Routes subtree — including each route's Suspense boundary — on every navigation.
- `src/supabaseClient.js:6` throws during module evaluation when the Vite env vars are missing. That happens before React mounts, so `ErrorBoundary` never sees it: the user gets a blank page and the carefully written remediation message appears only in the console.
- `ErrorBoundary` is a single app-wide boundary (main.jsx:157) with no reset-on-navigation. Once `hasError` is true the entire app — shell included — is replaced by the error card until a full reload. A render crash in one page takes down navigation with it.
- `PreferencesContext.jsx:72` calls `localStorage.setItem` with no try/catch, unlike `readPreferences` which is guarded. In a browser with site data blocked, the first preferences effect throws inside a `useEffect` and trips the app-wide ErrorBoundary.
- Guest identity is `sessionStorage`-scoped (api.js:26) while the idle clock is `localStorage`-scoped. Two tabs are two guests for backend rate limiting but one session for idle logout.
- Mounting a new tab calls `recordActivity()` unconditionally (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:131), writing a fresh shared stamp — so opening a tab resets the idle clock for every other tab, and a background tab that is merely open can indefinitely defer the 30-minute logout of the tab the user actually walked away from.
- `isu-session-started-at` survives a browser close (it is only removed on sign-out or expiry). Reopening more than 12 h later fires `expire('absolute')` on the first tick even though the Supabase refresh token may still be valid — intended, but surprising.
- `connect-src` depends on `${SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN}` being supplied separately from `${SUPABASE_ORIGIN}` (docker-compose.operations.yml:60). If it is unset, envsubst emits an empty string, the CSP stays syntactically valid, and the `global_feature_updates` realtime channel (AuthContext.jsx:204) is silently blocked — feature-permission changes then never propagate without a reload, with no console clue beyond the CSP violation.
- The FastAPI middleware sets `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` (main.py:167) while nginx sets `strict-origin-when-cross-origin` (nginx/default.conf.template:16). Different surfaces so not a conflict, but `scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs:166` asserts the nginx value, so pointing it at the API origin always fails.
- `eslint.config.js` scopes every rule to `files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}']`. The `.mjs` files — `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs`, `scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs`, `e2e/run-playwright.mjs`, `e2e/vite-server.mjs` — match no config object and are therefore effectively unlinted by `npm run lint`. `globals` is `globals.browser` only, so `process` would be `no-undef` in any `.js` build script added later.
- `npm run lint` is `eslint .` with no `--max-warnings 0`, and `complexity: ['warn', 24]`, `max-depth: ['warn', 5]`, `no-console: ['warn', …]` are warnings. CI (.github/workflows/quality.yml:175) runs the same command, so those three rules cannot fail the build — unlike the backend's Pylint gate, which fails on any message.
- `spaAwareApiProxy`'s bypass is Accept-header based, so a browser navigation to a real API path such as `/upload/tracks` or `/settings/public` renders the SPA (and then NotFound) instead of JSON. Only affects manual URL poking in dev.
- `vite.config.js:81-83` is mis-indented inside the `define` block (the `import.meta.env.VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` key sits at column 0). Purely cosmetic, but it reads as a merge artefact.
- `backendReadiness()` is registered as a plugin in every mode (vite.config.js:79) but only implements `configureServer`, so `/__backend-ready` does not exist on the preview server. Harmless today because `SHOULD_WAIT_FOR_DEV_BACKEND` is false for any build, but the pairing is not obvious.
- `chatQuery` takes ten positional arguments (api.js:167-178). Any caller that omits a middle one has to pass `null` placeholders; inserting a parameter is a silent breaking change at every call site.
- `updateUserRole` accepts either a bare string or an object (api.js:367) — a compatibility shim for an older call shape that no longer has a documented reader.
- The `/settings` route is guarded only by `<ProtectedRoute>` (App.jsx:67), i.e. any signed-in user, while the `/settings` API prefix is authenticated and its PUT is superadmin-only. Same string, two entirely different authorization rules.
- `prefetchRoute` deliberately prefetches nothing for `novelty` and `admin` (routePrefetch.js:218-223) because a speculative request there can only produce a 403 — but hovering those items still pre-warms the chunk and still marks the segment `warmed`.
- `RouteSkeleton` keys off the FIRST path segment (PageSkeleton.jsx:292), so `/upload/batch` renders the single-upload skeleton.

### Open questions

- Is the guest-401 hard redirect (routers/chat.py:2117 -> api.js:141-146) intentional? A guest sending a `session_id` is a client bug, not an authentication failure, and the current handling signs them out of a session they never had and sends them to /login.
- Should the 401 branch skip the sign-out/redirect entirely when there was no Supabase session to begin with?
- Should the AuthContext `value` be wrapped in `useMemo` (and `signOut` in `useCallback`)? It would remove the idle-clock reset on every feature broadcast and stop every useAuth consumer re-rendering on each provider render — but the comments in IdleSessionGuard suggest the location half of this was fixed deliberately and the signOut half may simply have been missed.
- Is `SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN` documented anywhere as required? Nothing fails loudly when it is unset; the realtime feature-broadcast channel just stops working behind the production CSP.
- Should `routePrefetch.warmed` be cleared on identity change (it is module-level and survives sign-out/sign-in), and should `warmed.add` move after the `signedIn` guard so a guest hover does not permanently suppress a signed-in user's query prefetch?
- Should the `.mjs` build/e2e scripts be brought into the ESLint config, and should `npm run lint` pass `--max-warnings 0` to match the backend's zero-message Pylint gate?
- Is `src/data/objective2Summary.json` (imported by the modified AdminOverview.jsx) meant to be committed? It is currently untracked, so a clean checkout fails the production build.

**Depends on:** @supabase/supabase-js 2.110.8 — auth session, token refresh, MFA (listFactors / getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel), direct `profiles` SELECT with the anon key, and the Broadcast realtime channel, axios 1.18.1 — the single transport instance and both interceptors, @tanstack/react-query 5.101.4 — cache, the retry predicate in main.jsx, and `queryClient.clear()` as the identity boundary, react-router 8.3.0 — BrowserRouter, nested Routes, Navigate, useSearchParams (Login `next`), NavLink, framer-motion 12.42.2 — MotionConfig/MotionGlobalConfig driven by PreferencesContext, vite 8.1.5 + @vitejs/plugin-react 6.0.4 + @tailwindcss/vite 4.3.3, rag-thesis-backend routers/settings.py::DEFAULT_FEATURES — mirrored by src/lib/permissions.js, rag-thesis-backend dependencies/auth.py::PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL — substring-matched by src/lib/privilegedMfa.js, rag-thesis-backend main.py CORSMiddleware allow_headers — must list Authorization, Content-Type, X-Guest-ID, Idempotency-Key, X-Turnstile-Token, docker-compose.operations.yml — supplies API_ORIGIN / SUPABASE_ORIGIN / SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN / DOLLAR to the nginx envsubst template and the four VITE_ build args, Cloudflare Turnstile (challenges.cloudflare.com) — allowed by script-src, connect-src and frame-src; the site key is baked at build time

**Used by:** Every page under src/pages/ imports its backend calls from src/api.js (and its error helpers via the api.js re-export), src/components/AppShell.jsx — useNavItems reads canChat/canArchive/canScan/canUpload/isAdmin; HealthStatus polls healthCheck() every 30 s with retry:false, src/components/PrivilegedMfaGate.jsx — consumes privilegedMfaRequired / privilegedMfaRefusals and shouldPromptForPrivilegedMfa, src/components/useMfaStatus.js — the shared ['mfa-factors'] query, read by the dashboard nudge, the settings row and PrivilegedMfaGate, src/pages/Login.jsx — reads LOGIN_NEXT_PARAM/safeNextPath, useAuth().needsMfa, and requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn, src/pages/chat/useGuestChatGate.js — reads the `x-guest-verification` response header off an axios error to re-arm Turnstile, src/pages/admin/AdminOverview.jsx — imports src/data/objective2Summary.json (currently untracked in git), e2e/critical-flows.spec.js — seeds `isu_e2e_auth_fixture` via addInitScript and routes `**/__e2e_api/**`, relying on the e2e define block and the 501 guard
