  **Actual:** package.json is 65 lines; scripts are :11-22. Script contents (lint = `eslint .`, test = `node --test`, coverage 85/85/80 to coverage/lcov.info, test:e2e = `node e2e/run-playwright.mjs`, bundle:budget, security:headers) are all correct. (`rag-thesis-frontend/package.json:11-22`)
- **Wrong:** 'E2E builds go to dist-e2e … (e2e/vite-server.mjs:68)'.
  **Actual:** vite-server.mjs is 45 lines. `const OUT_DIR = 'dist-e2e'` is at :12, used by build() :17 and preview() :22; strictPort/127.0.0.1:4173 at :23; the literal stdin 'close' at :43. (`rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/vite-server.mjs:12`)
- **Wrong:** 'scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs:166 asserts the nginx value'.
  **Actual:** That file is 28 lines total. The `referrer-policy: /strict-origin-when-cross-origin/i` assertion is at :13, inside the `required` map :9-15. The substantive point stands — main.py:167 sets `no-referrer`, so pointing the script at the API origin fails. (`rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs:13`)
- **Wrong:** CORS allow_headers at main.py:196.
  **Actual:** main.py:198. Contents verified exactly: ['Authorization', 'Content-Type', 'X-Guest-ID', 'Idempotency-Key', 'X-Turnstile-Token']. (`rag-thesis-backend/main.py:198`)
- **Wrong:** 'The realtime features_updated broadcast calls setFeatures (AuthContext.jsx:209)'.
  **Actual:** Line 209 is a comment. The broadcast handler calls `void loadFeatures()` at :210; loadFeatures calls `setFeatures` at :132. The causal chain the gotcha describes (setFeatures -> new value object -> new signOut -> new expire -> idle effect re-runs -> recordActivity()) is real and verified. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:210`)
- **Wrong:** 'signOut() clears the cache twice — before the network round trip and again after'.
  **Actual:** Only the SECOND clear is unconditional. `forgetPreviousAccount(null)` at :281 early-returns without calling `queryClient.clear()` when `identityRef.current === null` (already signed out) or `=== undefined` (unresolved) — see the guards at :74 and :78. The unconditional `queryClient.clear()` is at :289. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:72-80,281,289`)
- **Wrong:** 'privilegedMfa.test.js — 13 tests'.
  **Actual:** 15 tests. The map's enumeration misses 'an unresolved profile never forces a method' (:130) and one of the truth-table cases. Anchors :137 and the others it does cite are correct. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.test.js:17-137`)
- **Wrong:** 'e2e/critical-flows.spec.js — 11 Playwright tests' and 'visual-quality.spec.js — … Material surface contrast'.
  **Actual:** critical-flows.spec.js has 13 tests. Three are absent from the map's list: 'light and dark Material surfaces keep readable semantic contrast' (:112 — the map wrongly attributes Material surface contrast to visual-quality.spec.js), 'password reset can send a verification code after the security check resolves' (:157), and 'sign-in reports server-side CAPTCHA misconfiguration accurately' (:192). visual-quality.spec.js contains exactly one test: 'public critical surfaces pass the four-viewport structural quality matrix' (:50). accessibility.spec.js has no fixed count — it generates one test per SURFACES entry in a module-level loop (:484-485). (`rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:112,157,192`)
- **Wrong:** 'eslint.config.js. files: ["**/*.{js,jsx}"] only'.
  **Actual:** There is a SECOND config object at :54-60 scoped to ['**/context/**/*.jsx', '**/components/ui/Motion.jsx', '**/pages/auth/AuthFx.jsx'] that disables react-refresh/only-export-components. Also a globalIgnores at :12 for dist, dist-e2e, node_modules, playwright-report, test-results. The .mjs-is-unlinted conclusion and the warn-level complexity/max-depth/no-console claims are correct (:45, :46, :49). (`rag-thesis-frontend/eslint.config.js:12,54-60`)
- **Wrong:** '~45 exported endpoint wrappers' in api.js.
  **Actual:** 43 top-level `export (async )?(function|const)` declarations, plus the one re-export line at :450. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:161-450`)
- **Wrong:** routePrefetch 'has no entry for /upload/batch'.
  **Actual:** True but understated. `prefetchRoute` keys off the FIRST segment only (`pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0]`, :47), so hovering /upload/batch resolves to segment 'upload', warms the WRONG chunk (pages/Upload, :12), prefetches ['departments'] (:31), and marks 'upload' warmed — which then makes a later hover on /upload a no-op. It never degrades to nothing; it degrades to the wrong page. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/routePrefetch.js:47-50`)
- **Wrong:** '<AppShell.jsx> … the single mount point for <PrivilegedMfaGate/>' and 'Three responsive navs built from one useNavItems() list'.
  **Actual:** PrivilegedMfaGate is mounted conditionally: `{user && <PrivilegedMfaGate />}` (:317), so it never exists for a guest. And there are four nav surfaces fed by `items`: the xl sidebar and the md compact rail (both aria-label 'Primary navigation', :205), the mobile bottom bar rendering `items.slice(0,4)` plus a 'More' button (aria-label 'Mobile navigation', :250-278), and the Sheet drawer (aria-label 'All navigation', :288). The mobile header (:227) carries no nav items. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/AppShell.jsx:138,205,250,288,317`)
- **Wrong:** turnstileConfig.js:4 for the isu_e2e_turnstile flag.
  **Actual:** `const E2E_TURNSTILE_FLAG = 'isu_e2e_turnstile'` is at src/components/security/turnstileConfig.js:3. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/turnstileConfig.js:3`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- The 401 hard-redirect FIRES IN E2E MODE. api.js:133 skips the silent refresh when isE2ETestMode, and :141 skips supabase.auth.signOut() — but `window.location.href = loginPathWithNext(current)` at :145-146 is NOT gated. Any 401 from the /__e2e_api guard or a page.route mock hard-navigates the Playwright page to /login mid-test. This is a strictly worse version of the guest-401 gotcha the map does raise, and it is unmentioned. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:141-146)
- <StrictMode> wraps the whole tree (main.jsx:60). In dev React 19 mounts-unmounts-remounts every effect, so the INITIAL_SESSION dedup at AuthContext.jsx:191 does NOT prevent a double profile+features fetch in dev, and IdleSessionGuard's main effect runs its unconditional recordActivity() (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:131) twice per mount. The map never mentions StrictMode.
- Provider nesting is load-bearing and unstated: AuthProvider sits OUTSIDE BrowserRouter (main.jsx:65-66). Nothing in AuthContext can call router navigation — which is exactly why the api.js 401 path must use window.location.href rather than a navigate(), and why IdleSessionGuard (mounted inside the Router at App.jsx:86) is the component that owns navigate(). AuthProvider is inside QueryClientProvider (main.jsx:62) because it calls useQueryClient. Full order: StrictMode > ErrorBoundary > QueryClientProvider > PreferencesProvider > PreferenceMotion > AuthProvider > BrowserRouter > TooltipProvider > App + Toaster.
- PreferencesContext DOES memoize — `useMemo` at PreferencesContext.jsx:81-88 and `useCallback` at :75 and :79. That in-repo contrast is the strongest evidence for the map's own AuthContext gotcha (the bare object literal at AuthContext.jsx:238) and it is not drawn.
- readE2EAuthFixture() validates the fixture shape, not just JSON: it returns null unless `fixture.user.id` AND `fixture.profile?.role` are both present when a user is declared (e2eSession.js:10). A malformed fixture silently degrades the whole E2E run to guest.
- A third reset path for privilegedMfaRefusals: checkMfa(null) — i.e. any sign-out — zeroes it at AuthContext.jsx:86, alongside the aal2 reset at :101 the map does list.
- The in-repo doc comments contradict the code on the MFA marker: privilegedMfa.js:13 and dependencies/auth.py:222 both say 'lowercase PREFIX', but the implementation is `detail.toLowerCase().includes(...)` — a substring (privilegedMfa.js:22). The map's description is right; the source comments are wrong.
- /health/worker (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:289) is reachable through the '/health' proxy prefix but is never called from the frontend — the only health consumer is AppShell's ['health'] query hitting /health. It returns a real HTTP 503 when no live worker row exists, so any monitor pointed at it is on a different contract from the 200-always /health that HealthStatus renders.
- routePrefetch registers the SAME React Query key ['papers'] for two different call expressions: `listPapers()` for dashboard (:20) and `listPapers(null)` for archive (:22). Identical request in practice, but the module-level `warmed` Set means only whichever nav item is hovered first ever fires it.
- Confirmed the untracked-file hazard the map raises as an open question: rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json exists on disk, is imported at src/pages/admin/AdminOverview.jsx:24, is absent from `git ls-files`, and is NOT covered by any .gitignore rule (`git check-ignore` returns nothing). A clean checkout fails `npm run build`.

## 2. `fe-chat` - Frontend chat experience and landing demo

Renders the single user-facing RAG surface: a request/response (NOT streaming) chat against `POST /chat`, with citation-grouped evidence cards, an 85% duplication banner, per-message provenance/export actions, authenticated session history CRUD, and a lazily-armed Cloudflare Turnstile gate for guests. `src/pages/chat/*.js` holds the four pure helpers that were extracted specifically so the transcript-mutation and notice-classification rules could be asserted by `node --test` without a DOM. `landing/AskDemo.jsx` is a fully scripted, zero-network mock of the same UI used on the marketing page.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx` (1477 lines) - The entire chat page: 12 presentational sub-components (lines 40-875) plus the `Chat()` container (877-1477) holding all state, the request lifecycle, and session management.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/messageNotice.js` (35 lines) - Pure `messageNoticeLabel(message)` -> chip text or null. Reconciles the live response's `no_relevant_thesis`/`notice_type` with the restored row's `messageKind`.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/responseActions.js` (71 lines) - Pure export/copy/TTS/provenance helpers: `plainResponseText`, `copyText`, `responseWithSources`, `downloadResearchNote`, `responseDetails`.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/transcript.js` (41 lines) - Pure transcript surgery: `dropPendingPrompt` (withdraw a trailing unanswered user bubble) and `branchBeforePrompt` (truncate to a prompt + compute its zero-based user-turn index for `edit_from_turn`).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/useGuestChatGate.js` (76 lines) - One-time guest Turnstile gate hook. Owns the `iskai_guest_gate_ok` sessionStorage flag, a token ref, lazy arming, and re-arm on the backend's `X-Guest-Verification` header.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/SecurityCheck.jsx` (208 lines) - Presentation shell around Turnstile: 7 status presentations, a 12s `pending` deadline that synthesises the `unavailable` status, and `quiet` mode that renders zero height while a check passes silently.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/TurnstileWidget.jsx` (122 lines) - Imperative `turnstile.render()` wrapper. Module-level singleton script loader with poisoned-promise recovery; callbacks read through refs so parent re-renders never tear down an in-flight challenge.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/turnstileConfig.js` (11 lines) - `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` / `turnstileEnabled`, computed once at module load from `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` with an E2E localStorage opt-in escape hatch.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/useSecurityGate.js` (49 lines) - NOT used by chat — the auth-form gate. Documented here because it fails OPEN on error/unsupported/unavailable while the chat gate fails CLOSED.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/landing/AskDemo.jsx` (303 lines) - Scripted 5-phase demo (idle/typing/thinking/streaming/sources). No API, no state shape shared with Chat.jsx — the markup is duplicated by hand.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/chatPrefs.js` (21 lines) - localStorage `isu-thesis-chat-prefs-v1`: `{defaultCategory, sendKey}`. Written only by `src/pages/settings/ChatSection.jsx:98`.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js` (450 lines) - `chatQuery` (10 positional args, api.js:167-194) and the session CRUD calls; the request interceptor attaches the Supabase JWT or `X-Guest-ID` (api.js:79/87).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js` (—) - Three guest-chat Playwright flows at lines 210-320: grounded answer + reload, failure-then-retry, and stop-without-losing-the-question.

### Control flow

Message state is a single flat `messages` array (Chat.jsx:882) of two discriminated shapes keyed on a local `kind` field. A user turn is `{id, kind:'user', text}`. A live assistant turn is built at Chat.jsx:1161-1164 as `{id: nextMessageId(), ...res, kind:'ai', messageKind: res.kind, createdAt: new Date().toISOString(), isNew: true}` — the spread of the raw `ChatResponse` lands `answer`, `sources`, `duplication_alert`, `session_id`, `no_relevant_thesis`, `history_saved`, `archive_current`, `notice_type`, and `kind`, and the literal `kind:'ai'` after the spread deliberately clobbers the server's `kind` (models.py:72) while preserving it under `messageKind`. A restored turn (loadSession, Chat.jsx:1046-1064) is assembled field-by-field and carries only `answer`, `sources`, `duplication_alert`, `messageKind`, `notice_type`, `createdAt` — never `archive_current`, `no_relevant_thesis` or `isNew`, because `routers/sessions.py:25` selects a fixed column list and derives `notice_type` from the answer text at read time (`routers/sessions.py:95-99`). Ids come from a module-level counter (`nextMessageId`, Chat.jsx:50-54), not the array index, so a trim never lets React reuse a bubble's entry animation or its local editing state.

There is no streaming anywhere in the app — no `EventSource`, no `ReadableStream`, no SSE. `chatQuery` is one axios POST returning the whole `ChatResponse`. The "stop" affordance (`StreamingRing`, Chat.jsx:149; `stopWaiting`, Chat.jsx:1198) aborts the XHR via an `AbortController` and says so in its own copy ("The browser stopped waiting, while any secure server cleanup may continue"). The only token-by-token effect in the product is AskDemo's framer `staggerChildren` over a hard-coded word array. `send` (Chat.jsx:1096) is the single request path: it trims `text ?? input`, bails if empty or if `requestControllerRef.current` is already set (a hard one-in-flight lock), then consults `guestGate.isReady()`. Not ready means park: `arm()` the widget, push the question back into the composer, set `awaitingCheckRef`/`awaitingCheck`, clear `chatError`, return without touching the transcript. Ready means clear the composer, append the optimistic user bubble onto `options.baseMessages ?? messages`, set `sending`, create the controller, and stash the question in `pendingQuestionRef` (that ref is how `updatePrompt` recognises which bubble is the in-flight one). For guests only it derives `guest_history` (last 5 user texts) and `guest_source_ids` (paper ids from the most recent assistant message that had sources, deduped, capped at 10) from `baseMessages` — Chat.jsx:1121-1136. `conversation_replies` defaults to the visible conversational notices (`notice_type === 'conversation'`), filtered by `withinReplyLimit` (≤500 chars, Chat.jsx:68) and `.slice(-30)` to match `ChatRequest`'s `max_length=500`/`max_length=30` (models.py:42-44). On success: `guestGate.markPassed()`, append, warn by toast if `history_saved === false` for a signed-in user, and adopt `res.session_id` + invalidate the `['sessions']` query when it differs. On failure: ignore `ERR_CANCELED`/`CanceledError`, else trim the optimistic bubble, restore the question to the composer, run `guestGate.handleChatError(err)` (re-arms if the response carried `x-guest-verification`), and raise both a banner (`chatError`) and a toast. The `finally` only resets `sending`/refs when `requestControllerRef.current === controller`, so a path that already nulled the ref (stop, edit, session switch) owns its own cleanup.

Four callers mutate the transcript around a request and all of them route through the pure helpers. `stopWaiting` (1198) aborts, calls `setMessages(dropPendingPrompt)`, and posts a "Response stopped" banner holding the question — note it does NOT restore the question to the composer (the E2E asserts `composer` is empty, critical-flows.spec.js:317). `updatePrompt` (975) has two branches: if the edited prompt is the in-flight one it aborts with reason `edit-prompt`, trims via `dropPendingPrompt`, and re-sends against the trimmed branch; otherwise it calls `branchBeforePrompt`, truncates the visible transcript to before that prompt, and re-sends with `editFromTurn: user && sessionId ? branch.turn : null`, which the backend uses to truncate the saved session only after the new answer succeeds (routers/chat.py:2036-2047). `regenerateResponse` (1001) locates the user bubble immediately preceding the answer, branches there, and additionally passes `conversationReplies` containing the answer being regenerated — that list exists purely so the server picks a different canned conversational line, and `withinReplyLimit` is the fix for the 422s that a full grounded answer used to cause. `loadSession` (1028) aborts the in-flight request, bumps `sessionLoadRef` as a generation token, sets the session id (and, for a superadmin, syncs `filterDepartment` from the session's department), then awaits `getSessionMessages` and discards the result if a later switch already won.

Guest gating is a two-layer lazy state machine. `useGuestChatGate` computes `active = !user && needed`, where `needed` is seeded from `sessionStorage['iskai_guest_gate_ok'] !== '1'` AND `turnstileEnabled`. `armed` is `active && armed`, and `arm()` fires on composer focus (Chat.jsx:1433) or on a parked send — so a reader who never touches the composer never loads Cloudflare's script. `GuestGate` (204) renders `SecurityCheck` only when `armed`, with `quiet={!awaiting}` so a clean pass renders zero height and only the BLOCKING statuses (`interactive|expired|error|unsupported|unavailable`, SecurityCheck.jsx:12) force chrome. `TurnstileWidget` loads `api.js?render=explicit` through a module-level singleton promise that self-heals on failure (drops the cached promise and removes the dead `<script>`, TurnstileWidget.jsx:250-253), then `render()`s with `theme` forced from the app's `isDark` preference (Turnstile cannot restyle in place and the app's dark mode is a class, not a media query). Its callbacks map to the six statuses; `SecurityCheck` synthesises a seventh, `unavailable`, after a 12s `pending` deadline (SecurityCheck.jsx:18/156-160) because a challenge whose subresource is unreachable never fires `error-callback`. When the token arrives, `acceptToken` writes the ref first and then calls `onVerified` from inside Turnstile's own callback, which runs `releaseParkedSend` (Chat.jsx:914) and invokes `sendRef.current()` — the ref is refreshed every render (Chat.jsx:1194-1196) so the parked send sees current state without being rebuilt per keystroke. Because `releaseParkedSend` calls `send()` with no argument, the question that actually goes out is whatever is in `input` at that instant, which is intentional (the parked question stays editable).

Rendering diverges on three signals. `AiBubble` (726) always renders `message.answer` through bare `ReactMarkdown` inside `.prose-chat` (index.css:745-768; no remark-gfm, so tables/strikethrough are inert), then conditionally the "Searched the current indexed archive" line when `archive_current`, then the gold chip from `messageNoticeLabel`. Sources render only when `message.sources?.length`: `groupEvidenceSources` (288) buckets by `source.id` (falling back to `citation-${citation_id}`) so several chunks of one paper collapse into one `SourceCard` bearing several citation chips, and inside the card `evidenceSources` (those with `chunk_id != null`) get a per-chunk row with chunk ordinal, `p. n`/`pp. n–m`, section, and `normalizePercent(similarity).toFixed(2)% match` — backend `retriever.public_source` already multiplies similarity by 100 (services/retriever.py:65) and `normalizePercent` only rescales values in (0,1], so the pair is safe given the 0.30 retrieval floor. `DuplicationBanner` (365) renders when `duplication_alert.flagged`, using the same `normalizePercent` pair against `aggregate_matches`'s already-percent figures (services/novelty.py:152/173). `AiResponseActions` (579) always mounts under every assistant bubble, including notices: Redo (the only control gated by `disabled={sending}`), Copy, and an overflow menu with Listen (Web Speech over `plainResponseText`), Copy with sources, Download research note, Ask a follow-up (which only focuses the composer — no prefill), and See response details, which opens a per-message Modal of `responseDetails`.

AskDemo shares no code with Chat.jsx beyond `Logo`/`AnimatedLogo`/`LogoActivityDots`/`Badge`/`GlassCard` — the header, bubble, thinking indicator, source card and composer markup are copy-pasted and drift independently (it still hardcodes "CCSICT" where the live page interpolates `effectiveDepartment`, and its `ArrowUpwardAltIcon` is a second literal copy of the same SVG path). It runs a `useInView(once:true, margin:'-120px')` trigger into a chained `setTimeout` pipeline (26ms/char type, 600ms send pause, 1000ms think, 42ms/word reveal) whose handles are collected in a `timers` array and cleared on unmount or replay. `usePreferences().reducedMotion` short-circuits straight to phase 4. The demo's composer is `aria-hidden="true"` with a `tabIndex={-1}` disabled button, its thread scrolls itself via `threadRef.scrollTo` rather than `scrollIntoView` (explicitly to avoid dragging the page), and a Replay button bumps `runId` to restart. It has no error, notice, duplication, regenerate, export, session or gate surface at all.

### Public surface

- `export default function Chat()` — Chat.jsx:877. No props. Mounted lazily by App.jsx:12 and routed at App.jsx:48 as `<ProtectedRoute isAllowed={canChat} allowGuest={true}>`, so guests reach it but a role whose `role_features.chat` is false does not.
- `messageNoticeLabel(message) -> string|null` — messageNotice.js:29. Precedence: `no_relevant_thesis` -> NO_EVIDENCE_LABEL; `notice_type === 'conversation'` -> null; `messageKind === 'notice'` -> SYSTEM_NOTICE_LABEL; else null.
- `NO_EVIDENCE_LABEL = 'Search completed · no qualifying archive evidence.'` / `SYSTEM_NOTICE_LABEL = 'System message · not a research answer.'` — messageNotice.js:17-18.
- `plainResponseText(answer) -> string` — responseActions.js:40. Strips md links to their label, rewrites `[n]` to ` citation n `, removes `*_\`#>`, collapses whitespace, de-spaces before punctuation. TTS-only.
- `copyText(text) -> Promise<void>` — responseActions.js:50. `navigator.clipboard.writeText`, else a hidden `<textarea>` + `document.execCommand('copy')`.
- `responseWithSources(message) -> string` — responseActions.js:66. `answer + '\n\nEvidence sources\n' + '[n] Title - Authors (Year)'` per source; returns the bare answer when `sources` is empty.
- `downloadResearchNote(message) -> void` — responseActions.js:79. Blob -> object URL -> synthetic `<a download='iskai-research-note.txt'>.click()` -> immediate `revokeObjectURL`. Anchor is never appended to the DOM.
- `responseDetails(message) -> {type, generation, citations, passages, studies, archive, createdAt}` — responseActions.js:91. `citations` = unique `[n]` markers in the answer text; `passages` = `sources.length`; `studies` = unique truthy `source.id`.
- `dropPendingPrompt(messages) -> messages` — transcript.js:130. Removes the last element only when it is `kind === 'user'`. Non-mutating; tolerates null/undefined.
- `branchBeforePrompt(messages, promptId) -> {messages, turn}` — transcript.js:139. `turn` is the count of `kind === 'user'` entries before the match, or `null` when the id is not a user message.
- `useGuestChatGate(user, {onVerified}) -> {active, armed, hasToken, resetKey, acceptToken, arm, isReady(), tokenForRequest(), markPassed(), handleChatError(error)}` — useGuestChatGate.js:164.
- `SecurityCheck({action, onToken, onStatusChange, resetKey, variant='panel', title, description, quiet=false, appearance='interaction-only', className})` — SecurityCheck.jsx:128. Returns `null` when `!turnstileEnabled`.
- `TurnstileWidget({action, onToken, onStatus, resetKey, appearance, className})` — TurnstileWidget.jsx:257.
- `useSecurityGate() -> {token, status, unavailable, blocked, onToken, onStatusChange}` — useSecurityGate.js:372. Not used by chat.
- `getChatPrefs()` / `setChatPref(key, value)` — chatPrefs.js:231/240. Defaults `{defaultCategory:'', sendKey:'enter'}`.
- `AskDemo()` — AskDemo.jsx:62. No props; consumed once at Landing.jsx:25 (eagerly imported, not lazy).
- `chatQuery(query, session_id, department_filter, guest_history, guest_source_ids, signal, turnstileToken, thesis_category_filter, edit_from_turn, conversation_replies)` — api.js:167. Posts `/chat`; sets `X-Turnstile-Token` only when a token is passed.
- `getSessions()` / `renameSession(id, title)` / `deleteSession(id)` / `getSessionMessages(id)` — api.js:197-212.
- `getPublicSettings()` — api.js:228. Supplies `evaluation_department`, the guest/unknown department label.

### Invariants

- `kind` on a message object is ALWAYS the local role `'user'|'ai'`; the server's answer/notice classification is ALWAYS carried as `messageKind`. Enforced by the literal `kind:'ai'` appearing after the `...res` spread (Chat.jsx:1162) and pinned by a source-text regex in messageNotice.test.js:60-64.
- Every message carries a counter-derived `id` from `nextMessageId()` (Chat.jsx:50-54); array indices are never used as React keys for messages. `SourceCard` is the one exception, falling back to the map index when `group[0].id` is falsy (Chat.jsx:739).
- At most one chat request is ever in flight: `send` returns immediately if `requestControllerRef.current` is non-null (Chat.jsx:1097), and every abandon path nulls that ref before re-sending.
- `send`'s `finally` only clears `sending`/`pendingQuestionRef` when `requestControllerRef.current === controller` (Chat.jsx:1184), so an aborting caller must do its own `setSending(false)` — stopWaiting, updatePrompt and loadSession all do.
- Both abandon paths must withdraw the pending prompt through `dropPendingPrompt`; transcript.test.js:212-234 greps `stopWaiting` and `updatePrompt` for the call and asserts no inline `kind === 'user' ? current.slice(0,-1)` copy remains.
- `conversation_replies` entries must each be 1-500 chars and the list ≤30, matching models.py:42-44. `withinReplyLimit` + `.slice(-30)` enforce it on both the default path (Chat.jsx:1146-1151) and the regenerate path (Chat.jsx:1015-1019). Sending a full grounded answer here previously produced a 422 on every Redo.
- `edit_from_turn` is only sent when `user && sessionId` are both truthy — the backend 400s a prompt edit without a saved session before generation (routers/chat.py:2035-2038).
- `loadSession` must abort the in-flight request AND take a `sessionLoadRef` generation, discarding a superseded `getSessionMessages`. Pinned by transcript.test.js:236-255.
- Guests never send `department_filter`: the argument is `isSuperadmin ? filterDepartment || null : null` (Chat.jsx:1141). E2E asserts `chatPayload.department_filter === null` (critical-flows.spec.js:252).
- The default chat request carries `thesis_category_filter: null` so the frozen unfiltered retrieval path stays in use unless a visitor opts in (critical-flows.spec.js:253-255).
- `guest_history` ≤5 and `guest_source_ids` ≤10 on the client, matching the `max_length` on the Pydantic fields.
- Turnstile callbacks are read through refs (`onTokenRef`/`onStatusRef`, TurnstileWidget.jsx:269-275) so a parent re-render never tears down a live challenge; the widget effect keys only on `[action, appearance, isDark, resetKey]`.
- `useGuestChatGate.tokenForRequest()` returns the ref, not the state, because the parked send fires from inside Turnstile's callback before the token state has committed (useGuestChatGate.js:171-173).
- `handleChatError` keys on `user` rather than `active`, because the backend's re-verification demand can arrive after a pass has already set `needed=false` (useGuestChatGate.js:211-221).
- `normalizePercent` is applied only to similarity/threshold values that cannot legitimately be ≤1 (utils.js:38-51); the duplication banner and source match percentages are the sanctioned call sites.
- The composer action is one persistent DOM node across send/idle/verifying/stop — never an `AnimatePresence` swap — because the E2E clicks Send then Stop on the same element within a frame (Chat.jsx:76-82).

### Data model

- Local `messages: Array<UserMessage | AiMessage>` — `UserMessage = {id, kind:'user', text}`; live `AiMessage = {id, kind:'ai', messageKind, createdAt, isNew:true, answer, sources[], duplication_alert, session_id, no_relevant_thesis, history_saved, archive_current, notice_type}`; restored `AiMessage = {id, kind:'ai', answer, sources[], duplication_alert, messageKind, notice_type, createdAt}`.
- Backend `ChatResponse` (rag-thesis-backend/models.py:58-76): `answer, sources[], duplication_alert?, session_id?, no_relevant_thesis=False, history_saved=False, archive_current=False, kind: Literal['answer','notice']='answer', notice_type: Literal['conversation']|None`.
- Backend `ChatRequest` (models.py:15-44): `question (1-4000)`, `session_id (≤64)`, `department_filter`, `thesis_category_filter ^(student|faculty)$`, `guest_history[≤5][≤4000]`, `guest_source_ids[≤10][≤64]`, `edit_from_turn ≥0`, `conversation_replies[≤30][1-500]`. `extra='ignore'`.
- Source object from `retriever.public_source` (services/retriever.py:44-75): `{id, title, authors, year, track, department, thesis_category?, program_code?, program_name?, specialization_code?, specialization_name?, similarity (already ×100), citation_id?, chunk_id?, chunk_index?, page_start?, page_end?, section?}`.
- `DuplicationAlert` (models.py:47-55): `{flagged, similarity, threshold, matched_paper{}, matched_location{}, summary}` — similarity/threshold already in percent (services/novelty.py:152/173).
- Session row from `GET /sessions` (`_SESSION_FIELDS`, routers/sessions.py:21): `id, user_id, title, department, created_at`. Message row from `GET /sessions/{id}/messages` (`_MESSAGE_FIELDS`, line 25): `id, session_id, question, answer, sources, duplication_alert, kind, created_at` plus a derived `notice_type`.
- React Query keys used on this page: `['public-settings']` (staleTime Infinity), `['sessions']` (enabled `!!user`), `['departments']` (enabled `isSuperadmin`).
- Browser storage: `localStorage['isu-thesis-chat-prefs-v1'] = {defaultCategory, sendKey}`; `sessionStorage['iskai_guest_gate_ok'] = '1'`; `sessionStorage['iskai_guest_rate_id']` (the `X-Guest-ID` value, api.js:25-40); `localStorage['isu_e2e_turnstile']` (E2E opt-in, turnstileConfig.js:333).

### Config knobs

- VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY — the only switch for the entire client-side guest gate (turnstileConfig.js:339). Must be kept in step with the backend's TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY or guests are hard-locked.
- localStorage `isu_e2e_turnstile = '1'` under `--mode e2e` — synthesises the site key `e2e-turnstile-site-key` so an E2E run can exercise pending→solved without Cloudflare (turnstileConfig.js:333-340).
- localStorage `isu-thesis-chat-prefs-v1` — `defaultCategory` ('' | 'student' | 'faculty') seeds the header category Select on mount and on every new conversation; `sendKey` ('enter' | 'ctrlEnter') flips the composer keybinding. Written by src/pages/settings/ChatSection.jsx:98.
- `/settings/public` -> `evaluation_department` — the department label shown to guests and used as the last fallback for a superadmin; defaults to the literal 'CCSICT' (Chat.jsx:927).
- Server-side `role_features.chat` drives `canChat` (AuthContext.jsx:262) and hence the route guard at App.jsx:48; `allowGuest={true}` keeps the page reachable signed out.
- VITE_API_URL / the Vite dev proxy — `/chat` is both a React route and an API prefix, so vite.config.js uses an HTML-aware bypass for it.
- PreferencesContext `isDark` (forced into `turnstile.render({theme})`) and `reducedMotion` (AskDemo jumps straight to its final frame).

### Error handling

- Transport failures surface twice: a persistent in-page banner (`chatError = {title, message, question}`, Chat.jsx:1180) rendered at Chat.jsx:1349-1374 with Edit / Retry / Dismiss, and a transient sonner toast. `apiErrorMessage` (lib/apiErrors.js:10) prefers FastAPI's `detail` (string or the 422 field-error list joined as `loc: msg`), then `data.message`, then `error.message` when no response arrived.
- Cancellation is silent: `isCancelledRequest` (Chat.jsx:56) matches `ERR_CANCELED` / `CanceledError` and the catch returns before any banner or trim. Abort reasons in use: `'unmount'`, `'session-switch'`, `'conversation-reset'`, `'edit-prompt'`, `'user-stop'`.
- User-initiated stop produces its own banner (`title: 'Response stopped'`, Chat.jsx:1206-1210) plus `toast.info`, preserving the question in the banner only.
- `history_saved === false` on a signed-in turn raises `toast.warning('Answer received, but chat history was not saved')` (Chat.jsx:1165-1169). The answer is still rendered.
- Provider capacity is NOT an error path: the backend returns HTTP 200 with `CAPACITY_MESSAGE` and `kind='notice'`, so it renders as a normal assistant bubble carrying the SYSTEM_NOTICE chip.
- 401 is handled centrally in api.js:128-148: one collapsed silent `refreshSession` and replay, then `supabase.auth.signOut()` and a hard navigate to `loginPathWithNext(current)`. GET-only retries on 502/504 (api.js:116-125) never apply to `POST /chat`.
- 403 with `X-Guest-Verification` re-opens the Turnstile widget via `handleChatError` (useGuestChatGate.js:213-221): clears the sessionStorage flag and the token ref, sets `needed`/`armed`, and bumps `resetKey` to force a fresh `turnstile.render()`. Requires `expose_headers=['X-Guest-Verification']` (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:200) when the API is cross-origin.
- Session CRUD failures toast only: `toast.error('Rename failed'|'Delete failed', {description: apiErrorMessage(err)})` (Chat.jsx:1224/1240). Session-list fetch failure swaps the list for an inline alert with a Retry that calls React Query's `refetch` (Chat.jsx:1005-1010 region / SessionList error branch).
- `getSessionMessages` failure toasts 'Could not load conversation' and leaves the previous transcript on screen (Chat.jsx:1066-1069).
- Configuration degradation is a non-blocking strip: `ConfigurationWarning` renders when `/settings/public` errors or (for a superadmin) `/departments` errors (Chat.jsx:269-279, mounted at 1348).
- Clipboard and TTS failures toast ('Could not copy the response', 'Text-to-speech is not available in this browser') rather than throwing.
- Turnstile script-load failure rejects the singleton promise, which emits `status='error'` and self-heals the cached promise and `<script>` tag so the next mount retries cleanly (TurnstileWidget.jsx:248-253, 309-312).

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/messageNotice.test.js — 10 cases pinning the label precedence, the null for `notice_type:'conversation'`, and two source-text regexes asserting Chat.jsx still spells `...res, kind: 'ai', messageKind: res.kind` and `notice_type: m.notice_type`.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/responseActions.test.js — TTS rewriting, the exact `responseDetails` object for a two-citation one-paper answer, the copy-with-sources string format, and a grep asserting all seven action labels plus `branchBeforePrompt(messages, prompt.id)`, `answerMessage.answer` and `createdAt: new Date().toISOString()` survive in Chat.jsx.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/transcript.test.js — `dropPendingPrompt` purity/edge cases, `branchBeforePrompt` turn arithmetic, plus four source-text guards: updatePrompt's two `send(updated` call sites, both abandon paths routing through `dropPendingPrompt` with no inline slice copy, loadSession's abort + generation guard, and the `conversation_replies` `.slice(-30)` shape.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:210-265 — guest grounded answer: asserts the answer text and source title render, `department_filter`/`thesis_category_filter`/`session_id` are all null in the payload, and the guest banner survives a hard reload.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:267-296 — 503 then success: the banner appears, the composer is repopulated with the question, and a second Send succeeds (exactly 2 attempts).
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:298-320 — Stop: the 'Response stopped' alert carries the quoted question, the composer is left empty, and exactly one request was made.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js:337-338 and visual-quality.spec.js:10 — `/chat` as `guest-chat` in the axe and visual-regression sweeps.
- No unit test covers `useGuestChatGate`, `SecurityCheck`, `TurnstileWidget`, `isVerifyingSend`, `groupEvidenceSources`, `pageLabelFor`, or `AskDemo` — there is no DOM test runner in this project (node:test only, over colocated *.test.js).

### Gotchas

- BUG — `isVerifyingSend` (Chat.jsx:196) checks only `'error'` and `'unsupported'`, omitting `'unavailable'`. That status is precisely what SecurityCheck.jsx:158 synthesises after the 12s hung-challenge deadline, and `useSecurityGate.js:370` lists all three together as VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE. So the exact outage the deadline was added for (2026-08-04, Cloudflare challenge host unreachable) leaves the guest composer's send button disabled behind a spinner indefinitely.
- INCONSISTENT FAILURE POSTURE — auth forms fail OPEN (`useSecurityGate.blocked` goes false on error/unsupported/unavailable, with a long rationale at useSecurityGate.js:346-369), but guest chat fails CLOSED: `isReady()` is `!active || tokenRef.current` with no unavailable escape, so a permanently broken Turnstile makes every guest send re-park forever. The only exit is the panel's own 'Try again' (SecurityCheck.jsx:109-117).
- MISLEADING COPY — `bodyFor('unavailable')` renders 'You can continue — your sign-in is still verified by the server.' (SecurityCheck.jsx:62). On the guest chat gate there is no sign-in, and the reader cannot continue.
- CONFIG FOOTGUN — `turnstileEnabled` is a frontend-only constant from `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY`, while the backend guard keys on `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` (services/turnstile.py:36). With the secret set but the site key missing, every guest chat 403s with `X-Guest-Verification: required`; `handleChatError` sets `needed`/`armed`, but `SecurityCheck` returns null on `!turnstileEnabled`, `onStatusChange` never fires so `gateStatus` stays `'pending'`, and the guest is locked out behind a permanent spinner with no visible widget.
- INCONSISTENT TRIM — the error path uses a raw `setMessages((m) => m.slice(0, -1))` (Chat.jsx:1177) instead of `dropPendingPrompt`, even though transcript.test.js enforces the helper for the other two abandon paths. Harmless today because the last element is always the just-appended user bubble, but it is exactly the duplication the helper was extracted to eliminate.
- ORPHANED PROMPT — `loadSession` aborts the in-flight request (whose catch returns early on cancel, leaving the optimistic bubble in place) and only replaces `messages` inside the `try`. If `getSessionMessages` rejects, the old transcript stays on screen with an unanswered user bubble and `sending === false`, and nothing ever withdraws it.
- DEAD CONDITION — `if (updated === original.text && !wasPending) return` (Chat.jsx:991) is unreachable with `wasPending` true, since that branch returns at line 989. The `&& !wasPending` is redundant.
- DUPLICATED CLIPBOARD LOGIC — `copyPrompt` (Chat.jsx:957) reimplements `copyText` from responseActions.js:50 inline, with a different teardown (`document.body.removeChild(helper)` vs `helper.remove()`), so only the response path is covered by the extracted, testable helper.
- STALE PREFERENCE — `sendKey` is read once via `useState(() => getChatPrefs().sendKey)` (Chat.jsx:889) and never refreshed; `filterCategory` is at least re-read on `newConversation()` (Chat.jsx:1091). Safe only because Chat unmounts when navigating to /settings.
- HIDDEN RESET — changing the superadmin department Select calls `newConversation()` (Chat.jsx:1313), which silently resets `filterCategory` back to the stored default and clears the transcript.
- ALWAYS-ON RETRY — the error banner renders Retry unconditionally (Chat.jsx:1370) and ignores `isRetryableFailure` (apiErrors.js:38-41), so a 422 (question over the backend's 4000-char cap) or a 403 Turnstile refusal advertises a recovery that cannot succeed. The composer textarea has no `maxLength` at all; the only `maxLength` on the page is 120 on the rename input (Chat.jsx:1455).
- NO COOLDOWN UI — the backend's 60s capacity cooldown returns HTTP 200 with `CAPACITY_MESSAGE` and `kind='notice'` (routers/chat.py:1360, stamped at 2094). The frontend renders it as an ordinary assistant bubble with the generic 'System message' chip and a live Redo button. There is no countdown, no retry-after, and nothing distinguishes it from a guard refusal.
- ASYMMETRIC RESTORE — a reloaded no-evidence notice gets SYSTEM_NOTICE_LABEL rather than NO_EVIDENCE_LABEL, because `no_relevant_thesis` is not a `chat_messages` column (routers/sessions.py:25 selects a fixed list). Deliberate and documented (messageNotice.js:20-28), but it means the same exchange reads differently before and after a reload. Restored rows likewise never carry `archive_current`, so the 'Searched the current indexed archive' line and `responseDetails.archive` flip to 'Saved response snapshot'.
- NOTICE CLASSIFICATION SPLIT — `responseDetails.type` keys on `messageKind === 'notice'` while `generation` keys on `notice_type === 'conversation'`. A conversational fast-path reply is stamped `kind='notice'` server-side (chat_notices.response_kind, chat_notices.py:256), so its details modal reads 'System response' + 'Local conversational response' — but its chip is suppressed by `messageNoticeLabel`'s `notice_type` branch, so the two surfaces disagree about how loudly to mark it.
- OVERFLOW CLIPPING — the response overflow menu is `absolute bottom-full ... z-40` (Chat.jsx:664) inside a scroll container declared `overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto` (Chat.jsx:1378). `z-index` does not escape an overflow clip, so the menu is cut off for any message whose actions row sits near the top of the scrolled viewport.
- MENU DISMISS IS OVER-BROAD — the same handler is bound to `pointerdown` and `keydown` and closes on ANY key whose target is outside the menu (Chat.jsx:589-593), not just Escape. Typing in the composer closes an open response menu.
- `downloadResearchNote` calls `URL.revokeObjectURL(url)` synchronously on the line after `anchor.click()` (responseActions.js:87-88) and never appends the anchor to the document. Works in current Chromium/Firefox but is the classic shape that races in stricter engines. Filename is a fixed constant, so multiple exports collide in the downloads folder.
- `plainResponseText` strips `#` and `>` characters from anywhere in the text, not just line-leading positions (responseActions.js:44), so a literal '#1' or a quoted '>' in an answer is mangled in the spoken version.
- LATE-RESPONSE SAFETY relies on the AbortController alone plus a post-await `if (controller.signal.aborted) return` (Chat.jsx:1152); there is no generation counter on the chat request the way `sessionLoadRef` guards the session fetch.
- `AskDemo` duplicates the live chat markup by hand — header, bubbles, thinking indicator, source card, composer, footnote and the `ARROW_UPWARD_ALT_PATH` SVG (AskDemo.jsx:32-48 vs Chat.jsx:130-146). It hardcodes 'CCSICT' in four places where the live page interpolates `effectiveDepartment`, so a non-CCSICT deployment's landing page lies. It is also eagerly imported by Landing.jsx:5 rather than lazy.
- `messageSequence` is a module-level mutable counter (Chat.jsx:50); ids are unique per page load, not per session, and reset on a hard reload.
- The `busy` flag (Chat.jsx:897) is shared by the rename and delete dialogs, and `guestGate.markPassed()` runs after every successful response but no-ops after the first because it early-returns on `!active`.
- `bottomRef.current?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth'})` fires on every `messages`/`sending` change (Chat.jsx:948-950) with no `block`/`nearest` hint, so it can scroll the page container as well as the message list.

### Open questions

- Is the omission of `'unavailable'` from `isVerifyingSend` (Chat.jsx:196) intentional, or an oversight from when SecurityCheck gained the synthesised status? The sibling `useSecurityGate.VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE` lists all three, which suggests oversight.
- Should guest chat adopt the auth forms' fail-open posture? The backend already rejects an unverified guest with a readable 403, so failing open would degrade to a server-side refusal rather than a dead composer — but it would also let a bot burn one Gemini call per rotated guest ID before the 403.
- Nothing validates the 4000-char question cap client-side; should the composer textarea carry `maxLength={4000}` so the 422 never happens, and should the error banner respect `isRetryableFailure` before offering Retry?
- Should AskDemo interpolate the deployment's department instead of hardcoding 'CCSICT' four times, and should it be lazy-loaded given the landing page's 330 kB eager bundle budget?
- The response overflow menu's `absolute bottom-full` inside an `overflow-y-auto` container will clip; is a portal (the codebase already has Modal/Sheet primitives) the intended fix?
- `loadSession` leaves an orphaned optimistic prompt if `getSessionMessages` rejects — should the abort path call `setMessages(dropPendingPrompt)` before the fetch?

**Used by:** rag-thesis-frontend/src/App.jsx:12,48 — lazy route `/chat` behind ProtectedRoute with allowGuest, rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Landing.jsx:5,25 — AskDemo (eager import), rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/ChatSection.jsx — the writer for chatPrefs consumed here, rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/* and Login — the other SecurityCheck/useSecurityGate consumers (auth forms, which fail open where chat fails closed)

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 12 correction(s), 13 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Chat.jsx holds '12 presentational sub-components (lines 40-875)'.
  **Actual:** There are 18 JSX-returning components plus 4 pure helper functions (22 module-level functions) before `Chat()`: isCancelledRequest(56), withinReplyLimit(68), ArrowUpwardAltIcon(133), StreamingRing(149), composerActionState(164), ComposerAction(170), isVerifyingSend(196), GuestGate(204), Composer(224), ConfigurationWarning(269), pageLabelFor(281), groupEvidenceSources(288), SourceCard(298), DuplicationBanner(365), MaterialEditIcon(416), PromptAction(425), UserBubble(449), AiAvatar(545), ResponseMenuItem(556), DetailRow(570), AiResponseActions(579), AiBubble(726), TypingIndicator(778), SessionList(798). Range is 56-875, not 40-875. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:56-875`)
- **Wrong:** On a permanently broken Turnstile 'The only exit is the panel's own Try again (SecurityCheck.jsx:109-117).'
  **Actual:** `view.retry` is set only on the `error` and `unavailable` presentations. The `unsupported` presentation has no `retry` key, so no Try-again button renders at all. Combined with `isVerifyingSend` returning false for `unsupported`, the guest sees a live-looking Send button that silently re-parks on every click with no recovery affordance anywhere on the page. The map's own `isVerifyingSend` gotcha therefore understates the damage: `unavailable` disables the button forever, `unsupported` leaves it enabled but inert. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/SecurityCheck.jsx:25-29 vs Chat.jsx:196-198`)
- **Wrong:** The gate's token lifecycle is described as `tokenForRequest()` returning the ref, with `markPassed()` merely writing the sessionStorage flag and no-opping after the first call.
  **Actual:** `markPassed()` also sets `tokenRef.current = null` and `setNeeded(false)`, so the Turnstile token is strictly one-shot. Every guest message after the first sends with NO `X-Turnstile-Token` header (chatQuery omits the header when the token is null, api.js:189-190) and depends entirely on the backend's server-side verified-guest cache keyed by `X-Guest-ID` (services/turnstile.py `is_guest_verified`). The map never states this, yet it is the load-bearing reason a single challenge unlocks the session. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/useGuestChatGate.js:56-63`)
- **Wrong:** 'DUPLICATED CLIPBOARD LOGIC — copyPrompt reimplements copyText ... with a different teardown (document.body.removeChild(helper) vs helper.remove()), so only the response path is covered by the extracted, testable helper.'
  **Actual:** The divergence is behavioural, not cosmetic. `copyText` gates on the *existence* of `navigator.clipboard?.writeText` and awaits it unguarded, so a rejected write (denied permission, non-secure context, document not focused) throws straight out of the helper and never reaches the textarea fallback. `copyPrompt` wraps the write itself in try/catch and does fall back. The inline duplicate is the more robust of the two, and the extracted/tested helper is the one with the gap. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/responseActions.js:15-29 vs Chat.jsx:957-964`)
- **Wrong:** messageNotice.test.js — '10 cases'.
  **Actual:** 11 tests: 9 behavioural plus the 2 source-text regex guards. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/messageNotice.test.js:10-77`)
- **Wrong:** Anchors for the four helper modules and the security components (transcript.js:130/139, useGuestChatGate.js:164/171-173/211-221, responseActions.js:40/50/66/79/91, TurnstileWidget.jsx:257/269-275/250-253/309-312, useSecurityGate.js:372/370/346-369, chatPrefs.js:231/240, turnstileConfig.js:333/339-340, transcript.test.js:212-234/236-255).
  **Actual:** All are past end-of-file (these files are 41, 76, 71, 122, 49, 21, 11 and 126 lines). Correct anchors: dropPendingPrompt transcript.js:24, branchBeforePrompt transcript.js:33; useGuestChatGate.js:17 (hook), :50-52 (isReady), :53-55 (tokenForRequest), :66-74 (handleChatError); responseActions.js:5 (plainResponseText), :15 (copyText), :31 (responseWithSources), :44 (downloadResearchNote), :56 (responseDetails); TurnstileWidget.jsx:49 (component), :61-67 (callback refs), :42-45 (poisoned-promise self-heal), :101-104 (load-failure -> status error); useSecurityGate.js:31 (hook), :29 (VERIFICATION_UNAVAILABLE), :5-28 (fail-open rationale); chatPrefs.js:8 / :17; turnstileConfig.js:3-5 (E2E flag), :9 (site key), :11 (turnstileEnabled); transcript.test.js:76-98 (abandon-path guard) and :100-119 (loadSession guard). SecurityCheck.jsx, messageNotice.js, AskDemo.jsx and Chat.jsx anchors are mostly correct. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/transcript.js:41 (EOF)`)
