Everything else the map cites in the backend checks out exactly: models.py:15-44/47-55/58-76 and kind at :72, sessions.py:21/25, retriever.py:44-74 with `round(similarity*100,2)` at :65, novelty.py:152/173, main.py:200 expose_headers, AuthContext.jsx:262, App.jsx:12/48. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/chat_notices.py:246`)
- **Wrong:** AskDemo 'shares no code with Chat.jsx beyond Logo/AnimatedLogo/LogoActivityDots/Badge/GlassCard' and has 'a tabIndex={-1} disabled button'.
  **Actual:** It also imports `Button` (shared with Chat.jsx) plus Reveal, SectionHeading, contentKeys and usePreferences. The send button is `disabled={!typed}` — it is *enabled* throughout phase 1 while the scripted text types, not unconditionally disabled; only `tabIndex={-1}` and the wrapper's `aria-hidden` keep it out of reach. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/landing/AskDemo.jsx:5,10-13,279-288`)
- **Wrong:** AskDemo pipeline is '26ms/char type, 600ms send pause, 1000ms think, 42ms/word reveal'.
  **Actual:** Correct, but the chain also has a 450 ms lead-in before typing begins and a +500 ms tail after the last word before phase 4 (`SEND_PAUSE_MS + THINK_MS + ANSWER_WORDS.length * WORD_MS + 500`), so the full script is ~7.0 s, not ~3.6 s. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/landing/AskDemo.jsx:97-111`)
- **Wrong:** downloadResearchNote is 'Blob -> object URL -> synthetic <a> -> revoke'.
  **Actual:** Accurate but incomplete: the payload is wrapped, not raw. `IskAI research note\n\n${responseWithSources(message)}\n\nGenerated by the ISU Thesis AI Library. Verify important claims against the cited archive metadata.\n`. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/responseActions.js:44-54`)
- **Wrong:** E2E anchor 'the E2E asserts composer is empty, critical-flows.spec.js:317'.
  **Actual:** That assertion is at line 321. The other E2E anchors (210, 252, 255, 267, 298, accessibility.spec.js:337-338, visual-quality.spec.js:10) are exact. (`rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:321`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- STARTERS — four empty-state suggestion buttons (Chat.jsx:40-45, rendered 1385-1397) call `send(s)` directly with no composer involvement. The map's request-lifecycle section claims `send` trims `text ?? input`, but never mentions this second entry point. For an unverified guest a starter click takes the park branch, which calls `setInput(question)` and silently drops the starter's text into the composer — the only place in the UI where a click populates the textarea.
- TypingIndicator (Chat.jsx:778-796) and its gate `isAwaitingAnswer = sending && messages[messages.length-1]?.kind === 'user'` (Chat.jsx:921, rendered 1425). This is the whole 'thinking' surface — AnimatedLogo + LogoActivityDots with role=status/aria-live=polite. The map covers StreamingRing and the stop affordance but never mentions the indicator, nor that it is suppressed whenever the trailing message is not a user bubble.
- SourceCard's `locationPending` branch (Chat.jsx:302-305, 352-356) renders 'Some evidence locations are pending citation backfill.' whenever any evidence chunk has neither a page label nor a section — the visible signal for pre-citation-migration rows.
- Citation-number synthesis: `item.citation_id ?? itemIndex + 1` (Chat.jsx:300 and :336). When the backend omits `citation_id` (public_source only sets it when the caller passes one, services/retriever.py:66-67) the card invents chip numbers from array position, which can disagree with the `[n]` markers actually present in the answer text and with `responseDetails.citations`.
- Unguarded storage access during render: `useGuestChatGate` reads `window.sessionStorage.getItem` inside a `useState` initializer with no try/catch (useGuestChatGate.js:19-21), and `api.js:27/37` does the same in the request interceptor. In a browser with site data blocked this throws during the first render of Chat and takes the whole lazy route down. `chatPrefs.getChatPrefs` (chatPrefs.js:9-14) is the only storage reader in this subsystem that guards.
- `isRetryableFailure` is already consumed by a sibling page — Novelty.jsx:471 gates its Retry on it. So the chat banner's unconditional Retry (Chat.jsx:1370) is an inconsistency with an established in-repo pattern, not just an unimplemented idea, and apiErrors.test.js:43-49 already pins the 4xx/5xx split.
- Dead CSS: `.prose-chat table`/`thead`/`th`/`td` rules (index.css:773-782) carry a comment claiming they defend against a model emitting a table, but react-markdown 10.1.0 is mounted with no plugins and raw HTML disabled, so a pipe table can never become a `<table>` element. The rules are unreachable.
- `TurnstileWidget.loadTurnstile` has a latent hang: when a `<script id='cloudflare-turnstile-script'>` already exists, the executor attaches load/error listeners and returns early (TurnstileWidget.jsx:25-33). If that tag already fired `load` neither listener will fire again, the promise never settles, no `error` status is emitted, and SecurityCheck's 12 s pending deadline is the only thing that rescues the UI.
- `SecurityCheck` forwards `resetKey={resetKey + retryNonce}` (SecurityCheck.jsx:202), so the gate's re-arm counter and the panel's own Try-again counter share one numeric space feeding `TurnstileWidget`'s effect key.
- `stopWaiting` early-returns unless BOTH `requestControllerRef.current` and `pendingQuestionRef.current` are truthy (Chat.jsx:1201), so a request whose question ref was already cleared cannot be stopped through this path.
- `submitDelete` calls `newConversation()` when the deleted session is the active one (Chat.jsx:1243) — deleting the open conversation clears the transcript and resets `filterCategory` to the stored default as a side effect.
- `GET /settings/public` returns exactly `{'evaluation_department': settings.thesis_evaluation_department}` and nothing else (rag-thesis-backend/routers/settings.py:39-42) — the map treats it as a settings payload, but it is a single-key response consumed for one label.
- `_chat_impl` is a thin wrapper: it calls `_chat_impl_unstamped` (routers/chat.py:2098) and then stamps `response.kind = chat_notices.response_kind(response)` at :2094. The map attributes the whole pipeline to `_chat_impl`; the ~12 return statements the kind-stamping docstring refers to live in the unstamped function.

## 3. `fe-upload-archive` - Frontend upload, batch upload, archive and novelty pages

The four privileged React routes that put manuscripts into the pgvector archive and read them back out: a 4-step single-file upload wizard with client-side polling of the durable ingestion job, a 3-step batch wizard that stages up to 20 manuscripts as N independent jobs polled through one list endpoint, a metadata-only archive browser with client-side filter/sort/pagination and a duplication-screening panel, and a novelty scanner with history, follow-up chat and a metadata-only JSON export. All four are pure presentation over `src/api.js`; every authorization and department boundary is enforced server-side, and these pages only mirror it (AuthContext `canUpload`/`canScan`/`canArchive`, `isSuperadmin`). The two upload pages are the only places in the app that own long-lived polling loops and sessionStorage resume state.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx` (626 lines) - Single-manuscript wizard shell: useReducer over uploadState, autofill confirm dialog, hand-rolled setTimeout poll loop with generation/job-id guards, cancel dialog, sessionStorage resume under the raw key 'activeUploadJob'.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/UploadBatch.jsx` (1187 lines) - Batch wizard shell: 13 local presentational components (CapacityMeter, BatchDefaultsForm, ProgramBulkBar, AutoGrowTitle, ReviewCard, ProgressTable, BatchOverview, FinishedBanner, IngestingStep…) plus the page component that owns the React Query polling of /upload/jobs, the 30-minute ceiling timer and owner-stamped sessionStorage resume.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/batchState.js` (425 lines) - Pure batch reducer + selectors. 19 actions, row factory, validation split, job-status folding, summary/progress/label helpers, serialize/restore with owner stamping.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/uploadState.js` (118 lines) - Pure single-upload reducer (12 actions), UPLOAD_STEPS enum, emptyUploadForm, the shared uploadMetadataErrors validator (used by BOTH wizards), and isCurrentPoll.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/wizardSteps.js` (184 lines) - JSX-free presentation logic: WIZARD_STEPS, the 7 PIPELINE_STAGES, stageView (stage→timeline), stepInteraction, isTerminalJob, railMode, summaryRows, formatFileSize, extractionDepartment, autofilledKeys.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/PipelineTimeline.jsx` (167 lines) - Renders stageView output as a 7-node ol with per-stage icons, the progressbar, the live-stage message chip and the retry_wait notice. Lazily mounts IngestScene3D unless hide3D.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/IngestOutcome.jsx` (~240 lines) - The three terminal screens (failed / cancelled / indexed), the exact-duplicate special case, and UploadScreening — the ingest-time 85% duplication panel with MostSimilar + the Novelty Review cross-link.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/MetadataForm.jsx` (388 lines) - Step 2: Identity / Classification / Description sections, AutofillChip provenance markers, superadmin-vs-pinned department branch, program→specialization cascade, collapsible abstract.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/Dropzone.jsx` (204 lines) - Shared PDF drop target for both wizards; validates through batchState.fileValidationError and resets input.value so re-picking the same file fires onChange.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/WizardStep.jsx` (78 lines) - Direction-aware AnimatePresence mode="wait" pane + height spring driven by useAutoHeight; toggles overflow only while animating.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/WizardStepper.jsx` (138 lines) - Shared horizontal/vertical stepper; a node is a <button> only when state==='done' AND onSelect is supplied (Playwright strict-mode constraint).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/ReviewPanel.jsx` (154 lines) - Step 3 record card + collapsible PipelinePreview listing PIPELINE_STAGES.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/WizardRail.jsx` (~180 lines) - DEAD FILE. Exports WizardRail, RailFileChip, RailSummaryList, RailHeading; zero importers anywhere in src/ or e2e/.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/useAutoHeight.js` (37 lines) - ResizeObserver height measurement, returns 'auto' under reducedMotion or before first measurement.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx` (623 lines) - Archive page: ConcentrationMeter (banded 25/70 track), MatchedTheses, ScreeningFigures, ScreeningRun, ScreeningDetail (current vs at-upload), PaperCard, ArchiveResults (filmstrip paging), ArchiveDetailModal, sticky pagination.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/useArchiveCatalog.js` (135 lines) - All archive state: 7-key filter object, sortBy, page + pageDirection, three React Query reads ('papers','tracks','departments'), memoized filter→sort→paginate chain, delete mutation.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/archiveFilters.js` (127 lines) - Pure filter/sort/catalog-resolution: filterArchivePapers, sortArchivePapers (7 comparators + relevance scoring), archiveYears, resolveArchiveTracks, resolveArchivePrograms, SORT_OPTIONS.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/ArchiveFiltersBar.jsx` (276 lines) - Search input, CategoryTabs radiogroup, chip-styled Selects for program/track/specialization/department/year/sort, and ActiveFilterChips.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Novelty.jsx` (561 lines) - Novelty page: ScanDropzone (PDF/TXT, 25 MB), ScanResult (5 metric tiles + ProgressRing on concentration + top_matches + markdown advisory + follow-up chat), history sidebar with per-row and bulk delete.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.js` (57 lines) - buildNoveltyReport / downloadNoveltyReport — the metadata-only JSON export, report_version '2026-07-25'.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.jsx` (280 lines) - Landing surface: 4 derived stat tiles over the same ['papers'] query, role-gated QuickActions, SecurityNudge (MFA), 5 most recent papers.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/catalog.js` (92 lines) - Department→program→specialization resolution shared by both wizards and the archive: programSelection (by code, for the extractor), programSelectionById, specializationSelection, THESIS_CATEGORIES.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/pagination.js` (76 lines) - totalPageCount / clampPage / getPaginationPages (Google sliding window) / paginateItems.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/utils.js` (~300 lines) - scanMetrics/normalizePercent/verdictTone/verdictLabel/verdictExplanation/currentScreening/atUploadScreening/hasRescan/isScreeningFlagged/screeningIsUnchanged/archiveSizeLabel/mostSimilarPaper — the duplication vocabulary all four pages read.

### Control flow

**Single upload (`Upload.jsx`).** `useReducer(uploadReducer, enforcedDepartment, createUploadState)` where `enforcedDepartment = userDepartment || 'CCSICT'`; an effect at Upload.jsx:196 re-pins `form.department` for every non-superadmin. Step 0 renders `<Dropzone onFile={handleFileSelect}>`. `handleFileSelect` (263) mints a fresh `crypto.randomUUID()` idempotency key and — crucially — sets **pendingFile**, not `file`; the `ConfirmDialog` at the page foot is the gate. Both dialog buttons commit the file; only Confirm calls `runAutofill`. `runAutofill` (204) POSTs `/upload/extract-metadata`, then splits the answer through `extractionDepartment({isSuperadmin, extracted: metadata.department, current: form.department, enforced})` — `extractedDepartment` is blank for everyone but a superadmin, so nobody else ever gets a department autofill chip — and resolves `metadata.program_code`/`specialization_code` against the *resolved* department with `programSelection`. The form patch merges per-field with `||prev` fallbacks (so a blank extraction never clears a typed value) except the classification, which is applied whole-or-not-at-all via `...(selection||{})`. `dispatch({type:'set-autofilled', keys: autofilledKeys(patch)})` credits only what actually landed. Step 1 → `validateMetadata()` runs `uploadMetadataErrors`; `revalidateTouched` on blur only *refines* an already non-empty error map so tabbing out of a blank field never introduces a new complaint. Step 2 → `submit()` (374) POSTs `/upload/paper` multipart with the `Idempotency-Key` header, writes `sessionStorage.activeUploadJob = {jobId, idempotencyKey, title}`, seeds `job = {status, stage:'download', progress:8}` and calls `startPolling(res.job_id)`.

`startPolling` (288) is a hand-rolled `setTimeout` loop, first tick at 500 ms then 1500 ms. Staleness is guarded four ways through `isCurrentPoll`: `mountedRef`, a monotonically incremented `pollGenerationRef` (bumped by `stopPolling`, captured per loop), and `jobIdRef`. Each tick: a 30-minute wall-clock ceiling from `pollStartedRef` sets `pollError` and stops; `getUploadStatus` on success resets `pollFailuresRef` and `setJob(status)`; `completed`/`failed`/`cancelled` clear sessionStorage, toast, and do **not** reschedule (on completed it also `invalidateQueries(['papers'])` and, when `status.duplication.flagged`, emits a second toast built from `scanMetrics` + `mostSimilarPaper`); anything else reschedules at 1500 ms. On error, a 404 clears storage and stops permanently; otherwise `pollFailuresRef++` and the next tick is at `1500 * failures` until 5 failures, which stops with "The server could not confirm the upload status. The job was not cancelled." The mount effect (355) reads `sessionStorage.activeUploadJob`, restores the idempotency key and title, jumps straight to `UPLOAD_STEPS.ingesting` with a synthetic queued job and resumes polling — a deliberate one-shot with an `exhaustive-deps` disable. `terminal = isTerminalJob(job)` (428) swaps `IngestingPanel` for `IngestOutcome`; `onReviewNovelty` is passed only when `canScan`, because `/novelty` is gated by the same server matrix.

**PipelineTimeline / stageView.** `stageView(job)` normalizes the worker's stage through `STAGE_ALIASES = {store:'download', queued:'download', '':'download'}` (the backend's pre-worker `store` stage otherwise `findIndex`ed to -1 and greyed the whole stepper), finds its index in `PIPELINE_STAGES`, and returns per-stage `done = completed || position < index` and `active = !completed && position === index && IN_FLIGHT_STATUSES.includes(status)` where in-flight is `staging|queued|processing|retry_wait`. A `completed` job forces `progress: 100` and all-done regardless of the last stage reported, because the terminal poll does not reliably carry `index`. `PipelineTimeline` paints that as a 7-node `<ol>` with a scaleY connector, the worker's `job.message` on the active node only, one `role="progressbar"`, an `sr-only aria-live` line, and a `retry_wait` footer showing `attempt_count/max_attempts` and the localized `next_retry_at`. `Upload.jsx`'s `IngestingPanel` renders `IngestScene3D` itself and passes `hide3D` so only one WebGL scene mounts.

**IngestOutcome.** Three branches, dispatched purely on `job.status`. `failed` → the message is tested with `/exact duplicate/i` against `job.error` (the string the worker's `DuplicateManuscriptIngestionError` produces at `services/ingestion.py:179`); a match retitles the screen "Already in the archive" and adds a View-archive button. `cancelled` → a warning shell. Anything else falls through to the success shell, which prints `job.chunks`, a hard-coded `768d / gemini-embedding-001` telemetry strip, `UploadScreening` and four static ASSURANCES tiles. `UploadScreening` renders nothing unless `scan.flagged`; when it does, it prints `verdictLabel`, four figures from `scanMetrics`, `MostSimilar(mostSimilarPaper(scan))` first (the "similar to *what*" fix), the remaining `matched_papers` as a list, and — only when `onReviewNovelty` was supplied — the Novelty Review cross-link.

**Batch upload (`UploadBatch.jsx`).** `batchReducer` over `createBatchState`. The 19 actions: `add-rows` (dedupe by `` `${name} ${size}` ``, append, then `slice(0, MAX_BATCH_FILES)`); `remove-row` by id; `set-row-field {id,key,value}`; `patch-row {id,patch}` where patch may be an updater (needed because picking a program rewrites four fields at once); `apply-extraction {files, departments}`; `apply-program {selection}`; `set-defaults-field` (**never dispatched**); `set-defaults` (object or updater); `set-default-errors`; `set-row-errors {errorsById}` which *replaces* every row's `errors`, clearing rows absent from the map; `set-step` (derives `direction`); `set-extracting`; `set-submitting`; `set-upload-progress`; `apply-submit-results`; `apply-job-statuses`; `set-poll-error` (**never dispatched**); `restore {rows}` which forces `step = ingesting, direction = 1`; `reset {department}`; `default` returns the same object by identity.

`applyExtraction` (batchState.js:137) indexes the response by `entry.index`, marks the row `extracted: true`, records `extractError` when the server rejected that file, and otherwise fills `title/authors/year` only where the row is still blank (`row.title || entry.title || ''`). The program is resolved with `programSelection(departments, state.defaults.department, entry.program_code, entry.specialization_code)` **and only when `row.program_id` is empty**, so a hand-set or bulk-applied program is never clobbered. `applyProgramToRows` deliberately overwrites every row *except* `extractError` ones. `applySubmitResults` maps result index → row id through the optional `rowIds` array (that is how "Retry N rejected" resubmits a subset and folds the answers back to the right rows); a result with `job_id` sets `jobId` and seeds `job` only if the row has none, a result without sets `submitError` + `submitStatusCode`. `applyJobStatuses` skips rows with no `jobId` or an already-terminal job (terminal rows are frozen against late polls), applies a matched job and resets `missingPolls`, and otherwise increments `missingPolls`, synthesizing `status:'expired'` at `MISSING_POLLS_BEFORE_EXPIRED`.

Page flow: step 0 `<Dropzone multiple>` → `addFiles` (892) re-derives room/duplicates/overflow **outside** the reducer purely to toast about the files `add-rows` will silently drop. `continueToReview` (910) POSTs `/upload/batch/extract-metadata` with all files in one request (the upload rate limit is per-minute and shared, so N calls would 429), dispatches `apply-extraction` with the catalog, toasts the reject count, and **advances to review in the `finally` block** so an extraction outage still lets the batch be filled in by hand. Step 1 renders `BatchDefaultsForm` (category + department only), `ProgramBulkBar` (keyed on `defaults.department` so switching college remounts it) and one `ReviewCard` per row. `updateDefaults` (866) clears every row's classification whenever the department changes, because a program belongs to one college. `submitRows(targets)` (935) runs `validateBatch`, pushes `rowErrorsById`/`defaultErrors` into state, refuses if any target lost its `File` (post-refresh rows have `file: null`), then POSTs `/upload/batch` with `rows` as a JSON string carrying per-row title/authors/year/idempotency_key/program_id/specialization_id and request-level department+category, tracking `onUploadProgress`. On success it folds results with `rowIds`, starts `pollStartedRef` if unset, clears `ceilingReached`, and moves to step 2.

Polling is React Query, not a timer: `useQuery({queryKey: ['upload-batch-jobs', idsKey], queryFn: () => getUploadJobs(idsKey.split(',')), enabled: hasActiveJobs && !ceilingReached, retry: false, refetchOnWindowFocus: false, refetchInterval: ... POLL_MS})` where `idsKey = activeJobIds(rows).join(',')`. Every time a job goes terminal the key changes, so a *new* cache entry is created and fetched immediately. An effect dispatches `apply-job-statuses` whenever `polledJobs` changes by reference. A separate effect arms a single `setTimeout` for `POLL_CEILING_MS - (now - pollStartedRef)` that flips `ceilingReached`. `resumePolling` re-stamps `pollStartedRef`, clears the ceiling and calls `refetchJobs()`. `finished = step === ingesting && allTerminal(rows)`; `reset()` clears storage, the refs, the ceiling and the reducer.

**Persistence.** Batch: `writeStoredBatch` runs on every `rows` change while ingesting and *removes* the key once `allTerminal`; `serializeActiveBatch` stamps `ownerId` and `restoreActiveBatch` refuses a missing owner, a mismatched owner, and the pre-2026-09-12 bare-array shape. The restore effect waits for `ownerId` and only then sets `restoredRef`. Single upload does none of this.

**Archive.** `useArchiveCatalog` holds the whole page's state. Three unconditional queries: `['papers']` via `listPapers(null)`, `['tracks']`, `['departments']`. `selectedDepartment = isSuperadmin ? filters.department : userDepartment` feeds `resolveArchiveTracks`/`resolveArchivePrograms`, so a non-superadmin's chips are pinned to their own college without a `department` filter ever being set. The derivation is a three-stage memo chain: `filterArchivePapers(papers, {...filters, superadmin})` → `sortArchivePapers(matched, sortBy, filters.query)` → `paginateItems(filtered, page, 6)`. Note the department predicate is `!filters.superadmin || !filters.department || paper.department === filters.department` — the client never filters by department for a non-superadmin, because the server already scoped the list. `setFilter` resets to page 1 and cascades: changing `department` clears track+program+specialization, changing `program_id` clears `specialization_id`. `setPage` computes `pageDirection` (`target >= page ? 1 : -1`) *before* committing, which is what lets `ArchiveResults` drive a `popLayout` filmstrip keyed on `page` with ±100% x-transforms; under `reducedMotion` it renders a plain grid instead. Query keys are deliberately coarse — everything is client-side over one `['papers']` payload, so filtering and paging never hit the network and `submitDelete` just invalidates `['papers']`. `PaperCard` reads `scanMetrics(currentScreening(paper.duplication_scan))` so the badge cannot disagree with the modal, shows the badge on `isScreeningFlagged` (which ORs the at-upload and rescan layers) and prefers `concentration` over `coverage`. `ScreeningDetail` picks `currentScreening` for the headline, collapses the at-upload run into a `<details>` only when `hasRescan && !screeningIsUnchanged`, and renders `archiveSizeLabel` so a stale verdict is dated against the corpus it saw.

**Novelty.** `ScanDropzone` validates extension (.pdf/.txt) ∪ `SCAN_MIME_TYPES` and a 25 MB literal, then `runScan` POSTs `/duplication/scan` with `isSuperadmin ? department || null : null`, sets `activeScan`, invalidates `['scan-history']` and toasts on `verdict === 'high_overlap'` vs otherwise. `ScanResult` is keyed on `scan.id`, so switching history rows resets its local `chatLog`/`question`. The ring and the "Closest single thesis" tile both read `metrics.concentration`; the follow-up chat optimistically appends the user turn, POSTs `/duplication/chat`, replaces the whole log with the server's trimmed 20, and on failure pops the optimistic turn. Keys come from `contentKeys` because the server trims the log and entries shift position. `downloadNoveltyReport` builds the JSON in-memory and never touches the network; `buildNoveltyReport` reads only `filename/created_at/department/threshold`, the `scanMetrics` figures and `top_matches` metadata — the test at `report.test.js:30` asserts no `matched_chunks[].uploaded_text`, no `database_text` and no `chat_log` leaks into the serialization.

**Dashboard.** One `['papers']` query shared with Archive; `stats` memo derives total / distinct tracks / year span / summed `chunk_count`. `isLoading` short-circuits to `<PageSkeleton/>` before any of the page renders. QuickActions are gated on `canArchive`/`canScan`/`canUpload` from AuthContext — mirroring, not enforcing, `routers/settings.py::DEFAULT_FEATURES`.

### Public surface

- UPLOAD_STEPS = Object.freeze({ manuscript:0, metadata:1, review:2, ingesting:3 }) — uploadState.js:1
- emptyUploadForm(department='CCSICT') → {title,authors,year,abstract,track,department,program_id,specialization_id,requires_specialization:false,thesis_category:'student'} — uploadState.js:3
- createUploadState(department='CCSICT') → {step,direction:1,file,form,errors,autofilled,job,submitting,parsing,pendingFile,pollError} — uploadState.js:11
- uploadReducer(state, action) — 12 actions: set-step (derives direction), set-file, set-pending-file, set-autofilled (keys[]→map), set-field (clears that one autofill claim), set-form (object or updater; drops every claim whose value changed), set-errors, set-job, set-submitting, set-parsing, set-poll-error, reset — uploadState.js:59
- isCurrentPoll({mounted, generation, currentGeneration, jobId, currentJobId}) → boolean — uploadState.js:91
- uploadMetadataErrors(form) → {title?,program_id?,specialization_id?,department?,year?}; title <5 chars after trim; program required unless thesis_category==='faculty'; specialization required when program_id && requires_specialization; year must be /^\d{4}$/ and 1978..currentYear+1 — uploadState.js:101
- WIZARD_STEPS = [manuscript, metadata, review] (3 entries; the ingesting step has no stepper node) — wizardSteps.js:11
- PIPELINE_STAGES = [download 'Secure source', malware_scan, extract, chunk 'Chunk (800 tokens)', embed 'Embed (768d)', screen 'Screen novelty (85%)', index] — wizardSteps.js:18
- clampPercent(value) → 0..100 integer, 0 for null/NaN/Infinity — wizardSteps.js:37
- stageView(job) → { index, progress, stages:[{key,label,done,active}] } — wizardSteps.js:48
- stepInteraction(index, current) → 'done' | 'current' | 'upcoming' — wizardSteps.js:76
- isTerminalJob(job) → status ∈ {completed, failed, cancelled} — wizardSteps.js:85
- railMode(step, job) → 'hidden'|'progress'|'steps'|'summary' — wizardSteps.js:102 (tested only; no runtime caller)
- summaryRows(form, {program, specialization, categoryLabel}) → rows with empty values dropped — wizardSteps.js:119 (tested only; no runtime caller)
- formatFileSize(bytes) → '<n> KB' below 1 MiB else 'n.nn MB'; '0 KB' for junk — wizardSteps.js:133

### Invariants

- `uploadMetadataErrors` (uploadState.js:101) is the single validator for BOTH wizards — `splitMetadataErrors` calls it per batch row and partitions the output by key. A rule added there applies to a batch row automatically; adding a rule whose key is in neither ROW_METADATA_KEYS nor DEFAULT_KEYS makes it silently unreportable in the batch UI.
- Year band is `1978 .. new Date().getFullYear() + 1`, recomputed per call — the tests at uploadState.test.js:55-58 derive their expectations from the clock rather than pinning a literal.
- Idempotency keys are minted client-side and must survive a failed submit: `createBatchRow` mints one per row and `serializeActiveBatch` persists it; `Upload.jsx` re-mints on file change (263/270) and on `reset` (400), and does NOT re-mint on a failed `submit`, so retrying a failed POST is idempotent.
- A batch row's `id` and `idempotencyKey` are the row identity; every reducer path addresses rows by `id`, never by index (`updateRow`, `remove-row`, `apply-submit-results` via `rowIds`). Index is used only to align the server's `{index}`-tagged extraction and submit results with the array order at the moment of the call.
- `apply-extraction` must never overwrite a non-blank row field, and must apply a program selection whole or not at all — a half-classified row (program from one college, specialization from another) is exactly what the server rejects.
- `restoreActiveBatch` returns null unless the stored `ownerId` strictly equals the current `user.id`; the pre-owner-stamp array shape is discarded outright. Pinned by batchState.test.js:388.
- Terminal batch rows are frozen: `applyJobStatuses` early-returns for `isTerminal(row.job)` so a late or replayed poll cannot walk a completed job backwards. Pinned by batchState.test.js:296.
- `stageView` must return an `active` stage only for `staging|queued|processing|retry_wait`, and must mark everything done for `completed` regardless of the reported stage.
- A `WizardStepper` node may render as a focusable `<button>` only when `stepInteraction` says 'done' AND `onSelect` was supplied. Playwright matches accessible names as case-insensitive substrings with no `exact: true` in the upload suite, so a focusable rail node named 'Review' or containing 'back' collides with the footer buttons. The `aria-label` is deliberately `Return to the <label> step`, not 'Go back to'.
- `nameFromLabel={false}` on every `Select` in MetadataForm/BatchDefaultsForm: `Field` otherwise lends its visible label as `aria-labelledby`, which outranks the `aria-label` the E2E suite matches these comboboxes by (e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:498-503, 585-590).
- Only `Select` renders its own `FieldErrorHint`; `Input`/`Textarea` emit theirs only when given a `label`. The batch `ReviewCard` cells label themselves with `CardLabel` + `aria-label`, so title/year use the local `<FieldError>` and program/specialization deliberately must NOT — rendering both trips a strict-mode violation (documented at UploadBatch.jsx:263-277).
- Verdict colour is always `verdictTone(metrics.verdict)` and the displayed figure is always `concentration` (falling back to `coverage` only when a legacy record carries no chunk counts). The badge, the panel surface, the meter fill and the history row all key off the same tone so they cannot disagree.
- `isScreeningFlagged` ORs the at-upload and rescan layers — never read `duplication_scan.flagged` directly, or a thesis clear at upload and flagged on recheck renders with no badge.
- The novelty export is metadata-only by contract: no `matched_chunks[].uploaded_text`, no `database_text`, no `chat_log`. `report_version` is '2026-07-25' and pinned by report.test.js.
- `MAX_BATCH_FILES = 20` mirrors `config.max_batch_files` and `MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 MB` mirrors `config.max_upload_mb`; both are client hints — `routers/upload.py:929` and `:493` are the enforcement.
- Both upload pages must clear their sessionStorage key on terminal state, or a refresh resumes a finished job.
- `WizardStep` uses `mode="wait"`, never `popLayout`: overlapping panes would briefly mount two copies of the manuscript title, which is a Playwright strict-mode violation on the review step's `getByText` assertions.

### Data model

- `uploadState` shape: `{ step:0..3, direction:±1, file:File|null, form:{title,authors,year,abstract,track,department,program_id,specialization_id,requires_specialization,thesis_category}, errors:{[field]:string}, autofilled:{[key]:true}, job:UploadJobStatus|null, submitting, parsing, pendingFile:File|null, pollError:string }`
- `batchState` shape: `{ step:0..2, direction:±1, rows:BatchRow[], defaults:{department,thesis_category}, defaultErrors:{department?,rows?}, extracting, submitting, uploadProgress:0..100, pollError:'' (inert) }`
- `BatchRow`: `{ id, idempotencyKey, file:File|null, name, size, title, authors, year, program_id, specialization_id, requires_specialization, track, extracted:bool, extractError:string|null, errors:{}, jobId:string|null, submitError:string|null, submitStatusCode:number|null, job:UploadJobStatus|null, missingPolls:number }`
- Server `UploadJobStatus` (models.py:122) consumed field-by-field: `job_id, status, stage, progress, message, paper_id, chunks, duplication, error, attempt_count, max_attempts, next_retry_at, cancel_requested, cancelled_at, can_cancel, last_event_at`. `last_event_at` and `paper_id` are never read by any of these pages.
- Server `UploadCancelResponse` (models.py:148): `{job_id, outcome, status, message, cancel_requested, cancelled_at}`. `outcome` unused by both pages; `message` used only by the batch page.
- Server `BatchExtractedFile` (models.py:187): `{index, filename, title, authors, year, department, program_code, specialization_code, error, status_code}`. `department` is read by nothing on the client.
- Server `BatchFileResult` (models.py:206): `{index, filename, idempotency_key, job_id, status, message, error, status_code}`. `idempotency_key` is echoed back but not reconciled client-side.
- `sessionStorage['activeUploadJob']` = `{jobId, idempotencyKey, title}` — no owner stamp.
- `sessionStorage['activeUploadBatch']` = `{ownerId, rows:[{id,name,size,title,idempotencyKey,jobId}]}` — owner-stamped since 2026-09-12.
- React Query keys touched: `['departments']` (Upload, UploadBatch, Archive, Novelty-superadmin-only), `['papers']` (Archive, Dashboard; invalidated by Upload on completion, by UploadBatch on first completion, by Archive on delete), `['tracks']` (Archive), `['upload-batch-jobs', idsKey]` (UploadBatch; key churns as jobs go terminal), `['scan-history']` (Novelty).
- Archive `filters` object: `{query, track, program_id, specialization_id, year, department, thesis_category}` — all strings, `''` means unset; `hasFilters = Object.values(filters).some(Boolean)`.
- Duplication record shape read through `lib/utils.js`: `{flagged, highest_similarity, matched_chunk_percentage|duplication_percentage, matched_chunk_count, total_chunks, verdict_level, threshold, archive_size, archive_size_estimated, screened_at, matched_papers[]|top_matches[], most_similar_paper, rescan:{…same shape…}}`. `scanMetrics` derives `{highest, coverage, concentration, matchedChunks, totalChunks, verdict}`.
- Novelty scan record: `{id, filename, created_at, department, threshold, highest_similarity, matched_chunk_percentage, matched_chunk_count, total_chunks, verdict_level, verdict_summary, top_matches[], matched_chunks[], chat_log[{role,content}]}`.

### Config knobs

- `MAX_BATCH_FILES = 20` (batchState.js:30) — client mirror of backend `config.max_batch_files` (default 20, ge=1 le=50). Drift makes the UI cap disagree with the 413 at routers/upload.py:929.
- `MAX_FILE_BYTES = 25 MB` (batchState.js:31) — mirror of `config.max_upload_mb = 25`. Duplicated again as a literal in Novelty.jsx:75.
- `_MAX_JOB_IDS = 50` (routers/upload.py:1053) — the batch page can only ever send 20, so this is slack.
- `POLL_MS = 2000`, `MAX_POLL_FAILURES = 5` (inert), `POLL_CEILING_MS = 30 min` — UploadBatch.jsx:39-41.
- Single-upload poll cadence: 500 ms first tick, 1500 ms steady, `1500 * failures` backoff, 5-failure stop, 30-minute ceiling — all inline literals in Upload.jsx:288-352.
- `MISSING_POLLS_BEFORE_EXPIRED = 3` — batchState.js:35.
- `ARCHIVE_PAGE_SIZE = 6` — useArchiveCatalog.js:15; `ARCHIVE_SKELETONS = slotKeys(6, …)` must match.
- `REVIEW_MARK = 25` / `HIGH_OVERLAP_MARK = 70` (Archive.jsx:62-63) — the drawn band boundaries; must track `services/novelty.py::verdict_for_concentration` and `verdictTone`.
- `report_version = '2026-07-25'` (report.js:10) — bump on any export-shape change.
- `ACTIVE_BATCH_STORAGE_KEY = 'activeUploadBatch'`; the single wizard's `'activeUploadJob'` has no constant.
- `SCAN_MIME_TYPES` (Novelty.jsx:39) — must stay in step with the `/duplication/scan` contract.
- Auth-derived gates: `canUpload`, `canScan`, `canArchive`, `isSuperadmin`, `department` from AuthContext, themselves derived from `system_settings.role_features` mirrored in `src/lib/permissions.js`.
- Extraction request timeouts: 10 min for `/upload/batch/extract-metadata`, 15 min for `/upload/batch` (api.js:288, :308) against the axios instance default of 3 min.
- ESLint `no-unused-vars` `varsIgnorePattern: '^[A-Z_]'` (eslint.config.js:38) — the reason dead components are not flagged. `npm run lint` is `eslint .` with no `--max-warnings`, and this subsystem currently lints clean.

### Error handling

- Single-upload polling: 404 → permanent stop with 'This upload job has expired or is no longer available.'; any other error → linear backoff `1500 * failures` up to 5 consecutive failures, then stop with 'The server could not confirm the upload status. The job was not cancelled.'; 30-minute wall-clock ceiling → 'Status checking paused after 30 minutes. You can resume it safely.' All three surface a 'Resume status check' button inside `IngestingPanel`.
- Batch polling: `retry: false`, ceiling at 30 minutes → 'Status checking paused after 30 minutes. The worker keeps running.' with a Resume button. The failure-count branch exists but is unreachable (see gotchas), so transport failures are invisible.
- `extractMetadata` failure (single) → `toast.error('Autofill failed')`, the wizard continues with a blank form. `extractMetadataBatch` failure → `toast.warning('Metadata extraction unavailable')` and the page **still advances to review** (the `finally` at UploadBatch.jsx:930) so rows can be filled in by hand.
- Per-file extraction rejection: `applyExtraction` stores `extractError`, `validateBatch` turns it into `rowErrors.file = 'Remove this file: …'`, the `ReviewCard` turns flame-red with an inline explanation, and a 'Remove N rejected' bulk button appears in the section heading.
- Per-file submit rejection: `submitError` + `submitStatusCode` on the row, a flame 'Not accepted' badge in the progress table, and a 'Retry N rejected' button that resubmits only `rows.filter(r => r.submitError && r.file)` via the `rowIds` mapping. Rows whose `File` was lost to a refresh are refused up front with 'Files unavailable'.
- Validation failure on submit → `toast.error('Check the highlighted fields')` plus per-row and shared-card error state; `defaultErrors.rows` ('Add at least one manuscript') renders above the list.
- `getDepartments` failure → an inline `role="alert"` strip with a Retry button on both wizards (Upload.jsx:44, UploadBatch.jsx:1088); on Novelty it degrades to a single line ('Department choices could not be loaded. The enforced default remains active.') because the server pins the department anyway.
- Archive `['papers']` failure → full-card `EmptyState` 'Archive unavailable' with Retry. Dashboard shows a dedicated banner whose copy explicitly says 'No unavailable values are being presented as measured zeros' and swaps the stat tiles for skeletons.
- Novelty history failure prints `apiErrorMessage(historyFailure, …)` verbatim — deliberate, so a privilege or `PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL` refusal is actionable — and shows Retry only when `isRetryableFailure`.
- Cancellation failures toast and leave the row/job untouched; both dialogs close in `finally`.
- `writeStoredBatch`/`readStoredBatch` swallow storage exceptions (private mode) and degrade to no-resume. `Upload.jsx` does not (see gotchas).
- `Dropzone` aggregates per-file validation problems and toasts at most three of them joined by ' · '; files that fail are dropped, valid ones still proceed.

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/uploadState.test.js — 8 tests: full reducer walk through every step and terminal job, `reset` deep-equality against a fresh state, the student-thesis default, `isCurrentPoll` staleness across generation/job-id/unmount, the whole `uploadMetadataErrors` matrix (including the faculty exemption and clock-derived year bounds), direction tracking, and the two autofill-claim rules (`set-field` clears one key; `set-form` compares before/after and preserves object identity when nothing claimed changed).
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/wizardSteps.test.js — 13 tests: the 3-step / 7-stage counts, `clampPercent` against null/junk/Infinity/negatives/'42.6', the `store`/`queued`/'' stage aliases, done-vs-active semantics per status, the completed-forces-100 rule, a null job, `stepInteraction`, `railMode` for all four steps and all three terminal statuses, `summaryRows` dropping blanks and an unpicked program, `formatFileSize` sub-megabyte behaviour, `autofilledKeys`, and `extractionDepartment`'s superadmin-only rule.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/upload/batchState.test.js — 23 tests: `fileValidationError`, stable row ids, dedupe + cap, id-addressed updates, extraction folding with per-file rejections, the error split between row and shared card, mixed-program validation, extraction resolved against the batch department, `apply-program` skipping rejected rows, both `apply-submit-results` modes (index and `rowIds` partial resubmit), `apply-job-statuses` incl. the expiry counter and the terminal freeze, `batchSummary` across every status, tone/label coverage, serialize/restore round-trip, **cross-account restore refusal and the legacy-shape discard**, the flag/progress/reset actions and unknown-action identity, `stageLabel`/`batchProgress`, the wizard step names and direction, and `batchRailMode`.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/archiveCatalog.test.js — 8 tests: legacy-safe filtering with null entries, combined filters, thesis-category filtering treating pre-migration rows as 'student', normalized program/specialization ids, `archiveYears` ordering, the department-derived track label and the mixed-catalog fallback, department-owned program/specialization options, and all seven sort comparators plus relevance scoring and null/empty inputs.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.test.js — 2 tests: the export carries the useful metadata and the serialized JSON contains none of `uploaded_text` / `database_text` / `chat_log`; and a null scan fails closed to 'Unnamed submission' with zeroed advisory counts.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:430-518 — the single-upload journey: extract → confirm → program+specialization → review → ingest, asserts the `retry_wait` notice and the Cancel button, asserts the idempotency key is a UUID, then **reloads mid-job** to prove sessionStorage resume, and asserts 'Thesis indexed!' + '22 embedded chunks'.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:520-640 — the batch journey: two manuscripts from two degrees, asserts '2 / 20 manuscripts selected', per-row autofill, that the unclassified row blocks only itself, the bulk 'Apply to all rows' bar, specialization reveal/hide as programs change, and finally inspects the multipart `rows` JSON to prove per-row titles, authors and distinct idempotency keys reach the server.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js:367 — axe sweep of `/upload/batch`.
- No test covers: `Dropzone`, `PipelineTimeline`, `IngestOutcome`, `MetadataForm`, `ReviewPanel`, `WizardStep`/`useAutoHeight`, `ArchiveFiltersBar`, `useArchiveCatalog`, or any of `Novelty.jsx`'s component behaviour (only `report.js` is unit-tested).

### Gotchas

- **The batch page's 5-failure poll pause is unreachable dead code.** `UploadBatch.jsx:829` gates `refetchInterval` on `query.state.fetchFailureCount < MAX_POLL_FAILURES (5)` and `:833` derives `failurePaused = pollFailures >= 5`. With `retry: false`, `@tanstack/query-core@5.101.4` resets `fetchFailureCount` to 0 at the *start* of every fetch (`fetchState()`, query.js:413, spread by the `"fetch"` action at :356) and the retryer never calls `onFail` when `shouldRetry` is false (retryer.js:90-95), so only the `"error"` action increments it — to exactly 1, every time. The counter oscillates 0↔1 and never reaches 5. Consequence: a persistently failing `/upload/jobs` is polled every 2 s for the full 30 minutes with **no** error banner and no Resume button, and the string 'The server could not confirm the batch status. No job was cancelled.' can never render. `Upload.jsx`'s hand-rolled `pollFailuresRef` (345) does accumulate correctly, so the single-upload page has the behaviour the batch page only appears to have.
- **A vanished batch job can get stuck one poll short of `expired`.** `missingPolls` advances only inside `apply-job-statuses`, which is dispatched from `useEffect(..., [polledJobs])` (UploadBatch.jsx:842). TanStack's default `structuralSharing` returns the *previous* reference when a response is deeply equal, so identical consecutive payloads fire the effect once, not once per poll. The common stall — the only remaining job has 404'd server-side and `/upload/jobs` returns `{jobs: []}` on every tick — dispatches exactly once, leaves `missingPolls` at 1, never reaches `MISSING_POLLS_BEFORE_EXPIRED = 3`, so `allTerminal` stays false, `finished` never flips, and the row sits on 'Queued' forever.
- **`Upload.jsx` never owner-stamps its resume state.** It writes `{jobId, idempotencyKey, title}` to the raw string key `'activeUploadJob'` (378) and restores it on mount (356) with no idea who is signed in. `sessionStorage` is per-tab but is NOT cleared by signing out — which is precisely the leak the batch page was hardened against on 2026-09-12 (documented at batchState.js:373-382 and pinned by batchState.test.js:388). The single-upload page still renders the previous account's thesis title into the next reader's wizard in a shared tab. The job id itself is owner-scoped server-side and 404s, but the title has already painted.
- **`Upload.jsx`'s sessionStorage calls are unguarded.** `getItem` at :356 sits *outside* the effect's try block, so a throwing storage implementation (private mode, blocked site data) crashes the route on mount. Worse, `setItem` at :378 sits *inside* `submit()`'s try: a storage exception thrown after a successful `POST /upload/paper` is caught by the generic handler at :387, toasts 'Upload failed', and abandons a job the server has already queued — no polling starts and the user has no job id. `removeItem` at :399 is also unguarded. `UploadBatch.jsx` wraps all three (`readStoredBatch` 754, `writeStoredBatch` 762, `reset` 1008).
- **`src/components/upload/WizardRail.jsx` is entirely dead** — `WizardRail`, `RailFileChip`, `RailSummaryList` and `RailHeading` have zero importers in `src/` or `e2e/`. With them, `railMode` (wizardSteps.js:102), `summaryRows` (:119) and `batchRailMode` (batchState.js:27) are reachable only from their unit tests. ESLint cannot see it because `no-unused-vars` is configured with `varsIgnorePattern: '^[A-Z_]'` (eslint.config.js:38), which exempts every capitalised import.
- **Two batch reducer actions are never dispatched**: `set-defaults-field` (batchState.js:227) and `set-poll-error` (:239). `state.pollError` is initialised in `createBatchState` but is not destructured at UploadBatch.jsx:776 and is shadowed by a same-named local `const pollError` at :834, so the field is inert.
- **Radix `Select` silently drops every `<option value="">`.** `Input.jsx:336` builds `selectableOptions` by filtering out the empty value, and `SelectDropdownPortal` renders only those. So 'All programs', 'All departments', 'All years', 'All specializations' (ArchiveFiltersBar), 'Select a Department…' (BatchDefaultsForm), 'Select program…' (MetadataForm, ReviewCard, ProgramBulkBar) and 'Default (CCSICT)' (Novelty.jsx:410) are **never in the dropdown** — they only supply the placeholder text. Once a value is picked there is no in-control way back to blank. The archive is rescued by the active-filter chips' X and 'Clear all'; the Novelty department picker and the upload program pickers have no escape at all.
- **Choosing a second file in the single wizard inherits the first file's metadata.** `handleFileSelect` (263) only sets `pendingFile`; the form is not cleared, and `runAutofill`'s merge is `patch.title || prev.title`, so if the new title page cannot be read the previous manuscript's title, authors and year stay in the form and go to the server. Only `handleFileSelect(null)` (the Remove button) resets the form.
- **The batch discards every per-file department the extractor returns.** `BatchExtractedFile.department` is populated by `routers/upload.py::extract_metadata_batch`, is asserted in the e2e fixture, and is never read by `applyExtraction` (batchState.js:137) — the program code is always resolved against the shared `defaults.department`. A superadmin batching manuscripts from two colleges loses the classification of every file outside the selected one, silently (`programSelection` returns null and the row simply keeps a blank program).
- **`Autofilled` is claimed for files the extractor read nothing from.** `_extracted_file(index, filename)` with no data returns blank strings and no `error`, and `applyExtraction` sets `extracted: true` unconditionally on the no-error path, so `ReviewCard` (UploadBatch.jsx:358) shows the green Sparkles 'Autofilled' badge on a completely empty row. The single wizard gets this right — `autofilledKeys` only credits non-blank values.
- **`addFiles` (UploadBatch.jsx:892) duplicates the reducer's dedupe/cap arithmetic** to produce toasts, re-deriving `` `${row.name} ${row.size}` `` and the `slice` semantics that `addRows` (batchState.js:110) owns. Two copies of the same rule; they agree today.
- **`rowPresentation` (UploadBatch.jsx:461) hard-codes its own terminal list** (`completed|failed|cancelled|expired`) instead of calling `isTerminal`/`TERMINAL_STATUSES`. Same for `StatusIcon` (:480). Adding a status means editing three places.
- **`invalidatedRef` invalidates `['papers']` exactly once per batch** (UploadBatch.jsx:855), on the first completion only. Later completions in the same batch do not refresh the archive cache; it is rescued only because Archive refetches on mount.
- **`Upload.jsx::confirmCancellation` ignores the server's `UploadCancelResponse.message`** and hand-writes two English strings (:413-415), while `UploadBatch.jsx::confirmCancellation` (:993) uses `result.message`. `outcome` is ignored by both. The optimistic `setJob` is also spread from the render-closure `job` and is overwritten 1.5 s later by the next poll.
- **`UploadBatch` polling has a latent instant-ceiling path**: the effect at :846 computes `POLL_CEILING_MS - (Date.now() - pollStartedRef.current)`, and if `pollStartedRef` is still 0 while `hasActiveJobs` is true the remainder is hugely negative and `ceilingReached` fires immediately. Currently unreachable (both the restore path and `submitRows` stamp the ref first), but nothing enforces it.
- **`IngestingStep`'s 3D stage mapping never yields `chunk` or `screen`** — the nested ternary at UploadBatch.jsx:669 goes download→malware_scan→extract→embed→index by progress band. Cosmetic, but the scene misreports the pipeline.
- **Archive `page` is never clamped back when the result set shrinks.** `paginateItems` and `GooglePagination` both clamp internally, so the UI looks right after deleting the last item on the last page, but the `page` state stays out of range and the next `setPage` computes `pageDirection` from the stale number — the filmstrip can slide the wrong way. `setFilter`/`clearFilters`/`setSortBy` reset to 1; `submitDelete` and background refetches do not.
- **`buildNoveltyReport` omits `concentration`** — the figure the page leads with, that `ProgressRing` visualises, that the toast quotes, and that the backend's `verdict_for_concentration` actually grades on. The export carries `highest_passage_similarity_percent` and `matched_chunk_coverage_percent` only, so a downloaded advisory cannot be reconciled with the screen it came from. `verdict_summary` (the AI prose) is also dropped without comment.
- **`downloadNoveltyReport` revokes the object URL synchronously after `anchor.click()`** (report.js:55-56) and never attaches the anchor to the DOM. It works in current Chromium/Firefox but is a known race in some engines.
- **`Novelty.jsx`'s file input never resets `e.target.value`** (:83), so re-selecting the same manuscript after a failed scan or a toast-rejected validation fires no `change` event and appears dead. The shared `Dropzone` does reset it (Dropzone.jsx:86). The same file also duplicates the 25 MB limit as a literal (:75) instead of using `MAX_FILE_BYTES`/`fileValidationError`.
- **`Dashboard.jsx` has two dead loading branches** — `isLoading` returns `<PageSkeleton/>` at :132, so `isLoading || papersError` at :181 and `isLoading ?` at :238 can only ever take the error/else path. `STAT_SKELETONS`/`RECENT_SKELETONS` render only via the `papersError` arm.
- `Dashboard` calls `listPapers()` and `useArchiveCatalog` calls `listPapers(null)` under the *same* `['papers']` query key — harmless only because both produce the identical unparameterised request. A future default argument on `listPapers` would make the two callers disagree while sharing a cache entry.
- **`ScreeningDetail` computes four values before its `if (!isScreeningFlagged(scan)) return null`** guard (Archive.jsx:240-245) — wasted work on every unflagged card in the modal, and a trap if a hook is ever added above the guard.
- `PaperCard` (Archive.jsx:304) wraps a single `<GlassCard>` in a redundant fragment; the card keeps an `onClick` with no role purely as a mouse-target widener, which means the whole card is clickable but exposes nothing to AT — intentional (R9 comment) but easy to 'fix' back into a nested-button violation.
- **`ArchiveFiltersBar` shows a track picker only when the selected department has no programs** (`programs.length > 0 ? … : activeTracks.length > 0 ? …`, :181-207) — a department with both a normalized program catalog and legacy free-text tracks can never be filtered by track, and any `filters.track` set earlier stays applied but invisible except as a chip.
- `sortArchivePapers` mutates in place (`copy.sort(...)`) but `copy` is a fresh `papers.filter(Boolean)`, so the React Query cache is safe — do not remove the `.filter()` as an optimisation.
- `relevanceScore` (archiveFilters.js:16) is only consulted when `sortBy === 'relevance' && query`; selecting 'Most relevant' with an empty search box silently falls through to `compareByNewest`.
- `ProgramBulkBar` is keyed on `defaults.department` in JSX (UploadBatch.jsx:1119) so it remounts on a department switch; it *also* guards `incomplete` off the resolved program object rather than the raw id, because the guard has to hold in the frame before the remount.
- `AutoGrowTitle` (UploadBatch.jsx:232) listens on `window.resize` rather than using a `ResizeObserver` on itself — deliberate, since the callback resizes the observed element — so it does not re-fit when the card narrows for any reason other than a viewport change (e.g. a specialization cell appearing).
- `useAutoHeight` returns `'auto'` when `ResizeObserver` is undefined; under jsdom-free unit runs and in reduced-motion the height spring is simply skipped, so nothing in `WizardStep` is covered by the node test suite.
- The 3D scene is lazily imported twice — `Upload.jsx:32` and `PipelineTimeline.jsx:9` both `lazy(() => import('../components/three/IngestScene3D'))`. Vite resolves these to one chunk, but the `bundle:budget` gate depends on both staying lazy.

### Open questions

- Was the batch page's `MAX_POLL_FAILURES` guard ever verified against TanStack v5's `fetchState()` reset, or was it written against the v4 semantics? The single-upload page's equivalent works, which suggests the batch version was assumed rather than observed.
- Is the missing owner stamp on `sessionStorage['activeUploadJob']` an oversight, or was the 2026-09-12 audit scoped to the batch page only? The threat model in batchState.js:373-382 applies verbatim to the single wizard.
- Should `buildNoveltyReport` carry `concentration` (and possibly `top_paper_percentage` / `verdict_summary`)? The export currently cannot reproduce the verdict it states, since the backend grades on concentration.
- `WizardRail.jsx`, `railMode`, `summaryRows` and `batchRailMode` are a complete, tested rail feature with no call site — was the rail removed from both wizards in favour of the horizontal `WizardStepper` header, and should the module and its tests be deleted or the rail restored?
- The batch drops `BatchExtractedFile.department`. Was a per-row department deliberately rejected (the department is request-level on `POST /upload/batch`), or is this an unfinished half of the same change that moved `program_id` into the rows?
- `filterArchivePapers`'s department predicate is superadmin-gated on the client while the server already scopes `/papers` — is the client-side check intended as defence in depth, or is it just a leftover that makes the non-superadmin `filters.department` key permanently inert?

**Depends on:** src/api.js (the only transport: uploadPaper, getUploadStatus, cancelUploadJob, extractMetadata, extractMetadataBatch, uploadBatch, getUploadJobs, listPapers, deletePaper, getTracks, getDepartments, scanDuplication, getScanHistory, deleteScanHistory, clearScanHistory, scanDuplicationChat, apiErrorMessage, isRetryableFailure), src/context/AuthContext (user.id, department, isAdmin, isSuperadmin, canUpload, canScan, canArchive, displayName, role), src/context/PreferencesContext (reducedMotion — gates the archive filmstrip and useAutoHeight), src/lib/utils.js (cn, formatDate, timeAgo, normalizePercent, scanMetrics, currentScreening, atUploadScreening, hasRescan, isScreeningFlagged, screeningIsUnchanged, archiveSizeLabel, mostSimilarPaper, verdictLabel, verdictTone, verdictExplanation), src/lib/catalog.js, src/lib/pagination.js, src/lib/keys.js (slotKeys, contentKeys), src/components/ui/* (GlassCard, Button, Badge, Input/Textarea/Select/Field, Modal/ConfirmDialog, Skeleton, PageSkeleton, EmptyState, Motion, Pagination/GooglePagination, ProgressRing, TableScroller), src/components/three/IngestScene3D (lazy, props: progress, stage, stageKey), src/hooks/useMediaQuery (WizardRail only — dead), src/components/MfaEnrollDialog + useMfaStatus (Dashboard SecurityNudge), @tanstack/react-query 5.101.4, framer-motion, sonner, lucide-react, react-markdown, react-router, @radix-ui select (via components/ui/Input), Backend contracts: routers/upload.py (/upload/paper, /upload/status/{id}, /upload/jobs, /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel, /upload/extract-metadata, /upload/batch/extract-metadata, /upload/batch, /upload/tracks), routers/papers.py, routers/duplication.py, routers/catalog.py (/catalog/departments/legacy)

**Used by:** src/App.jsx — all five pages are lazy routes behind ProtectedRoute: /dashboard (auth only), /archive (canArchive), /novelty (canScan), /upload and /upload/batch (canUpload), vite.config.js — the /upload, /papers, /duplication, /catalog and /analytics prefixes must each have a proxy entry; /upload is both a React route and an API prefix so it uses the HTML-aware bypass, e2e/critical-flows.spec.js and e2e/accessibility.spec.js, src/components/upload/Dropzone.jsx imports fileValidationError + MAX_BATCH_FILES from pages/upload/batchState.js, and formatFileSize from pages/upload/wizardSteps.js — a components→pages import direction that is worth knowing about before moving either module

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 13 correction(s), 8 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

MetadataForm's own in-source comment cites e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:489-492 (MetadataForm.jsx:103), not the ranges the map gives. Everything else these claims assert is correct. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/upload/MetadataForm.jsx:103`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- **A storage throw on a TERMINAL poll silently converts a completed upload into a poll failure.** The map catalogues three unguarded sessionStorage calls in Upload.jsx (getItem :356, setItem :378, removeItem :399) but misses the fourth shape: the three `sessionStorage.removeItem('activeUploadJob')` calls on the completed/failed/cancelled branches (Upload.jsx:315, :329, :332) sit *inside* the poll's own `try { const status = await getUploadStatus(jobId) … }` (:311-342). A storage exception there is caught by the catch at :343, increments pollFailuresRef, and reschedules — so the success toast never fires, the terminal `setJob` has already landed but the loop keeps polling, and after five such ticks the page reports 'The server could not confirm the upload status. The job was not cancelled.' for a job that finished. UploadBatch's writeStoredBatch (:762-770) wraps this; Upload.jsx does not.
- **`allTerminal` treats a row with no jobId as terminal** (batchState.js:322: `!row.jobId || isTerminal(row.job)`). The map describes it only through `activeJobIds`. Consequence: a batch where every row was rejected at submit (submitError set, jobId null) makes `finished` true on the same tick, `writeStoredBatch` removes the storage key (:763), and IngestingStep renders the FinishedBanner — reading '0 of 2 manuscripts are now part of the semantic archive' beside a live 'Retry 2 rejected' button. `hasActiveJobs` is also false, so nothing polls.
- **`FinishedBanner`'s success test is against total rows, not accepted rows.** `allGood = summary.completed === summary.total` (UploadBatch.jsx:616), and `batchSummary.total = rows.length` including permanently rejected rows (batchState.js:327). A batch of 3 where 2 index cleanly and 1 was rejected at submit can never show 'Batch indexed!' even after the rejected row is removed from consideration — the only way out is deleting the row, which the ingesting step offers no control for.
- **`updateDefaults` fires the classification wipe on any department-keyed patch, not on an actual change.** UploadBatch.jsx:871-877 gates on `patch.department !== undefined`, so a superadmin re-selecting the department they already had blanks program_id, specialization_id, requires_specialization and track on every row (via `apply-program`, which also skips extractError rows). The map says 'whenever the department changes'.
- **`requires_specialization` and `track` are client-only and never transmitted.** `uploadPaper` (api.js:238-260) appends title/authors/year/abstract/track/department/thesis_category/program_id/specialization_id — it does send `track`, but not `requires_specialization`. The batch `rows` payload (UploadBatch.jsx:950-956) sends only title/authors/year/idempotency_key/program_id/specialization_id, so neither `track` nor `requires_specialization` reaches `POST /upload/batch`; the server re-derives the track through `services/catalog.resolve_academic_selection`. The map's BatchRow dataModel lists both fields without noting the asymmetry.
- **`getUploadJobs` round-trips the id list through a string.** api.js:313-316 sends `ids` as one comma-joined query param and UploadBatch.jsx:827 rebuilds the array with `idsKey.split(',')`, so a blank `idsKey` would send `['']`. Only `enabled: hasActiveJobs` (:826) prevents it — the same latent-guard shape the map flags for `pollStartedRef`, and worth pairing with it.
- **`Novelty.jsx`'s department badge is a local guess, not what was sent.** `effectiveDepartment` (Novelty.jsx:344-346) computes `userDepartment || 'CCSICT'` for a non-superadmin and renders it as a Badge (:415), while `runScan` posts `isSuperadmin ? department || null : null` (:382) and lets `resolve_effective_department` pin it server-side. If the profile department is stale the badge and the scan disagree.
- **The single wizard hides its stepper entirely during ingestion** (`{step < UPLOAD_STEPS.ingesting && …}`, Upload.jsx:454) and the batch does the same (UploadBatch.jsx:1043), which is why Upload passes `onSelect={setStep}` unconditionally while UploadBatch redundantly re-guards it as `step < BATCH_STEPS.ingesting ? setStep : undefined` (:1050). Relevant to the map's WizardStepper strict-mode invariant: the 'done' state is not the only thing keeping rail nodes out of the ingesting step's accessibility tree.

## 4. `fe-admin-auth` - Frontend admin console, settings and auth flows

The privileged and account-management half of the React SPA: the four-tab administration console (/admin), the six-section account surface (/settings), the multi-step sign-in card (/login), the TOTP enrollment and privileged-MFA recovery dialogs, and the unauthenticated Landing and NotFound pages. It renders server-owned state only — every role, department, feature-flag and approval decision is enforced in FastAPI (dependencies/auth.py), and these screens are a thin, deliberately fail-loud presentation of it. The auth card is a render-derived state machine over Supabase Auth, not an effect-driven wizard; the admin tabs are React Query readers plus a small number of mutations against /analytics, /maintenance, /departments and /settings.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Admin.jsx` (176 lines) - Admin shell: tab list (overview / upload_history / system, + operations for superadmin), lazy tab chunks, and a client-side 2FA gate (`adminSecurityState`) that runs before any tab mounts.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/AdminOverview.jsx` (383 lines) - Overview tab. Three parallel queries (analytics-overview / analytics-activity / users), stat tiles, the lazy Recharts panels, the Objective 2 evidence card, and the inline role-change Select. UNCOMMITTED: +110/-7, replacing a 'no figures' placeholder card with a live table fed by src/data/objective2Summary.json.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/OverviewCharts.jsx` (92 lines) - The two Recharts panels, split into their own chunk; must stay lazy (bundle budget pins it at 115 kB gzipped).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/SystemManagementTab.jsx` (784 lines) - Largest admin surface: user directory (search/role/status/dept filters, inline edit, approve/reject/delete), raw system logs, superadmin-only role-feature editor and departments editor, and the papers/buckets table.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/OperationsTab.jsx` (202 lines) - Superadmin-only. Five /maintenance queries on 15–30 s refetch intervals: summary metrics, workers, alerts (with acknowledge), durable jobs, retention dry run (deliberately outside the page-level gates).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/UploadHistoryTab.jsx` (206 lines) - Six-node RAG pipeline diagram plus a filtered archive table (query / track / department / thesis-category / year).
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Settings.jsx` (102 lines) - URL-derived section rail (?section=…) over six statically imported section components.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/SecuritySection.jsx` (241 lines) - 2FA row + EmailCard (updateUser → verifyOtp 'email_change'), PasswordCard (resetPasswordForEmail → verifyOtp 'recovery' → updateUser), SessionsCard (signOut scope:'others').
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/ProfileSection.jsx` (183 lines) - Avatar upload/remove against the Supabase `avatars` bucket with rollback, plus the full_name form; role/department are read-only.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/ChatSection.jsx` (153 lines) - localStorage chat prefs (default category, Enter-to-send) and bulk conversation deletion.

Generated Objective 2 result (run 5e8fb7f21db6, 2026-09-13, 40/40, ragas==0.4.3): five strata with baseline/rag/delta/p/CI/Cohen's d_z, quoted_stratum='present', faithfulness 0.6634 and context_precision 0.1944.

- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py` (118 lines) - UNTRACKED. Asserts the committed JSON equals build(), is LF-terminated, has exactly one quoted stratum, and that `present` stays n=16 / p=0.1257 / not significant.

### Control flow

**Auth state machine (Login.jsx).** `step` is local state initialised to `'reset'` when `type=recovery` appears in `location.hash + location.search`, else `'signin'` (Login.jsx:35-36, :95). Exactly one effect writes it: an `onAuthStateChange` subscription that sets `'reset'` on `PASSWORD_RECOVERY` (:120-125). Everything else is derived at render into `effectiveStep` (:127-133): if `user && needsMfa && step∉{reset,success}` → `'verify'`; else if `user && !needsMfa && step∉{reset,forgot}` → `awaitingVerify ? 'verify' : 'success'`. `awaitingVerify` is armed by `SignInForm.onPasswordSuccess` immediately before `reloadSession()` (SignInForm.jsx:103-105), so every password login lands on the verification picker; magic-link, signup-confirm and recovery sign-ins never set it and therefore fall straight to `'success'`, whose `SuccessStep` fires `onDone` after 1500 ms and navigates to `safeNextPath(searchParams.get('next'))` (Login.jsx:105, :202). Slide direction comes from a second piece of state adjusted *during* render (the React "adjust state when props change" pattern, :138-146): `Math.sign(STEP_DEPTH[next] - STEP_DEPTH[prev])`, falling back to tab order for signin↔signup. Step transitions: signin→forgot (`onForgot`), signin→otp (`onOtpSent` after `signInWithOtp({shouldCreateUser:false})`), signin→verifyEmail (on the `email not confirmed` error, SignInForm.jsx:109-112), signup→verifyEmail (`onVerifyNeeded` when `signUp` returns no session), verify→signin (`onBack` calls `supabase.auth.signOut()` then clears `awaitingVerify`, Login.jsx:185-189), reset→success (`ResetPasswordStep.onDone`). `VerifyMethodStep` receives `totpEnrolled={needsMfa}` (:184) — the context's `needsMfa` is `rawNeedsMfa && !mfaBypass` (AuthContext.jsx:245), so passing an emailed code (`satisfyMfa()`) flips this prop to false as a side effect.

**Second factor.** `AuthContext.checkMfa` (AuthContext.jsx:82-108) calls `mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel()` on every session sync and sets `needsMfa = nextLevel==='aal2' && nextLevel!==currentLevel`; any error is swallowed and treated as "no MFA needed" (fails open). `VerifyMethodStep` computes `emailCannotSatisfy = requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn({isPrivileged:isAdmin, totpEnrolled})` (VerifyMethodStep.jsx:52-55) and, only before the reader has chosen, forces the TOTP branch — because an emailed code cannot raise Supabase AAL and would strand an admin. The email branch shows the OTP boxes *before* awaiting `signInWithOtp` (the SMTP round trip is ~10 s) and rolls back to the picker only if the first send fails (:64-86). TOTP verification goes through `MfaChallengeStep`, which resolves the verified factor id on mount and calls `mfa.challengeAndVerify` (MfaChallengeStep.jsx:30-60). Both paths call `satisfyMfa()` then `onDone()`. `mfaBypass` lives only in memory (AuthContext.jsx:36-37), so a reload re-raises the challenge.

**Privileged-MFA recovery.** Any API 403 whose `detail` contains `multi-factor authentication is required` fires `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` from the Axios response interceptor (api.js:154, privilegedMfa.js:19-23). `AuthContext` subscribes once and increments `privilegedMfaRefusals` (AuthContext.jsx:233-236) — deliberately *not* clearing `mfaBypass`, because that would re-raise `needsMfa`, bounce `ProtectedRoute` to /login, which offers the emailed code again and loops. `PrivilegedMfaGate` is mounted once in `AppShell` for any signed-in user (AppShell.jsx:317), returns `null` until a refusal exists (PrivilegedMfaGate.jsx:32), then reads `useMfaStatus()` and shows either the headerless `MfaChallengeStep` (factor exists) or an enroll CTA opening `MfaEnrollDialog`. On success `resolved()` calls `handleChanged()` (refetch factors + `refreshMfa`) and a bare `queryClient.invalidateQueries()` so every query the refused session failed re-runs (:50-56). Dismissal records `dismissedAt = privilegedMfaRefusals`, so the next refusal re-opens it (`shouldPromptForPrivilegedMfa`, privilegedMfa.js:66-71). Only reaching `currentLevel==='aal2'` zeroes the counter (AuthContext.jsx:101).

**Admin gate.** `/admin` is guarded by `ProtectedRoute roles={['admin','superadmin']}` (App.jsx:63). Inside, `Admin` runs its own `['admin-mfa-factors']` query (`listFactors` + `getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel` in parallel, Admin.jsx:108-121, `enabled: isAdmin && !isE2ETestMode`) and maps it through `adminSecurityState` (:51-58) to loading → `<PageSkeleton variant="admin"/>`, error → retry card, setup (no verified factor) → "Open security settings" → `navigate('/settings?section=security')`, challenge (factor but aal1) → a button that calls `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` to raise `PrivilegedMfaGate` in place rather than navigating to /login (:76-88; the reason is pinned by a source-reading test). Only when the state is `'ready'` does the tab strip render; `tabs` gains `operations` for superadmins (:133-135) and the chosen component mounts under a `Suspense` whose fallback mirrors the overview layout (:171).

**Admin tab data.** *Overview* issues three unkeyed-by-department queries — `['analytics-overview']`, `['analytics-activity']` (limit 20), `['users']` — and shows one shared error banner with a `retryFailed` that refetches only the failed ones (AdminOverview.jsx:88-113). `per_track`/`per_year` are `Object.entries`'d into Recharts data (:115-116) and handed to the lazily imported `OverviewCharts`. The role Select is rendered only for other users and only when the viewer is superadmin or the target is not admin/superadmin (:361); `changeRole` posts `{role, status: selectedUser.status}` because `RoleUpdate.status` defaults to `'approved'` server-side (analytics.py:350) and sending the role alone silently approved pending accounts. The Objective 2 card renders `src/data/objective2Summary.json` — run metadata, one table row per stratum with a gold "the figure the paper quotes" badge on `quoted`, then a "How to read this" caveat and the retrieval diagnostics. *Upload history* fetches `listPapers(role==='admin' ? userDept : null)` keyed `['papers', role, userDept]`, derives the year list and the department-scoped track vocabulary via `resolveArchiveTracks` (UploadHistoryTab.jsx:62-65), and filters client-side across title/authors/track/year/department/thesis_category. *System management* holds four queries (`['users']`, `['departments']`, `['system-logs']` limit 200, `['papers','all']`), five filter states with page resets, an `editingUser` row-editor, and a single `confirmation` object driving one shared `ConfirmDialog` (`{title,message,confirmLabel,danger,run}`, :477-507). `handleUpdate` sends exactly `{full_name: displayNameFor(data), role, department, status}` (:431-436) because `UserUpdate.full_name` is required and rejects blanks. `updateUserStatus` branches: superadmin → `updateUserDetails`, admin → `updateUserRole` (:455-475). The superadmin-only block renders `FeaturePermissionsManagement` and `DepartmentsManagement` (:703-708). *Operations* runs five queries on `refetchInterval` 30 s / 30 s / 15 s / 30 s and no interval for retention (OperationsTab.jsx:53-57); the first four are gated as one (any `isLoading` → skeletons, first `isError` → a single retry card), while retention renders its own three-state block so a missing report cannot blank the tab and so counts are never shown as measured zeros (:147-165).

**Role-feature editor.** `FeaturePermissionsManagement` (SystemManagementTab.jsx:38-130) reads `GET /settings/features`, renders a 2×4 toggle grid for `student`/`faculty` × `chat|archive|novelty|upload`, and on toggle writes the whole object optimistically into the query cache, PUTs it, then calls `broadcastFeatureUpdate()` — a Supabase Realtime broadcast on `global_feature_updates` that every other signed-in client's `AuthContext` listens for and answers with `loadFeatures()` (AuthContext.jsx:202-220). The server validates that the payload has exactly those two roles and four boolean features (routers/settings.py:26-36) and invalidates its own role-feature cache. `canUseFeature` short-circuits to `true` for admin/superadmin and falls back to `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` when `features` is null (permissions.js:24-28).

**Settings.** The active section is derived from `?section=` and validated against the registry, defaulting to `profile`; selecting writes it back with `{replace:true}` (Settings.jsx:32-37), and an `AnimatePresence mode=\"wait\"` keyed on the section cross-fades panels. `ProfileSection` uploads `${user.id}/avatar-${Date.now()}.${ext}` to the `avatars` bucket, stores only the bucket path via `PUT /analytics/me`, removes the previous owned path, and on failure after upload but before the profile write deletes the orphan (ProfileSection.jsx:36-70). `SecuritySection` composes `TwoFactorSettings`, an email-change card (`updateUser({email})` → `verifyOtp type:'email_change'` → `reloadSession` + `refreshProfile`), a password card (`resetPasswordForEmail` behind a Turnstile gate → `verifyOtp type:'recovery'` → `updateUser({password})`), and a sessions card calling `signOut({scope:'others'})`.

**Landing.** `Landing` composes the sections; `Hero` and `Login` share `useSceneCapability` (768 px vs 1024 px thresholds) plus `useIdleReady` to decide whether the Three.js chunk loads at all, with page visibility pausing the loop rather than unmounting the GL context (Hero.jsx:36-74). `HowItWorks` picks a 340vh pinned scroll-scrub when `(min-width:1024px) and (min-height:640px)` and motion is not reduced, otherwise a stacked grid. `AskDemo` is a five-phase scripted transcript with a reduced-motion jump to the final frame. `StatsStrip` and `TracksMarquee` are the only landing sections that touch the API.

Tab registry `BASE_TABS` (:22) + conditional `{id:'operations'}` (:134).
