### Invariants

- Login never sets a step from an effect except for the Supabase `PASSWORD_RECOVERY` event (Login.jsx:28-33, :120-125); all other transitions are derived at render (:127-133). Breaking this reintroduces the sign-in/redirect loops the comment block documents.
- Step set and depths are fixed: signin 0, signup 0, otp 1, verifyEmail 1, verify 1, forgot 1, reset 1, success 2 (Login.jsx:41). A new step needs a depth entry or its slide direction silently becomes 0.
- `mfaBypass` must never be persisted (AuthContext.jsx:36-37): an emailed code satisfies this app, not Supabase, so a fresh load of an aal1 session must re-raise the challenge.
- Only `assurance.currentLevel === 'aal2'` clears `privilegedMfaRefusals` (AuthContext.jsx:101). A refusal handler must not clear `mfaBypass` (:226-236) or ProtectedRoute bounces to /login and loops.
- The refusal marker is the lowercase substring `multi-factor authentication is required` matched against a 403 `detail` (privilegedMfa.js:16-23). It mirrors `PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL` in rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:224 and is pinned on both sides (src/lib/privilegedMfa.test.js:15; backend test_auth_authorization.py). Rewording either side breaks the in-place recovery.
- `requiresAuthenticatorToSignIn` must require BOTH halves (privilegedMfa.js:85-89): forcing TOTP on a privileged account with no factor locks it out of its own sign-in; offering the emailed code to a privileged account that has a factor mints an aal1 session that cannot use the app.
- Admin.jsx's security-gate must keep the literal shape `const content = { … }[state]` with a `challenge: { … },` entry that calls `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` and never `navigate('/login…')` — src/lib/privilegedMfa.test.js:137-162 reads the file with regexes, so reformatting that block fails the unit suite.
- `OverviewCharts` must stay behind `lazy()` and AdminOverview must not statically import any binding from it (OverviewCharts.jsx:24-26); `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs:40-49` enforces `MUST_STAY_LAZY = ['useSceneRuntime','OverviewCharts']` and a 115 kB gzipped cap. The parent duplicates the panel height as `CHART_PANEL_HEIGHT` (AdminOverview.jsx:37) instead of importing it for exactly this reason.
- `src/data/objective2Summary.json` is generated, never hand-edited: it must byte-equal `export_objective2_summary.build()`, contain LF only with a trailing newline, carry exactly one `quoted: true` stratum, and keep `present` at n=16 / delta 0.0601 / p 0.1257 / not significant with a CI spanning zero (tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py:45-118).
- Feature payloads must be exactly `{student, faculty}` × `{chat, archive, novelty, upload}` with boolean values, or `PUT /settings/features` 422s (routers/settings.py:26-36). `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` in src/lib/permissions.js:10-13 must stay identical to `DEFAULT_FEATURES` in routers/settings.py:18-21.
- admin and superadmin bypass the feature matrix entirely (permissions.js:25); a null `features` means the fetch has not landed and the documented defaults apply, while `{}` is a real policy granting nothing (permissions.js comment 15-23).
- `forgetPreviousAccount` must run before any component can observe the new identity and must use `queryClient.clear()`, not per-key removal, so in-flight requests are cancelled too (AuthContext.jsx:52-80, called first in `syncSession` :143). `signOut` clears twice, before and after the network round trip (:268-290).
- `INITIAL_SESSION` from `onAuthStateChange` must be ignored because `getSession()` already synced it (AuthContext.jsx:187-192), otherwise every page load doubles the profile read and feature fetch.
- Permissions must resolve before `loading` clears — `fetchProfile` and `loadFeatures` are awaited together inside `syncSession` (AuthContext.jsx:146-158) or ProtectedRoute bounces students off gated routes on hard refresh.
- Every admin table renders three distinct async states through `TableStateRow`, and error outranks empty (TableStateRow.jsx:37-73). `SystemLogsPanel` spells them out by hand because it is not a table (SystemManagementTab.jsx:132-161).
- A user mutation sends exactly the four fields `UserUpdate` declares and never a blank `full_name` (SystemManagementTab.jsx:428-436; models.py:265-269).
- A role change from the overview panel must carry the target's current status (AdminOverview.jsx:120-125); omitting it re-approves pending accounts server-side.
- Badge tones go through `badgeToneClass`, which resolves the `success|warning|critical` aliases and degrades anything unknown to `neutral` rather than returning undefined (design/badgeTones.js:26-42). That is what makes SystemManagementTab.jsx:629's semantic tones render.
- Turnstile gating fails open: once the status is `error|unsupported|unavailable`, submission is no longer blocked (useSecurityGate.js:29-46), and `pending` has a hard 12 s deadline (SecurityCheck.jsx:18, :156-160). Supabase remains the authority on whether a token is required.
- E2E mode (`import.meta.env.MODE === 'e2e'`) short-circuits the admin 2FA gate to `'ready'` (Admin.jsx:52), the AuthContext session machinery (AuthContext.jsx:24-28, :179), and the Axios auth header (api.js:74-82); the app must still be correct without those branches.
- `LOGIN_NEXT_PARAM` is `'next'` and is the single spelling shared by Login, the 401 interceptor and the idle guard (lib/idleSession.js:34-55; api.js:144-146).

### Data model

- `useAuth()` value — {user (Supabase user), profile {role, full_name, email, department, status, avatar_url}, role, department (defaults 'CCSICT'), status ('unavailable' when signed in with no profile), loading, needsMfa (= rawNeedsMfa && !mfaBypass), profileError, isPending, isRejected, privilegedMfaRequired, privilegedMfaRefusals, features, canChat/canArchive/canScan/canUpload, isAdmin/isSuperadmin/isFaculty/isStudent, displayName, avatarUrl} (AuthContext.jsx:238-294).
- AuthContext local state: user, profile, loading, features, profileError, needsMfa, mfaBypass, privilegedMfaRefusals, plus `identityRef` (undefined = unresolved, null = signed out) and `broadcastChannelRef`.
- `/analytics/overview` → {papers:{total, per_track:{}, per_year:{}, per_category:{student,faculty}, total_chunks}, users:{total, per_role:{}}, usage:{chat_queries, chat_sessions, novelty_scans, avg_duplication_percentage, flagged_scans}} (rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:259-292). `per_category` is empty on a pre-migration schema, which is what hides the category strip (AdminOverview.jsx:164).
- `/analytics/activity` row → {id, action ∈ chat_query|paper_upload|paper_delete|novelty_scan|role_change|…, detail (chat_query: {fast_path, sources_cited, duplication_flagged}; uploads: {title|filename}; role change: {target_email, new_role}; else {reason}), created_at} (AdminOverview.jsx:42-67).
- `/analytics/users` row → {id, full_name, email, role, status, department, avatar_url, created_at}.
- `/maintenance/*` shapes — summary {healthy_workers, queued_jobs, pending_cleanups, failed_jobs}; workers[] {worker_id, state ('degraded' → flame), scanner_status, last_seen_at}; jobs[] {id, department, status, attempt_count, max_attempts, updated_at}; alerts[] {id, alert_type, severity, status ∈ open|acknowledged|resolved, last_seen_at, occurrence_count}; retention {upload_job_events, resolved_operational_alerts, security_audit_events}. `requireOperationsObject` throws a TypeError on a non-object or a missing array field (api.js:385-393).
- `/catalog/departments/legacy` row → {id, code, name, title, active, programs[{id, code, name, specializations[{id, code, name}]}], track_label ('Program / specialization'), tracks[] derived} (routers/catalog.py:104-115).
- Role-feature policy → {student:{chat,archive,novelty,upload}, faculty:{…}} booleans, stored in `system_settings` under key `role_features`; client mirror `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` (permissions.js:10-13).
- `src/data/objective2Summary.json` → {_generated_by, _do_not_edit, run:{id, date, generated_at, git_commit, prompt_version, formal_result, queries_scored, queries_total, department, framework, judge_model, answer_model, metric, evidence_path}, quoted_stratum, strata[{key, label, baseline, rag, n, delta, p_value, significant, cohens_d_z, ci_95[lo,hi], quoted}], diagnostics:{faithfulness:{mean,n}, context_precision:{mean,n}}}.
- Login component state: step, email, awaitingVerify, nav {step, dir}; plus motion springs mx/my.
- MfaEnrollDialog state machine: view ∈ loading|error|status|setup; enroll {id, qr, secret}; factor (first verified TOTP).
- PrivilegedMfaGate state: enrolling (bool), dismissedAt (refusal count at last dismissal).
- SystemManagementTab state: editingUser (a whole user row being edited), confirmation {title, message, confirmLabel, danger, run}, five filters + three page numbers; DepartmentsManagement form {name, track_label, tracks (comma-joined string)} with editingId ∈ null | 'new' | uuid.
- Browser storage: localStorage `isu-thesis-chat-prefs-v1` {defaultCategory, sendKey ∈ enter|ctrlEnter} (lib/chatPrefs.js), `isu-thesis-preferences-v2` (theme/palette/motion/effects/contrast), `isu-idle-last-activity`, `isu-session-started-at`, `isu_e2e_auth_fixture`, `isu_e2e_turnstile`, `isu_e2e_skip_animations`; sessionStorage `iskai_guest_rate_id` (api.js:26).

### Config knobs

- `import.meta.env.MODE === 'e2e'` — switches AuthContext onto localStorage fixtures, bypasses the admin 2FA gate, fixes the Authorization header, and points VITE_API_URL at `/__e2e_api` (e2eSession.js:3; Admin.jsx:52; AuthContext.jsx:24-28, :162-173, :179, :203, :269; api.js:74-82; vite.config.js:84-88).
- `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` — when empty, `turnstileEnabled` is false, `SecurityCheck` renders null and `useSecurityGate.blocked` is always false. Production builds fall back to the root `.env` value; e2e builds force it empty (turnstileConfig.js:9-11; vite.config.js:75-83).
- `VITE_API_URL` — empty in dev so the Vite proxy handles the backend prefixes (/upload /chat /papers /health /sessions /duplication /analytics /catalog /maintenance /departments /settings, vite.config.js:93-105). A new backend router prefix needs an entry here.
- `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` / `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` — the frontend's only Supabase credentials (anon key only).
- Backend `REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA` (config.py:113, default False; mandatory under APP_ENVIRONMENT=production, :248) — controls whether the API actually 403s aal1 admins, i.e. whether `PrivilegedMfaGate` ever fires. The client-side admin gate ignores it.
- Backend `THESIS_EVALUATION_DEPARTMENT` via `GET /settings/public` — the guest/evaluation department scope.
- `system_settings.role_features` row — the live role-feature matrix; absent means `DEFAULT_FEATURES` applies (routers/settings.py:57-60).
- localStorage `isu_e2e_turnstile='1'` — opts an e2e run into the Turnstile gate with a fake key (turnstileConfig.js:4-5).
- localStorage `isu_e2e_skip_animations='1'` — jumps Framer animations to their final frame for the axe matrix only (main.jsx:26-27).
- `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` — EAGER_BUDGET_KB, `LAZY_CHUNK_BUDGETS_KB` {useSceneRuntime:250, OverviewCharts:115}, `MUST_STAY_LAZY`.
- Preferences (localStorage `isu-thesis-preferences-v2`): theme, palette, motion (system|full|reduced), effects, contrast — `reducedMotion` switches HowItWorks to the stacked pipeline and AskDemo to its final frame.

### Error handling

- Axios response interceptor: GET-only retry (×2, 350 ms × attempt) on 502/504; one silent `refreshSession` replay on 401, then `signOut()` and a hard `window.location.href = loginPathWithNext(current)`; any 403 matching the privileged-MFA marker calls `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` and still rejects so callers keep their own handling (api.js:111-158).
- React Query defaults: no retry for status < 500 or 503 or 502/504, one retry otherwise; staleTime 30 s, gcTime 30 min, refetchOnWindowFocus off (main.jsx:35-53).
- Table surfaces route all three async states through `TableStateRow`, error first (components/ui/TableStateRow.jsx). `SystemLogsPanel` reimplements it for a non-table pane (SystemManagementTab.jsx:141-161).
- AdminOverview shows a single dismissible-free banner when any of the three queries errors and refetches only the failed ones (`retryFailed`, :109-113) — but still renders zeros for the missing values.
- OperationsTab gates the four core queries as one (skeletons / one retry card, :83-95) and gives retention its own isolated error state so a missing report shows a message instead of three fabricated zeros (:154-163).
- Every mutation is `try/catch` + `toast.error(..., {description: apiErrorMessage(err)})`: role change (AdminOverview.jsx:129), user update/delete/status (SystemManagementTab.jsx:441, :451, :471), department save/delete (:218, :231), feature toggle (:62, which also invalidates to undo the optimistic write), alert acknowledge (OperationsTab.jsx:80), session clear (ChatSection.jsx:57), avatar upload/remove (ProfileSection.jsx:65, :84).
- Avatar upload compensates: if the object was uploaded but the profile write failed, the orphan is deleted (`ProfileSection.jsx:62-64`); a failed cleanup of the previous object degrades to `toast.warning` rather than failing the operation (:56, :79).
- Supabase auth errors are never surfaced raw — `friendlyAuthError` maps captcha/rate-limit/credential/confirmation/TOTP/expiry/session/network cases and falls back to a generic string (authUtils.js:69-91); `{}` gets a specific "check Supabase Auth or Postgres logs" message (:75-77).
- Rate limiting: `isRateLimitError` + `retryAfterSeconds` drive a countdown that disables both submit paths behind `RateLimitAlert`; the timer is per-IP so it deliberately survives edits to the email field (SignInForm.jsx:36-47, :200-224).
- Turnstile: `pending` gets a 12 s deadline that becomes the reported status `unavailable` (SecurityCheck.jsx:156-160); `useSecurityGate` then stops blocking submission (fails open) and Supabase decides (useSecurityGate.js:29-46).
- MFA enroll surfaces the server's reason with plain-language translations for the two known Supabase 422s (TOTP disabled at project level, factor limit reached) — `mfaSetupErrorMessage` (MfaEnrollDialog.jsx:169-178) — and a `console.error` at :243.
- `useMfaStatus` throws on a `listFactors` error rather than reading `.data` as null, so 'unavailable' is distinguishable from '2FA off' (useMfaStatus.js:18-20; rendered at TwoFactorSettings.jsx:39, :47).
- `ProtectedRoute` renders full-page states rather than redirecting for `profileError` (reload), `isPending` (sign out), `isRejected` (sign out) — ProtectedRoute.jsx:29-86.
- Operations API responses are shape-validated client-side; a non-object or missing array throws a TypeError that the query surfaces as an error (api.js:385-393).

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.test.js — refusal recognition (403 + wording, casing, added clause, non-MFA 403s left alone), listener bus semantics, dismissal counting, the two sign-in predicates, and a source-reading assertion that Admin.jsx's `challenge` branch calls `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()` and never navigates to /login (:137-162).
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/authUtils.test.js — email validation and masking, the four password rules, fifteen friendlyAuthError mappings including the `{}` case and both captcha variants, retryAfterSeconds, and authOptions token passing.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/permissions.test.js — DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES mirror and the null-vs-{} fallback contract.
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/badgeTones.test.js — that success/warning/critical resolve and unknown tones degrade to neutral (what makes the user-status column render).
- rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/pagination.test.js, keys.test.js, catalog.test.js, idleSession.test.js, apiErrors.test.js, utils.test.js — the pure helpers these screens lean on.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py (untracked) — pins src/data/objective2Summary.json byte-for-byte to the source run, LF-only, exactly one quoted stratum, pooled 0.2169→0.3024 p=3.222e-05 significant, present n=16 delta 0.0601 p=0.1257 NOT significant with a CI spanning zero, all five strata present including n=4 and n=3.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:651 — superadmin Operations tab: workers, alerts (+acknowledge toast), jobs, retention dry run, with a fail-closed assertion that no unmocked API call was made.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:133 / :143 / :157 / :192 — signed-out redirects, guest appearance dialog, password-reset code after the security check resolves, CAPTCHA misconfiguration copy.
- rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js — axe over admin-overview / admin-upload-history / admin-system-management / admin-operations / authentication / landing × {light,dark} × {standard,high-contrast}, with an animation-settling wait because mid-fade opacity produced false contrast failures.
- rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs — enforces that OverviewCharts stays lazy and under 115 kB gzipped.
- No component-level tests exist for Admin.jsx, the admin tabs, Settings, the auth step components, MfaEnrollDialog, TwoFactorSettings or PrivilegedMfaGate — `npm test` is node:test over colocated pure modules only, so JSX behaviour is covered exclusively by Playwright.

### Gotchas

- **The forgot-password flow is broken for any account with a verified TOTP factor.** Login.jsx:129 excludes only `'reset'` and `'success'` from the `needsMfa` branch, while the sibling branch at :131 correctly also excludes `'forgot'`. So the moment `ForgotPasswordStep.verifyOtp({type:'recovery'})` succeeds (ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:64) a session lands, `needsMfa` becomes true, and Login swaps the whole step to `'verify'` — the new-password form at ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:116-153 never renders. After the TOTP challenge passes, `awaitingVerify` is false, so Login resolves to `'success'` and navigates to the dashboard with the password unchanged.
- **Auto-submit is dead in Settings → Security.** `OtpInput.commit` calls `onChange(clean)` then `onComplete(clean)` in the same tick (ui/OtpInput.jsx:32-36), but `SecuritySection` passes `onComplete={handleVerify}` where `handleVerify()` takes no argument and reads the still-stale `code` state, guarded by `if (code.length !== 6) return` (SecuritySection.jsx:42-43 and :126-127, wired at :66 and :151). Typing the sixth digit does nothing; the user must click Verify. Every other OTP call site takes the token as a parameter (VerifyEmailStep.jsx:21, VerifyMethodStep.jsx:89, MfaChallengeStep.jsx:43, ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:60, MfaEnrollDialog.jsx:250).
- **The departments editor is a silent no-op for two of its three columns.** `getDepartments()` hits `/catalog/departments/legacy` (api.js:422), which returns `_nested_catalog()` — and that function hard-sets `track_label` to the literal `'Program / specialization'` and recomputes `tracks` from the normalized programs/specializations tables (rag-thesis-backend/routers/catalog.py:109-113). `DepartmentsManagement` edits those two fields (SystemManagementTab.jsx:279-283) and PUTs them to `/departments/{id}`, which writes the `departments` columns — columns that response never reads back. The toast says "Department updated", the query invalidates, and the table reverts. Only the `_legacy_catalog()` fallback path (catalog.py:45-64, taken when the normalized catalog read throws) surfaces the edited values.
- **The overview error banner contradicts what the page renders.** AdminOverview.jsx:142 states "No missing values are being treated as measured zeros", but when `/analytics/overview` fails, `overview` is undefined, `isLoading` is false (503 is excluded from React Query retry, main.jsx:46), and the four stat tiles render `?? 0` (:155-158) alongside a novelty panel of `0`, `0%`, `0` (:297-313). Compare `StatsStrip` on the landing page, which renders `'—'` for exactly this case (StatsStrip.jsx:37).
- **Stale comment inside the uncommitted AdminOverview hunk.** The block at AdminOverview.jsx:186-199 ends with "Still deliberately no figures" immediately above a table that prints every figure, while the comment at :260-263 in the same hunk explains that the objection "is met by printing the caveat next to the figures, not by hiding them". One of the two is left over from the previous revision.
- **The admin console enforces 2FA even where the backend does not.** `adminSecurityState` (Admin.jsx:51-58) requires a verified TOTP factor and `currentLevel==='aal2'` for every admin, unconditionally. The API only enforces it when `settings.require_privileged_mfa` is true, which defaults to `False` (rag-thesis-backend/config.py:113) and is only mandatory in production (:248-249). In dev/staging an admin whose API calls all succeed still cannot open /admin.
- **The gate's two recovery branches disagree.** `challenge` raises `PrivilegedMfaGate` in place (Admin.jsx:87) and its comment explains at length why navigating away loops — but `setup` navigates the admin to `/settings?section=security` and tells them to "sign in again" (:71-75), even though the very same modal can enroll a factor in place and unlock the live session (PrivilegedMfaGate.jsx:84-101).
- **The privileged-MFA modal re-opens every 15 s on the Operations tab.** Dismissal sets `dismissedAt` to the current refusal count and any later refusal re-opens (privilegedMfa.js:66-71), while OperationsTab holds five polling queries at 15–30 s intervals (OperationsTab.jsx:53-57). On an aal1 admin session the dismissed dialog reappears on the next poll, forever.
- **Two MFA query keys.** Admin uses `['admin-mfa-factors']` (Admin.jsx:109) while everything else uses `['mfa-factors']` (useMfaStatus.js:13). They only stay consistent because `PrivilegedMfaGate.resolved()` calls a bare `queryClient.invalidateQueries()` with no key (PrivilegedMfaGate.jsx:55).
- **Opposite failure policies on the same signal.** `AuthContext.checkMfa` swallows any `getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel` error and sets `needsMfa=false` — fails open (AuthContext.jsx:91-107). `adminSecurityState` turns the same failure into the blocking `'error'` card — fails closed (Admin.jsx:54).
- **`totpEnrolled` is not an enrollment check.** Login passes `totpEnrolled={needsMfa}` (Login.jsx:184), and `needsMfa` is already `raw && !mfaBypass`. Calling `satisfyMfa()` therefore flips the prop to false mid-step, and an account whose session is already aal2 would be told the authenticator is "Not set up on this account" (VerifyMethodStep.jsx:137-140).
- **`OtpSignInStep` has no way to finish if Supabase sends a code.** The screen says "Click the link in the email" and offers only "Resend link" (OtpSignInStep.jsx:55-59, :100) with no OTP input, while the sign-in button that reaches it is labelled "Email me a login link" (SignInForm.jsx:245) and every other surface in the app promises a 6-digit code (SignUpForm.jsx:293). Whether this path works depends entirely on which Supabase `magiclink` template is configured.
- **Inconsistent captcha coverage.** `VerifyEmailStep.resend` calls `supabase.auth.resend({type:'signup', email})` with no `captchaToken` and renders no `SecurityCheck` (VerifyEmailStep.jsx:38-53) — with CAPTCHA enforced on the project it fails with a message the user cannot act on. `ForgotPasswordStep.updatePassword` also omits the token (:86) while rendering a `SecurityCheck` right next to the button (:139), so that widget is decorative.
- **Two different `redirectTo` values for the same operation.** `ForgotPasswordStep.send` uses `${origin}/login` (:44); `SecuritySection.PasswordCard.handleRequest` uses `window.location.origin` (:111), so that email's link lands on the Landing page where nothing listens for `PASSWORD_RECOVERY` (the listener is in Login.jsx:121-124).
- **PasswordCard verifies a `recovery` OTP against an already-signed-in session** (SecuritySection.jsx:129). That mints a fresh recovery session; for an admin with `require_privileged_mfa` on, the new token is aal1 and every privileged call starts 403ing with no signal until `PrivilegedMfaGate` fires. It also stores the new password in component state before the code is requested (:104-116).
- **Approving a nameless account rewrites its name.** `updateUserStatus` for a superadmin sends `full_name: displayNameFor(selectedUser)` (SystemManagementTab.jsx:457-464), which for a profile with no name is `email.split('@')[0]` (:349). Clicking Approve therefore persists a synthesized name. The admin branch uses `updateUserRole` and does not (:466), so the same button has different side effects depending on who clicks it — and the branch is unnecessary, since both endpoints accept an `AdminUser` and enforce identical department/superadmin rules (rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:411-435).
- **Three of four paginated tables can render an empty page.** OperationsTab uses the clamping `paginateItems` (lib/pagination.js:69-76), but SystemManagementTab's users (:401) and papers (:424) and DepartmentsManagement (:186) use a raw `slice((page-1)*5, page*5)`. Deleting rows or tightening a filter while on a high page leaves the body blank with no rows and no empty-state, because `TableStateRow` only fires when the whole filtered set is empty.
- **Feature toggles never invalidate on success.** `handleToggle` writes the cache optimistically (SystemManagementTab.jsx:53), PUTs, and relies on `broadcastFeatureUpdate()`. The Realtime channel is created without `broadcast: { self: true }` (AuthContext.jsx:204-213), so the acting superadmin's own `AuthContext.features` is never reloaded — only other clients refetch. It happens not to matter only because `canUseFeature` returns true for admin/superadmin unconditionally (permissions.js:25).
- **`handleToggle` round-trips the whole policy object.** If `GET /settings/features` served `DEFAULT_FEATURES` because the row is absent (routers/settings.py:57-60), the PUT inserts it — fine. But any stored row carrying a third role key makes `_validated_features` 422 (settings.py:27-28) and the UI only toasts the message.
- **Admin tab state is not in the URL** (Admin.jsx:107) while Settings' section is (Settings.jsx:32-33). No deep link to a specific admin tab exists; the a11y matrix has to click tabs to reach them (e2e/accessibility.spec.js:379-404). Settings' `setSearchParams(..., {replace:true})` (:36) also drops every other query parameter and writes no history entry, so Back leaves /settings entirely.
- **`ProtectedRoute` drops the return path.** `needsMfa` and signed-out users get `<Navigate to="/login" replace />` (ProtectedRoute.jsx:23, :27) with no `?next=`, unlike the 401 interceptor (api.js:146) and the idle guard, which both use `loginPathWithNext`.
- **Duplicate paper caches for admins.** UploadHistoryTab keys `['papers', role, userDept]` and calls `listPapers(userDept)` (UploadHistoryTab.jsx:39-42); SystemManagementTab keys `['papers','all']` and calls `listPapers(null)` (:413-414). The backend pins the department for non-superadmins anyway (rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:54-55), so an admin fetches and stores the identical rows twice.
- **`normalizeDepartments` runs twice** — inside `getDepartments()` (api.js:423) and again as a `select` default registered for the `['departments']` key (main.jsx:57). Idempotent, but the select silently applies to any future query keyed `['departments', …]`.
- **Unguarded field access in OperationsTab**: `alert.alert_type.replaceAll('_',' ')` (:133) and `job.id.slice(0,8)` (:183) throw on a null field, and the tab has no boundary below the app-level `ErrorBoundary`.
- **Superadmins get no track filter until they pick a department.** UploadHistoryTab renders the track Select only when `deptFilter` is truthy (:126) and seeds `deptFilter` to `''` for anyone who is not an admin (:47).
- **Navigation naming drift.** AppShell labels `/admin` "Operations" (short "Admin", AppShell.jsx:29) while the page heading is "Research Administration" and "Operations" is one superadmin-only tab inside it (Admin.jsx:134, :146).
- **`src/data/` is untracked but not ignored** (`git check-ignore` exits 1). The export script and its pinning test are likewise untracked. If `objective2Summary.json` is not committed alongside the AdminOverview change, the frontend build fails on a missing module — which is exactly what the script's docstring warns about (export_objective2_summary.py:21-22).
- **AdminOverview JSX nesting is malformed by indentation**: the `<div className="space-y-6">` opened at :147 and closed at :380 sits inside the fragment *after* the error banner (:138-146), so the banner is outside the vertical rhythm it visually belongs to. The component also carries `// eslint-disable-next-line complexity` (:82).
- **`MfaEnrollDialog.disable()` only unenrolls the first verified factor** (`data?.totp?.find(...)`, :201, used at :275). A second verified TOTP factor from another device survives "2FA disabled" and keeps `needsMfa` true. `startSetup` does clean up dangling *unverified* factors first (:218-224).
- **The enroll dialog's error view cannot retry** — it offers only Close (MfaEnrollDialog.jsx:306-317) — even though `TwoFactorSettings` deliberately shows "Manage" on `isError` so the reader can (:56-61).
- **`AvatarEditor` accepts more than it allows**: the file input is `accept="image/*"` (ProfileSection.jsx:111) while validation only permits jpeg/png/webp (:13-17, :27), so the picker offers files it will reject.
- **`ProfileSection.fullName` never resyncs.** It is seeded once from `profile?.full_name` (:128); an avatar change calls `refreshProfile()` but the name field keeps its stale initial value.
- **`PrivacySection` navigates with `window.location.href = 'mailto:…'`** (:48) rather than an anchor — no middle-click, no copy-link — and hard-codes `ccsict@isu.edu.ph`.
- **`AboutSection` statically imports `package.json`** for `version` (:2), pulling the manifest into the settings chunk.
- **`ClearConversations` fires N unbounded parallel deletes** via `Promise.allSettled(sessions.map(deleteSession))` (ChatSection.jsx:46) with no concurrency limit.
- **Landing policy inconsistency on unavailable data**: `StatsStrip` refuses to fabricate (renders '—', :37) while `TracksMarquee` silently substitutes a hard-coded five-track list (`FALLBACK_TRACKS`, :6-12) that may not match the live catalog at all.
- **The email-change card assumes single-confirmation.** `EmailCard.handleVerify` verifies one `email_change` OTP against the new address (SecuritySection.jsx:45). With Supabase's "Secure email change" enabled, both the old and the new address must confirm, and this UI has no path for the second code.

### Open questions

- Is the forgot-password/TOTP interaction (Login.jsx:129 omitting `'forgot'`) known and accepted, or has no TOTP-enrolled account ever exercised the in-card reset? Nothing in the tests or comments mentions it.
- Are `src/data/objective2Summary.json`, `scripts/export_objective2_summary.py` and `tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py` intended to land in the same commit as the AdminOverview change? The build breaks without the JSON, and the pinning test skips silently when either file is absent (test:30-33).
- Which Supabase magic-link template is configured? `OtpSignInStep` only works for the link variant, while the rest of the auth copy promises 6-digit codes.
- Is Supabase "Secure email change" (double confirmation) enabled? `EmailCard` only handles a single `email_change` OTP.
- Should the admin console's unconditional 2FA gate track the backend's `REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA`, or is enforcing it client-side even in dev deliberate? Nothing documents the divergence.
- Does the departments editor still have a purpose now that `/catalog/departments/legacy` derives `track_label` and `tracks` from the normalized catalog? If it does, it needs to read the raw `/departments/` list instead.
- Should the overview stat tiles adopt the StatsStrip '—' convention so the error banner's claim about measured zeros becomes true?
- Was the `updateUserStatus` superadmin/admin branch (SystemManagementTab.jsx:458-467) deliberate, given both endpoints accept an admin and only the superadmin path rewrites `full_name`?

**Additional notes returned by the reader** (fields outside the schema, preserved verbatim)

- `controlFlowExtra`: 

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 9 correction(s), 13 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** The forgot-password/TOTP bug ends with: 'After the TOTP challenge passes, awaitingVerify is false, so Login resolves to 'success' and navigates to the dashboard with the password unchanged.'
  **Actual:** The bug is real but the outcome is wrong. After the second factor passes, needsMfa is false and `step` is still 'forgot', so Login.jsx:131's `!['reset','forgot'].includes(step)` is FALSE and the else-if never fires — effectiveStep falls back to `step` = 'forgot'. ForgotPasswordStep remounts with fresh state (sent=false, verified=false, newPassword=''), because AnimatePresence mode="wait" is keyed on effectiveStep and destroyed it at Login.jsx:383-385. The reader is dumped back at the email-entry screen of the reset flow, signed in, password unchanged. No navigation to the dashboard happens (it only happens if they then click Back, which sets step='signin' and lets :131 resolve to 'success'). (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx:129 / :131 / :383-385; src/pages/auth/ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:60-75`)
- **Wrong:** '`totpEnrolled` is not an enrollment check … an account whose session is already aal2 would be told the authenticator is "Not set up on this account" (VerifyMethodStep.jsx:137-140).'
  **Actual:** The coupling is real, but that scenario is unreachable through Login. `effectiveStep==='verify'` requires either needsMfa (Login.jsx:129) or awaitingVerify (:132), and awaitingVerify is armed only by SignInForm.onPasswordSuccess (SignInForm.jsx:103), i.e. by signInWithPassword — which yields aal1 for a factor-holding account and can never yield aal2 for a factor-less one. When VerifyMethodStep does verify TOTP it calls satisfyMfa()+onDone() in one handler (:118-119), so needsMfa and awaitingVerify both clear in the same batch and the component unmounts before it can re-render with totpEnrolled=false. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx:129-132; src/pages/auth/SignInForm.jsx:103; src/pages/auth/VerifyMethodStep.jsx:116-120`)
- **Wrong:** 'Only `assurance.currentLevel === 'aal2'` clears `privilegedMfaRefusals` (AuthContext.jsx:101).'
  **Actual:** Incomplete. The signed-out branch of checkMfa also zeroes it: `if (!currentUser) { setNeedsMfa(false); setMfaBypass(false); setPrivilegedMfaRefusals(0); return false }`. Any syncSession with a null session resets the counter. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:83-88`)
- **Wrong:** 'SystemManagementTab state: … five filters + three page numbers' and 'a single `confirmation` object driving one shared `ConfirmDialog`'.
  **Actual:** The default export holds SIX filter states — deptFilter (:363), roleFilter (:364), statusFilter (:365), searchQuery (:366), paperSearchQuery (:368), paperDeptFilter (:369) — and TWO page numbers (:367, :370); the third page lives inside DepartmentsManagement (:182). It also omits `confirming` (:372). The file contains TWO ConfirmDialogs, not one: the shared one at :772-781 and DepartmentsManagement's own, driven by `deleteTarget`/`deleting` (:183-184, :328-337). (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/SystemManagementTab.jsx:362-372, :182-184, :328-337, :772-781`)
- **Wrong:** 'Three of four paginated tables can render an empty page … Deleting rows or tightening a filter while on a high page leaves the body blank.'
  **Actual:** Two errors. (a) Tightening a filter cannot cause it for users or papers: every filter setter resets the page (setUserPage(1) at :521, :525, :530, :537; setPaperPage(1) at :720, :724). Only a shrinking dataset (a delete + ['users'] invalidation) strands them; DepartmentsManagement has no filters at all, so only a delete can. (b) The count is wrong: OperationsTab holds three clamping surfaces (paginateItems at :63-65), so it is three clamping vs three raw-slice, not one vs three. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/admin/SystemManagementTab.jsx:401, :424, :521-537, :720-724, :186; src/pages/admin/OperationsTab.jsx:63-65`)
- **Wrong:** 'the branch is unnecessary, since both endpoints accept an `AdminUser` and enforce identical department/superadmin rules (analytics.py:411-435)'.
  **Actual:** They are not identical, and the branch is not obviously unnecessary. PUT /analytics/users/{id}/details adds a self-modification refusal (analytics.py:417-418), a cross-department reassignment refusal (:434-435) and a departments-table existence check (:437+) that PUT .../role (:335-352) has none of. More to the point, the admin branch is the only one that does NOT synthesize full_name — it is a mitigation for the very bug the map flags, not redundancy. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:335-360, :410-440`)
- **Wrong:** 'pages/Landing.jsx — Composes nine landing sections; no logic.'
  **Actual:** Ten components: LandingNav, Hero, StatsStrip, HowItWorks, AskDemo, TracksMarquee, BentoFeatures, Audiences, FinalCTA, Footer. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Landing.jsx:20-31`)
- **Wrong:** 'rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_objective2_summary.py … 157 lines'.
  **Actual:** 156 lines. (The JSON at 113 lines / 2595 bytes and the test at 118 lines are exact.) (`rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_objective2_summary.py`)
- **Wrong:** Assorted anchor drift in otherwise-correct findings.
  **Actual:** SecuritySection's argument-less handleVerify + guard is at :41-42 and :125-126 (map says :42-43 and :126-127); AdminOverview's role-change toast.error is at :130 (map says :129); ChatSection's toast.error is at :58 (map says :57); ForgotPasswordStep's new-password form is :117-153 (map says :116-153); PrivilegedMfaGate's enroll branch is :84-103 (map says :84-101). All substantively correct, each off by one or two. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/SecuritySection.jsx:41-42, :125-126`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- The forgot-password flow's terminal transition is undocumented. On success, ForgotPasswordStep.updatePassword calls `onBack()` (ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:91) — not an `onDone` — which Login wires to `setStep('signin')` (Login.jsx:194); because a session now exists, Login.jsx:131 immediately resolves to 'success' and navigates. The map claims to list 'every transition' but never mentions forgot→signin→success. The inline comment at ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:89-90 states this is deliberate.
- The 'otp' step has no forward transition at all. Login.jsx:177 passes only `onBack`; OtpSignInStep exposes no completion path, so leaving that step depends entirely on the magic link landing a session in this same tab via onAuthStateChange. That is the mechanical reason the map's magic-link gotcha bites.
- ForgotPasswordStep has a SECOND decorative SecurityCheck, not one. The map names :139 (next to 'Update password'); there is another at :192 in the OTP phase, and `verifyOtp` at :64 also omits captchaToken. Only the email-entry widget at :235 feeds a call that actually passes `captcha.token` (:43-45).
- MfaEnrollDialog's own state comment is stale dead documentation: `useState('loading') // loading | status | setup` omits the 'error' view the same component sets at :198 and :206 and renders at :306-317.
- SystemManagementTab issues SIX useQuery calls, not four. FeaturePermissionsManagement holds ['features'] (:41) and DepartmentsManagement holds a second ['departments'] (:178) duplicating the parent's (:384-387) — same key so one fetch, but two observers and two normalizeDepartments selects (main.jsx:57).
- UploadHistoryTab has NO pagination at all — it renders every filteredPapers row (:180). It also issues two catalog queries the map never lists: ['tracks'] (:50) and ['departments'] (:51), the latter a third observer on the shared key.
- Unguarded controlled-input null: `setEditingUser(u)` (SystemManagementTab.jsx:678) seeds the editor from the raw profile row and `value={editingUser.full_name}` (:576) renders it — a profile with full_name: null produces a null-valued controlled input (React uncontrolled→controlled warning). It is the same null that displayNameFor (:348-350) exists to paper over on submit.
- DepartmentsManagement has the same unguarded-field-access class the map flags in OperationsTab: `d.tracks.join(', ')` at :190 and `d.tracks.map` at :286 throw if the catalog row omits `tracks`.
- The 85% duplication constant is mirrored in two more UI strings the map missed, beyond HowItWorks: PrivacySection.jsx:19 ('checked against the archive at the 85% similarity threshold') and AboutSection.jsx:39 ('85% threshold'). Both are frozen-contract mirrors of config.py's duplication threshold.
- The map's open question about the departments editor is answerable from the code: `GET /departments/` already exists, is unauthenticated (rate-limited), and returns the raw columns the editor writes — `id,name,code,track_label,tracks,created_at` — via response_model=list[DepartmentOut] (rag-thesis-backend/routers/departments.py:23, :60-74). That is exactly the read that would make the editor non-illusory.
- ProfileSection enforces a 2 MB avatar size cap (:31-34) alongside the MIME allowlist — part of the upload contract the map describes in detail but omits.
- OperationsTab has a manual `refresh()` that invalidates all five query keys (:67-73), wired to both the error card's Retry (:92) and a header Refresh button (:101). It is the only manual all-queries refresh in the admin console and the map's controlFlow omits it.
- Login's guest escape hatch is `navigate('/chat')` at :403 and is rendered only when `showTabs` (signin/signup) — it disappears on every other step, including 'verify', so an admin stranded at the picker has only 'Back to sign in' (which signs out, :185-189).

## 5. `fe-design` - Frontend design system, UI primitives and 3D scenes

Supplies the whole React app with a runtime-generated Material 3 colour system (three seed palettes x light/dark x standard/high-contrast), a Tailwind v4 token layer, ~23 shared UI primitives built on Radix + framer-motion + CVA, and four decorative WebGL scenes behind a capability gate. Two colour systems coexist deliberately: a static ISU brand ramp in `@theme` (`forest-*`/`gold-*`/`flame-*`/`ivory-*`/`canvas-*`) used by legacy pages and badges, and a dynamic semantic layer (`--primary`, `--surface-N`, `--text-*`) written as inline custom properties on `<html>` by `applyMaterialTheme`. Accessibility is enforby construction rather than by review: de-emphasised text is explicit neutral tones instead of `opacity-*`, and badge foregrounds are paired with the composited tint they actually render on and checked numerically in unit tests. The three.js scenes are purely ornamental and must never enter the eager bundle — that is a CI gate, not a convention.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/index.css` (872 lines) - Tailwind v4 entry. `@theme` brand ramp + fonts + radii + motion tokens + 13 keyframes; `:root`/`.dark` fallback semantic tokens; `[data-palette=…]` overrides; `@layer base` reset/focus/scrollbar/reduced-motion; `@layer components` glass surfaces, skip-link, the entire sonner toast restyle, state-layer, gradients, `.prose-chat`; `html[data-effects="low"]` kill-switches; `@layer utilities` bg-noise/mask-fade/bg-grid
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/tokens.js` (47 lines) - Palette-independent tone constants: SURFACE_TONES, AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT, TEXT_TONES, textToneKey(). Deliberately imports nothing so bare Node ESM tests can read it
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/materialTheme.js` (84 lines) - The dynamic colour pipeline. MATERIAL_SEEDS, materialSemanticTokens(), applyMaterialTheme(). Derives 28 custom properties from 3 independent Material schemes (palette seed, gold seed, flame seed) plus the neutral palette
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/contrast.js` (47 lines) - WCAG 2.x maths: rgbFromHex, relativeLuminance, contrastRatio, blend (gamma-space alpha compositing). Test-only consumer in production code paths
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/badgeTones.js` (43 lines) - BADGE_TONE_CLASSES (4 hues), BADGE_TONE_ALIASES (success/warning/critical), DEFAULT_BADGE_TONE, badgeToneClass() with fail-visible fallback
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/motion.js` (10 lines) - motionTokens: duration/easing/spring/stagger. Consumed only by the 6 upload wizard components
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/badgeTones.test.js` (131 lines) - The real accessibility gate: composites each tint over hardcoded extreme surfaces and asserts >= 4.5:1; asserts no admin page passes an unresolvable tone; asserts gold-text clears AA
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/materialTheme.test.js` (69 lines) - Source-regex assertions over materialTheme.js (cannot import the Material package) + numeric bounds checks on TEXT_TONES
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/fontDelivery.test.js` (64 lines) - Cross-checks index.html's external stylesheet origins against the nginx CSP style-src/font-src, and pins default-src/object-src/frame-ancestors/script-src
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx` (106 lines) - Not in the brief but is the driver: owns the 5 preferences, resolves theme/reducedMotion, writes 6 html attributes + calls applyMaterialTheme + persists to localStorage in one effect; PreferenceMotion wraps MotionConfig
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Input.jsx` (484 lines) - Input, Textarea, Select (Radix Select wearing an <option> API), Field. Shared FloatingLabel + FieldErrorHint. The largest primitive
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/PageSkeleton.jsx` (294 lines) - 8 route-shaped skeletons + PageSkeleton(variant) + RouteSkeleton() which picks by first path segment
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Motion.jsx` (249 lines) - PageTransition, Reveal, staggerContainer/staggerItem, AnimatedCounter, TypewriterText, Magnetic, TiltCard, SpotlightCard, MarqueeRow
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Pagination.jsx` (230 lines) - Pagination (travelling layoutId pill, jump form) + PaginationFooter + Google* aliases
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Modal.jsx` (192 lines) - Modal + ConfirmDialog. useDeterministicDismiss replaces Radix's outside-interaction dismissal entirely
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/OtpInput.jsx` (127 lines) - Segmented OTP with paste/autofill/arrow/backspace handling and a shakeNonce error animation
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/AppearanceControls.jsx` (120 lines) - The only editor for the 5 preferences; shared by Settings and AppearanceDialog
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Button.jsx` (58 lines) - CVA: 6 variants x 6 sizes, asChild via Radix Slot, loading spinner + aria-busy
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Toaster.jsx` (60 lines) - sonner wrapper: 6 custom icon badges, theme from preferences, longer duration under reduced motion
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/IngestScene3D.jsx` (766 lines) - 7 stage sub-scenes + HolographicDocument + PHASE_CONFIGS copy table + normalizeStageKey + Css3DFallback + HUD overlay. The only scene with a visual fallback
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/ConstellationOrb.jsx` (282 lines) - 140-node instanced orb: 2 instancedMesh, halo points, one merged LineSegments arc geometry, 5-10 travelling pulse sprites, per-frame palette lerp
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/sceneCapability.js` (111 lines) - Pure capability policy: BLOCK_REASONS, evaluateSceneCapability (5 hard + 2 soft + visibility), readBrowserSignals, detectWebgl
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/sceneCapability.test.js` (214 lines) - 29 cases incl. an exhaustiveness test that every BLOCK_REASON is reachable and that no hard constraint is overridable by effects:'full'
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/useSceneRuntime.js` (91 lines) - Pointer parallax ref, visibility pause, rAF FPS sampler (3 consecutive sub-45fps windows -> degraded), webglcontextlost/restored wiring, renderer disposal
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/useSceneCapability.js` (68 lines) - Signal gatherer around the pure policy: static WebGL/memory/cores read once, live viewport/pointer/visibility/saveData subscriptions
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/constellation.js` (100 lines) - mulberry32, fibonacciSphere, buildArcs (dedup + distance window + merged buffer), makeGlowTexture (canvas radial gradient as fake bloom)
- `rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` (153 lines) - CI gate: 330 kB eager gzip budget parsed from built index.html, named caps useSceneRuntime=250/OverviewCharts=115, 150 kB default, and a MUST_STAY_LAZY absence check
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/utils.js` (294 lines) - cn() (clsx+twMerge) plus the duplication-screening presentation layer: scanMetrics, currentScreening/atUploadScreening, verdictLabel/Tone/Explanation, avatar path extraction
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/hooks/useMediaQuery.js` (24 lines) - useSyncExternalStore media query (re-reads on query change, false during SSR)
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/hooks/useIsDark.js` (19 lines) - MutationObserver on documentElement.classList — the scenes' theme source, independent of React context
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/hooks/useIdleReady.js` (18 lines) - requestIdleCallback latch (400 ms setTimeout fallback) that defers scene mount past first interaction

### Control flow

**Colour pipeline, end to end.** `index.html` ships `<html lang="en" class="dark">` (index.html:2) so the very first paint uses the `.dark` block of the static fallback tokens in `index.css:223-258`. React boots, `PreferencesProvider` (`src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx:34`) reads `localStorage['isu-thesis-preferences-v2']` merged over `DEFAULTS {theme:'system', palette:'isu', motion:'system', effects:'balanced', contrast:'standard'}` and subscribes to `prefers-color-scheme` and `prefers-reduced-motion`. One effect (PreferencesContext.jsx:58-73) then does everything in a single synchronous block: toggles `.dark`, writes `data-theme`, `data-palette`, `data-motion` (`'reduced'|'full'`), `data-effects`, `data-contrast`, sets `style.colorScheme`, calls `applyMaterialTheme(root, {palette, dark, highContrast})`, and persists. `applyMaterialTheme` (materialTheme.js:80) writes all 28 properties as **inline styles on `<html>`**, which is the crux of the whole design: every `:root`, `.dark` and `[data-palette=…]` declaration in `index.css` is an inline-style-loses fallback that only ever paints before that effect runs.

`materialSemanticTokens` (materialTheme.js:25) builds three independent Material schemes per call — `themeFromSourceColor(MATERIAL_SEEDS[palette])` for primary/tertiary/accent/background, `scheme(GOLD_SEED='#FFC72C')` for `--secondary*` and `--ring`, `scheme(FLAME_SEED='#D22630')` for `--destructive*` — plus `generated.palettes.neutral` for the four `--surface-N` and the two de-emphasised text roles. `themeFromSourceColor` returns the legacy `Scheme` contract with no `surfaceContainer*` roles, so surfaces are derived explicitly as `neutral.tone(SURFACE_TONES[dark?'dark':'light'][role])` and every value passes through `color()` (materialTheme.js:18), which throws `TypeError` rather than let an `undefined` role convert to opaque black. `highContrast` short-circuits exactly two derivations: `onSurface` and `outline` become `#FFFFFF`/`#000000`, border/input alpha becomes `FF` instead of `38`/`52`, and `textToneKey` swaps to the `*HighContrast` tone pairs. Note the ring/focus colour is gold-derived in every state and is *not* strengthened by high contrast.

**Token naming.** Three layers. (1) `@theme` brand ramp — `--color-forest-50…950`, `--color-gold-50…900`, `--color-flame-{200,400,500,600,700,800}`, `--color-ivory-*`, `--color-canvas-{950,900,800,700}` — compiled by Tailwind into `bg-forest-600`, `text-flame-800`, etc. (2) Text roles `--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-ink-faint` defined at index.css:54-56 as `var(--text-primary, #102018)` etc., i.e. Tailwind utilities (`text-ink-muted`) that *indirect through the dynamic layer*. This is the bridge, and it is why `text-ink-muted` is the sanctioned replacement for `opacity-70` — tokens.js:29-35 records that the old approach measured 1.04:1 on the upload-history stepper. `--color-gold-text: #7d5205` exists because `gold-500` is only 3.10:1 on light surfaces; gold-400/500 stay legal for fills/borders/icons at the 3:1 non-text threshold. (3) The shadcn-shaped semantic layer consumed as arbitrary values (`bg-[var(--primary)]`, `text-[var(--muted-foreground)]`).

**Dark mode** is class-based via `@custom-variant dark (&:where(.dark, .dark *))` (index.css:3), not `prefers-color-scheme`, so the `dark:` variant and the dynamic tokens flip from the same source. `html.dark[data-contrast="high"]` at index.css:169 carries the extra class specifically to outrank the bare attribute selector above it.

**Accessibility enforcement.** `contrast.js` is imported by tests only; nothing in the running app measures contrast. The guarantees are compile-time-ish: `badgeTones.test.js:39` composites each tint at its real alpha over the lightest and darkest surfaces a badge can sit on and asserts >= 4.5:1, then (`:54`) asserts the known failures `text-flame-600` (measured 4.06:1) and `text-gold-600` are absent and that no `opacity-\\d` appears in a tone class, then (`:70`) asserts every alias resolves to an audited tone and that nine junk values including `null`, `0` and `{}` still return a real class — the defect being a tone that resolved to `undefined` and rendered a colourless badge. `:95` greps `admin/SystemManagementTab.jsx` and `admin/OperationsTab.jsx` for `<Badge tone=…>` literals and fails if any is unresolvable, which is how `success`/`warning`/`critical` became aliases. `materialTheme.test.js` cannot import `@material/material-color-utilities` under bare Node ESM, so it asserts against the **source text** (`/neutral\\.tone\\(tone\\)/`, `/Number\\.isInteger\\(value\\)/`, the exact high-contrast ternaries) and separately bounds-checks TEXT_TONES against AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT; `materialSemanticTokens` is never executed by any test. `fontDelivery.test.js` is the only thing linking `index.html`'s Google Fonts links to the nginx CSP — dev serves no CSP and e2e stubs the font host, so a mismatch would otherwise only appear as a silent system-font fallback in production.

**Motion.** Four independent mechanisms. (a) CSS: `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` and `html[data-motion="reduced"]` both clamp `animation-duration`/`transition-duration` to `0.01ms !important` and `animation-iteration-count: 1` (index.css:348-363). (b) framer-motion: `PreferenceMotion` sets `MotionConfig reducedMotion={reducedMotion ? 'always' : 'user'}`, which suppresses transform and layout animations but **deliberately keeps animating opacity** — main.jsx:16-27 and e2e/accessibility.spec.js:442-448 both document this, and the a11y suite has to opt into `MotionGlobalConfig.skipAnimations` via a localStorage flag and wait until every inline `opacity` stops changing before it scans, because sampling mid-fade once reported 1.24:1. (c) Per-component JS gates reading `usePreferences().reducedMotion`: `PageTransition`/`Reveal` return a plain `div`, `TypewriterText` renders `phrases[0]` statically, `Magnetic`/`TiltCard` no-op their pointer handlers, `GlassCard` drops `whileHover`, `Pagination` drops the `layoutId`, `AnimatedLogo`/`LogoActivityDots` freeze, `Toaster` extends duration 4000 -> 6000 ms. (d) `data-effects="low"` (index.css:787-813), outside any `@layer` so it outranks `@layer components`: strips `backdrop-filter` from all five glass classes, `display:none` on `.effects-decorative`, and `animation: none` on eleven named loops. `@keyframes stage-sheen` is transform-only on purpose (index.css:150-153) precisely so the a11y suite's opacity-settling wait cannot be stalled by it.

**WebGL gate.** `useSceneCapability` (useSceneCapability.js:24) reads `detectWebgl()` + `readBrowserSignals()` once in a `useState` initializer (a repeated WebGL probe would allocate a throwaway GL context per render) and subscribes only to what can change: viewport width and `(pointer: coarse)` via `useMediaQuery`, `visibilitychange`, and the Network Information `change` event for Data Saver. `evaluateSceneCapability` then applies five hard constraints in order — no WebGL, reduced motion, `effects === 'low'`, Data Saver, `deviceMemory < 4`, `hardwareConcurrency < 4` — none overridable; then two soft ones (coarse pointer, narrow viewport) that `effects === 'full'` overrides; then `pageVisible` last, which is a render-loop concern rather than a capability verdict. `isBelow()` requires a finite positive number, so Safari/Firefox reporting nothing never blocks. Hero calls it at 768px and mounts when `allowed || reason === 'page-hidden'` (Hero.jsx:71), keeping the canvas mounted while a separate IntersectionObserver with `rootMargin: '600px'` drives `active` -> `frameloop` — unmounting would drop the GL context. Login calls it at 1024px and lets visibility gate the mount outright. Both then wrap in `useIdleReady`, so the chunk is not even requested until the browser has an idle window.

**Scene runtime.** `useSceneRuntime` keeps a window-level `pointermove` listener writing NDC coords into a ref (canvases are `pointerEvents: 'none'` so CTAs above stay clickable), pauses on `document.hidden`, and runs a rAF sampler that flips `degraded` after three consecutive one-second windows under 45 fps; `degraded` lowers `dpr` from `[1,2]`/`[1,1.75]` to `[1,1.25]` and turns off antialiasing. `onCreated` attaches `webglcontextlost`/`webglcontextrestored` to `gl.domElement`. `IngestScene3D` is the only scene with a visual fallback: `normalizeStageKey` maps a worker stage key *or* a free-text label onto the seven canonical stages by substring, `PHASE_CONFIGS` supplies badge/spec/accent copy for the HUD, seven `Stage*` components each damp their group scale toward `active ? 1 : 0.001` with `THREE.MathUtils.damp(…, 6, dt)` and set `visible = scale > 0.01` so all seven stay mounted and morph rather than remount, and `Css3DFallback` renders a pure-CSS hologram on context loss. `ConstellationOrb` regenerates identical geometry across StrictMode remounts because every generator is seeded (`mulberry32(42)` for nodes, `7` for arcs, `99` for pulses, `1234` for the runtime re-target), writes 140 instance matrices per frame from one scratch `Object3D`, merges all arcs into a single `LineSegments` draw call, and cross-fades the theme by lerping material colours toward the target palette at `1 - exp(-4·dt)` instead of popping. Additive blending is applied only in dark mode — additive washes to white on ivory.

**Why the three.js chunks must stay lazy.** `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` parses the built `index.html` for the entry module plus every `modulepreload` and `stylesheet` — the browser's own definition of "needed before first interaction" — gzips them at level 9, and fails over 330 kB total. It caps the two known-heavy lazy chunks (`useSceneRuntime` 250 kB, `OverviewCharts` 115 kB), caps every other chunk at 150 kB, and — the check that actually matters — fails if either name appears in the eager set, because converting a `lazy(() => import(...))` into a static import passes every size check while adding ~237 kB gzipped of Three.js/R3F/drei to every visitor's critical path with nothing in the build output looking wrong. `vite.config.js:113-125` deliberately declines to `manualChunks` three.js or recharts: rolldown group functions capture dependency subtrees, which drags shared deps (`scheduler`, `clsx`) into the group and makes the entry eagerly preload the whole thing — so `scheduler` is pinned into `vendor` specifically to stop react-reconciler pulling three into the entry, and `resolve.dedupe: ['three']` guards against R3F silently breaking on two copies of three.

### Public surface

- design/tokens.js — SURFACE_TONES {light:{surface:98,low:96,container:94,high:92}, dark:{6,10,12,17}}; AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT {light:44, dark:60}; TEXT_TONES {light:{36,41}, dark:{72,65}, lightHighContrast:{20,28}, darkHighContrast:{92,84}}; textToneKey({dark=false, highContrast=false}) -> 'light'|'dark'|'lightHighContrast'|'darkHighContrast' (tokens.js:44)
- design/materialTheme.js — MATERIAL_SEEDS {isu:'#046A38', emerald:'#006D46', gold:'#735C00'}; materialSemanticTokens({palette='isu', dark=false, highContrast=false}) -> 28-entry {'--name': '#RRGGBB'|'#RRGGBBAA'} map (materialTheme.js:25); applyMaterialTheme(root, options) -> same map, written via root.style.setProperty (materialTheme.js:80)
- design/contrast.js — rgbFromHex(hex) -> [r,g,b] (throws TypeError on anything but 6 hex digits, with or without '#'); relativeLuminance(hex) -> 0..1; contrastRatio(fg,bg) -> 1..21, order-independent; blend(fg, alpha, bg) -> '#rrggbb' composited in gamma sRGB (contrast.js:39)
- design/badgeTones.js — BADGE_TONE_CLASSES {forest,gold,flame,neutral}; BADGE_TONE_ALIASES {success->forest, warning->gold, critical->flame}; DEFAULT_BADGE_TONE='forest'; badgeToneClass(tone=DEFAULT) -> class string, degrading to neutral for anything unknown (badgeTones.js:40)
