### Invariants

- applyMaterialTheme writes inline styles on <html>, so inline always beats stylesheet: any semantic token it emits CANNOT be overridden from index.css. Only tokens it does NOT emit (--scrim, --radius, --shadow-color, --glass-opacity) are stylesheet-controllable.
- materialSemanticTokens must emit every role listed in materialTheme.test.js:11-15 (--background, --foreground, --primary, --primary-container, --secondary, --destructive, --surface-3, --ring, --text-primary/secondary/tertiary) as a single-quoted literal key, or that test fails.
- Surfaces must come from neutral.tone(tone) and never from primary.surfaceContainer* — asserted both ways (materialTheme.test.js:22-23). The Scheme contract genuinely lacks those roles and an undefined ARGB would become opaque black.
- color() rejects non-integers: every Material role read must be a real ARGB or startup throws TypeError rather than paint black.
- TEXT_TONES: light tones <= 44, dark tones >= 60 (AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT). Light secondary must be < light tertiary, dark secondary > dark tertiary, and each high-contrast tone must be further toward its extreme than the standard one (materialTheme.test.js:50-59).
- High contrast must resolve onSurface/outline to opaque #FFFFFF/#000000 and border/input alpha to FF — pinned by exact-source regex, so even reformatting those ternaries breaks the test.
- De-emphasised text uses --color-ink-muted / --color-ink-faint (i.e. --text-secondary/--text-tertiary), never opacity-*. An opacity-blended foreground has an unknowable effective ratio; one measured 1.04:1.
- badgeToneClass must never return undefined. Unknown, null, numeric and object tones all degrade to BADGE_TONE_CLASSES.neutral.
- Badge tone classes must not contain text-flame-600 (4.06:1 measured) or text-gold-600, and must not contain any opacity-* utility.
- Every alias in BADGE_TONE_ALIASES must name a key of BADGE_TONE_CLASSES, and badgeToneClass(alias) must return the audited tone's classes verbatim — that is what extends the measured AA ratio to the semantic name.
- admin/SystemManagementTab.jsx and admin/OperationsTab.jsx must each pass at least one Badge tone the regex can read, and every literal they pass must resolve.
- --color-gold-text must clear 4.5:1 on five named light surfaces. gold-400/500 remain legal for fills/borders/icons only.
- index.html must keep linking https://fonts.googleapis.com, and the nginx CSP must permit it in style-src plus https://fonts.gstatic.com in font-src. default-src and object-src stay ['self']/['none'], frame-ancestors ['none'], script-src exactly ['self','https://challenges.cloudflare.com'].
- evaluateSceneCapability defaults webgl to false — never ship ~237 kB of WebGL on an unverified guess (sceneCapability.test.js:33).
- No hard constraint may become overridable by effects:'full'. Data Saver in particular stays hard: it is the browser relaying an explicit instruction, outranking a decorative preference. Asserted for all five hard signals.
- Unknown capability readings must never block: null/undefined/0/NaN/'lots' for deviceMemory or logicalCores all allow. A reading exactly at the floor (4) allows; one below rejects.
- Every value in BLOCK_REASONS must be reachable by some signal combination (sceneCapability.test.js:141) — adding a reason without a probe fails the suite.
- PAGE_HIDDEN must rank below every real capability failure, so a hidden tab with no WebGL still reports no-webgl.
- Scene canvases stay mounted while hidden or off-screen; only frameloop is set to 'never'. Unmounting forces a GL context loss and re-init cost.
- Scene canvases are pointerEvents:'none'; parallax must read the window-level pointer from useSceneRuntime's ref.
- All scene geometry must be seeded (mulberry32) so StrictMode double-mount regenerates identical geometry.
- useSceneRuntime's static signals (webgl, deviceMemory, logicalCores) are read exactly once per document — repeated WebGL probing allocates throwaway GL contexts.
- useSceneRuntime and OverviewCharts must be absent from the built index.html's eager asset set, and under 250 / 115 kB gzipped respectively; total eager payload <= 330 kB; every other chunk <= 150 kB. Budget changes must land in the same commit as the size change.
- No manualChunks group may be added for three.js or recharts — rolldown group functions capture dependency subtrees and would make the entry eagerly preload them. `scheduler` must stay in the vendor group for the same reason.
- resolve.dedupe must keep 'three': two copies of three in the module graph break R3F silently.
- html[data-effects="low"] and html[data-motion="reduced"] rules live OUTSIDE @layer so they outrank @layer components; moving them inside a layer silently disables them.
- @keyframes stage-sheen must stay transform-only. A looping inline-opacity animation there would stall the accessibility suite's settle wait indefinitely.
- The `dark` variant is class-based (@custom-variant dark (&:where(.dark, .dark *))), not media-based; html.dark[data-contrast="high"] needs the extra class to outrank the bare attribute selector.
- List keys for fixed-length placeholder rows come from slotKeys(); caller-supplied repeatable lists use contentKeys(). Index keys tie state to position (lib/keys.js:1-7).
- scanMetrics derives coverage and concentration from chunk counts, never the stored percentage, whenever both counts are present — a stored value below 1 is indistinguishable from a legacy 0-1 ratio (utils.js:46-51 warns normalizePercent is unsafe for coverage).
- verdictTone bands mirror services/novelty.py::verdict_for_concentration (<25% clear, 25-70% review_suggested, >=70% high_overlap) and return semantic aliases so the audited AA pairings still apply.

### Data model

- Preference state (localStorage key `isu-thesis-preferences-v2`): {theme:'system'|'light'|'dark', palette:'isu'|'emerald'|'gold', motion:'system'|'full'|'reduced', effects:'balanced'|'full'|'low', contrast:'standard'|'high'}. DEFAULTS at PreferencesContext.jsx:7.
- PreferencesContext value: {...preferences, resolvedTheme, isDark, reducedMotion, updatePreference(key,value), resetPreferences()}.
- html attribute contract written by PreferencesContext.jsx:58-73: class='dark'?, data-theme, data-palette, data-motion='reduced'|'full', data-effects, data-contrast, style.colorScheme, plus 28 inline custom properties.
- 28 semantic custom properties emitted by materialSemanticTokens: --background, --foreground, --card, --card-foreground, --popover, --popover-foreground, --primary, --primary-foreground, --primary-container, --primary-container-foreground, --secondary, --secondary-foreground, --secondary-container, --secondary-container-foreground, --tertiary, --tertiary-foreground, --muted, --muted-foreground, --text-primary, --text-secondary, --text-tertiary, --accent, --accent-foreground, --destructive, --destructive-foreground, --border, --input, --ring, --surface-0..3.
- Static-only tokens (declared in index.css, never emitted): --scrim, --shadow-color, --radius, --glass-opacity, and the whole --color-* @theme ramp.
- Capability verdict: {allowed: boolean, reason: null | one of 9 BLOCK_REASONS strings}.
- useSceneRuntime state: {degraded, lost, paused} booleans + pointerRef {x,y} in NDC (-1..1); internal refs retryTimerRef, canvasRef, rendererRef, retriedRef, contextLoss/RestoreHandlerRef.
- IngestScene3D stage keys (7): download, malware_scan, extract, chunk, embed, screen, index. PHASE_CONFIGS[key] = {badge, spec, action, accent}. `action` is defined for all seven and rendered nowhere.
- ConstellationOrb geometry: NODE_COUNT=140, HUB_COUNT=12, RADIUS=2; PALETTES.dark/.light each carry node/hub/arc/halo/core/coreGlow/pulse colours plus five opacity scalars; boostRef is a Float32Array(140) scratch buffer of citation-arrival flash intensities.
- Bundle budget config: EAGER_BUDGET_KB=330, LAZY_CHUNK_BUDGETS_KB {useSceneRuntime:250, OverviewCharts:115}, DEFAULT_CHUNK_BUDGET_KB=150, MUST_STAY_LAZY ['useSceneRuntime','OverviewCharts'].
- scanMetrics return shape consumed by ProgressRing/Badge surfaces: {highest, coverage, concentration, matchedChunks, totalChunks, verdict}.

### Config knobs

- localStorage `isu-thesis-preferences-v2` — the five user preferences; the only runtime switch for the colour, motion, effects and contrast systems.
- localStorage `isu_e2e_skip_animations` — read once in main.jsx:26, gated on isE2ETestMode, sets MotionGlobalConfig.skipAnimations. Opt-in per spec because it also collapses AnimatePresence and breaks the critical-flows dialogs.
- localStorage `isu_e2e_auth_fixture` — deterministic session for the a11y matrix.
- Vite `--mode e2e` — swaps VITE_API_URL to /__e2e_api, stubs Supabase URL/key, installs the 501 fail-closed guard on both dev and preview servers, and suppresses the Turnstile site key.
- scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs constants (EAGER_BUDGET_KB, LAZY_CHUNK_BUDGETS_KB, DEFAULT_CHUNK_BUDGET_KB, MUST_STAY_LAZY) — the CI size contract; must be edited in the same commit as any intended increase.
- vite.config.js build.chunkSizeWarningLimit = 900 and the manualChunks function (framer-motion -> 'motion'; react/react-dom/react-router/scheduler -> 'vendor'; everything else auto-split).
- sceneCapability.js MIN_DEVICE_MEMORY_GB / MIN_LOGICAL_CORES (both 4) and the per-surface wideViewportQuery (Hero 768px, Login 1024px).
- useIdleReady timeout (default 1200 ms, 400 ms cap on the setTimeout fallback).
- useSceneRuntime FPS degrade policy: < 45 fps for 3 consecutive 1-second windows.
- nginx/default.conf.template Content-Security-Policy — pinned by fontDelivery.test.js for style-src/font-src/default-src/object-src/frame-ancestors/script-src.
- eslint.config.js disables react-refresh/only-export-components for context/**, components/ui/Motion.jsx and pages/auth/AuthFx.jsx (they export variants alongside components).

### Error handling

- materialTheme color() throws TypeError('<role> did not resolve to an ARGB color') — a missing Material role fails loudly at theme-apply time instead of painting opaque black. Thrown inside a useEffect, so it surfaces through main.jsx's ErrorBoundary.
- readPreferences() try/catches JSON.parse and falls back to DEFAULTS on corrupt localStorage.
- contrast.js rgbFromHex throws TypeError on anything that is not six hex digits; blend/relativeLuminance/contrastRatio propagate it.
- badgeToneClass degrades to the neutral tone for any unrecognised input — a legible badge is a better failure than an invisible one (badgeTones.js:35-39).
- detectWebgl wraps canvas creation and getContext in try/catch and returns false, so a blocked or throwing canvas reads as no-WebGL rather than crashing the gate.
- evaluateSceneCapability treats every absent/non-numeric signal as unknown and allows; only a finite positive reading below the floor blocks.
- useSceneRuntime intercepts webglcontextlost with preventDefault and flips `lost`; consumers render null (Hero/Auth) or Css3DFallback (IngestScene3D). The unmount cleanup clears the retry timer, removes both context listeners and calls renderer.dispose().
- TableStateRow ranks error above empty deliberately: a failed fetch usually also looks empty, and 'No users found.' for a broken connection is the misreading the component exists to prevent. Supplies a Retry button when onRetry is given.
- FieldErrorHint sets role='alert' only when `error` is truthy (hints are not announced), and the input gets aria-invalid + aria-describedby — but only on the labelled code path.
- Suspense fallbacks: HeroScene/AuthScene/ThesisCards use `fallback={null}` (the CSS Aurora is already painted underneath); the three IngestScene3D call sites use a pulsing 'Initializing 3D Vector Space…' placeholder.
- Scene load failures are not caught locally — there is no error boundary inside any scene wrapper, so a Canvas that cannot obtain a GL context escalates to the app-level ErrorBoundary.
- e2e/accessibility.spec.js asserts zero serious/critical axe findings across 4 theme states x desktop + 1 mobile per surface, writes per-surface JSON shards before asserting so a failing surface still produces evidence, and additionally fails on any unmocked API call.

### Tests that pin it

- src/design/contrast.test.js — pins relativeLuminance(#fff)=1/(#000)=0, contrastRatio 21:1 both directions and order-independence, hex parsing with and without '#', rejection of 3-digit hex and named colours, and blend() at alpha 0/0.5/1.
- src/design/materialTheme.test.js — regex-pins the presence of 11 semantic role keys, `neutral.tone(tone)`, `Number.isInteger(value)`, the absence of `primary.surfaceContainer`, and the exact high-contrast ternaries; bounds-checks all 8 TEXT_TONES values against AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT; asserts secondary-stronger-than-tertiary in both themes and that high contrast strengthens both; asserts all 4 appearance states resolve and textToneKey() defaults to 'light'. Does not execute the generator.
- src/design/badgeTones.test.js — the numeric AA gate: 6 tone/tint/alpha/foreground combinations x 2 extreme surfaces each, all >= 4.5:1, with the failing ratio printed; absence of text-flame-600/text-gold-600/opacity-*; alias->audited-tone identity; fail-visible resolution for 9 junk tones; a source grep over two admin pages for unresolvable Badge tones; gold-text >= 4.5:1 on 5 light surfaces.
- src/design/fontDelivery.test.js — index.html's external stylesheet origins must all appear in the nginx CSP style-src, fonts.gstatic.com must be in font-src, and default-src/object-src/frame-ancestors/script-src must keep their exact restrictive values with no unsafe-eval leaking into style/font/img/connect-src.
- src/components/three/sceneCapability.test.js — 29 cases: capable desktop allowed; bare call blocks on NO_WEBGL; each of 5 hard constraints blocks and still blocks under effects:'full'; effects:'low' blocks; both soft preferences block by default and are overridden by effects:'full'; unknown/zero/non-numeric memory and core readings allow; exact-floor allows and floor-minus-one rejects; PAGE_HIDDEN is reported separately and ranks below NO_WEBGL; an exhaustiveness test that the 9 probes cover exactly the 9 declared BLOCK_REASONS; readBrowserSignals across vendor-prefixed connection objects, absent APIs, explicit null navigator (chosen so Node's own hardwareConcurrency cannot leak in) and a string deviceMemory; detectWebgl for webgl2 (asserting the exact {antialias:false, powerPreference:'default'} options), webgl1 fallback, no context, and a throwing createElement.
- scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs (npm run bundle:budget, CI gate) — eager payload <= 330 kB gzipped, per-chunk caps, and the MUST_STAY_LAZY absence check; also fails if it parses zero eager assets, because a silently-matching-nothing parser would report 0 kB and pass everything.
- e2e/accessibility.spec.js — axe-core 4.12.1 across 4 theme/contrast states at 1280px plus dark-standard at 360px per surface, run with motion=reduced and effects=low (WebGL scenes deterministically off), waiting until every inline opacity settles; zero serious/critical tolerated; emits evaluation evidence JSON.
- e2e/visual-quality.spec.js — emulates light + reduced motion for visual checks.
- No test covers: materialSemanticTokens' actual output, the neutral badge tone's contrast, the --muted-foreground placeholder colour, any UI primitive's rendered behaviour (the unit runner cannot import JSX — badgeTones.js exists as a separate module for exactly this reason, badgeTones.js:1-8), or the correspondence between MUST_STAY_LAZY's names and real chunk names.

### Gotchas

- **Dead CSS: the whole [data-palette] override set.** index.css:260-292 defines --primary/--primary-container/--ring/--secondary for emerald and gold in both themes, but PreferencesContext sets `root.dataset.palette` and calls applyMaterialTheme in the *same synchronous effect*, and the inline properties beat the attribute selector. These 33 lines never take effect. Consequence: `.dark[data-palette="gold"]`'s attempt to make --secondary green under the gold palette is lost, so selecting the gold palette yields a gold --primary (seed #735C00) AND a gold --secondary (GOLD_SEED #FFC72C) — primary and secondary collapse into one hue family. materialSemanticTokens derives --secondary* from GOLD_SEED unconditionally, regardless of palette.
- **Dead CSS: high-contrast text overrides.** index.css:160-173 sets --text-primary/secondary/tertiary for `html[data-contrast="high"]`, but applyMaterialTheme writes those same three properties inline. High contrast does work — via TEXT_TONES.lightHighContrast/darkHighContrast inside materialTheme — but through a completely different code path than the CSS block suggests.
- **Dead CSS: duplicate focus rule.** index.css:175-178 gives `html[data-contrast="high"] :where(button,a,input,select,textarea):focus-visible` `outline: 3px solid var(--ring); outline-offset: 3px` — byte-identical to the base `:focus-visible` rule at index.css:322-325, which already applies to everything. High contrast does not strengthen the focus indicator at all, and --ring stays gold-derived in every state.
- **Dead tokens.** --glass-opacity (declared only under data-contrast=high) and --radius (declared on :root) have zero consumers anywhere in src. --tertiary, --tertiary-foreground, --secondary and --secondary-foreground are computed and written by materialSemanticTokens on every preference change but consumed by nothing (only the *-container variants are used, by Button's `gold` variant).
- **materialSemanticTokens is never executed by any test.** materialTheme.test.js asserts against the file's source text with regexes because the Material package's entry point is an extensionless specifier bare Node ESM cannot resolve. A refactor that keeps the regex-matching substrings but breaks the derivation passes. Likewise AA_TEXT_TONE_LIMIT is a hand-derived constant (the reproduction command is in a tokens.js comment, tokens.js:19-22) — no CI step actually sweeps the tones.
- **badgeTones.test.js measures hardcoded surfaces.** LIGHT_SURFACES ['#fbfdf7','#e7e9e4'] and DARK_SURFACES ['#111411','#282b28'] (badgeTones.test.js:26-27) are literals meant to represent the Material-generated neutral surfaces, but they are not derived from SURFACE_TONES or the seeds. Change MATERIAL_SEEDS and the AA test keeps passing while measuring surfaces that no longer exist.
- **The `neutral` badge tone is unaudited.** The TONES table (badgeTones.test.js:30-37) covers forest/gold/flame only. `neutral` — which is both the unknown-tone fallback and what verdictTone returns for a clear screening — has no measured ratio. Its foreground is `text-ink-muted` (--text-secondary), AA against plain surfaces but not against the `bg-forest-900/8` / `dark:bg-white/8` tint composited over them.
- **Two parallel colour systems, and badges are on the static one.** BADGE_TONE_CLASSES uses `forest-*`/`gold-*`/`flame-*` from the @theme ramp, so badges do not respond to the palette selector at all. New primitives use the dynamic layer; Pagination, PageSkeleton, Spinner, OtpInput, `.glass`, the scrollbar and the whole toast restyle also use the static ramp. `.glass`/.glass-strong/.glass-subtle (hardcoded white/forest) and `.surface`/`.surface-tonal`/`.surface-glass` (token-driven) are two competing glass vocabularies used side by side — PageSkeleton uses `.glass`, GlassCard uses `.surface-glass`.
- **GlassCard's `strong` prop does not use `.glass-strong`.** GlassCard.jsx:19 resolves `strong ? 'surface-glass shadow-2xl' : 'surface-glass'` — only the shadow changes; the surface is identical.
- **Dark-mode FOUC for light-mode users.** index.html:2 hardcodes `class="dark"` and there is no pre-hydration inline script reading localStorage, so a light-theme user paints the dark fallback palette until PreferencesProvider's first effect runs.
- **Low-effects mode makes glass *more* likely to fail contrast, and that is the configuration axe measures.** index.css:787-795 removes backdrop-filter and forces `background: var(--card) !important` — but --card is `${surfaces.low}E6`, i.e. still ~90% translucent. So `.surface-glass` under effects=low is semi-transparent with no blur. e2e/accessibility.spec.js:52-58 runs the entire matrix with `effects: 'low'`.
- **The effects=low animation kill-list is incomplete.** index.css:801-811 names eleven animations but omits `animate-shimmer` (every Skeleton keeps shimmering), `animate-caret`, `animate-sweep`, and all Tailwind built-ins — `animate-spin` (9 uses), `animate-pulse` (8), `animate-ping` (4), `animate-bounce` (1). `animate-float-slow` is in the kill-list but has zero call sites; `animate-float` has one.
- **`motion-reduce:` is not the app's motion preference.** Pagination.jsx:87,119,147 use Tailwind's `motion-reduce:active:scale-100`, which compiles to `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)`. A user who picks "Reduced motion" in AppearanceControls while their OS reports full motion still gets `active:scale-90`.
- **Non-labelled Input/Textarea silently swallow error and hint.** Input.jsx:124-143 and 210-229 render a bare element: no FieldErrorHint, no aria-describedby, and `containerClassName` is destructured and discarded. `<Input error="Required" />` without a label shows a red border and no message at all — including to screen readers.
- **Field's label is not programmatically associated with its child.** In the non-cloneElement branch (Input.jsx:458-482) `labelId` is put on the label span but nothing ever references it via aria-labelledby, and there is no htmlFor (the child is arbitrary markup). `descriptionId` is likewise only on the hint, never referenced. Field also only forwards props when `typeof children.type` is 'function' or 'object', so a Field wrapping a raw DOM element gets the decorative container and no wiring.
- **Select silently drops non-`<option>` children.** Input.jsx:334-336 filters `Children.toArray(children)` for `child.type === 'option'`, so `<optgroup>`, fragments, mapped-then-nested nodes and conditionals returning arrays disappear with no warning. A child with `value=''` is consumed as the placeholder and removed from the list. `onChange` receives a synthesized `{target:{name,value}, currentTarget:{name,value}}` — no real event, no `preventDefault`.
- **Hardcoded DOM id in Pagination's jump form.** `id="jump-to-page-input"` and `htmlFor` (Pagination.jsx:159-163) are literals, even though the same component deliberately uses `useId()` for the layout indicator because "shared-layout ids are global". Two paginations showing the jump control on one screen produce duplicate ids and a label pointing at the wrong input. The ellipsis key `ellipsis-${index < 3 ? 'start' : 'end'}` is also a positional heuristic rather than derived from the page list.
- **Pagination fights the browser's scroll anchoring with a double scrollTo.** changePage (Pagination.jsx:43-61) captures scrollY/scrollX and restores it in both a requestAnimationFrame and a setTimeout(0), plus `[overflow-anchor:none]` on two elements. Two competing scroll restorations per page change.
- **Pagination and PaginationFooter hide on different conditions.** Pagination hides when `totalPages <= 1`; PaginationFooter hides when `total <= limit`. Different predicates for "not worth showing".
- **useSceneRuntime's context-loss retry is unreachable dead code.** onContextLost sets `lost = true` and arms a 1500 ms timer to set `degraded`. But every consumer gates on `lost` first (HeroScene.jsx:32 and AuthScene.jsx:21 `return null`; IngestScene3D.jsx:718 returns the CSS fallback), which unmounts the Canvas. A detached canvas whose context was disposed never fires `webglcontextrestored`, so `lost` latches forever, `onContextRestored` never runs, `retriedRef`/`gl.dispose()` is never reached, and the `degraded` the timer sets has no effect. The 'one automatic remount then permanent fallback' described in AuthScene.jsx:15 does not happen — it is permanent fallback on the first loss.
- **`setDegraded` is exported from useSceneRuntime and consumed by nobody.** Dead return value across all four scenes.
- **IngestScene3D bypasses the capability gate entirely.** Upload.jsx:73, UploadBatch.jsx:665 and PipelineTimeline.jsx:104 mount it behind `lazy()` + Suspense with no useSceneCapability check. A user with reducedMotion, effects='low', Data Saver, 2 GB RAM or a coarse pointer still downloads and runs the 250 kB three.js chunk on the upload page — exactly the policy sceneCapability.js:1-30 exists to state. Worse, with no WebGL at all there is no graceful path: Css3DFallback only renders on the `lost` state, and a Canvas that cannot create a context throws into the nearest ErrorBoundary rather than degrading.
- **PipelineTimeline's 3D branch is unreachable.** Its only call site (Upload.jsx:106) passes `hide3D`, and Upload renders its own IngestScene3D in IngestingPanel. So PipelineTimeline.jsx:9's lazy import, its Suspense fallback and the `!hide3D` block are dead — while still counting as a third code path that pulls the same chunk.
- **The bundle guard keys on a chunk name that is an accident of the import graph.** MUST_STAY_LAZY = ['useSceneRuntime', 'OverviewCharts'] matches filename prefixes. `useSceneRuntime` names the three.js chunk only because it happens to be the module HeroScene, AuthScene and IngestScene3D all share. Rename the hook, or drop it from one scene, and the prefix stops matching — the check then passes silently while the guarantee is gone. There is no test asserting the guard's own names still correspond to real chunks.
- **ProgressRing's colour bands disagree with the verdict bands beside them.** ProgressRing.jsx:23 switches at 60 and 30, while verdictTone/verdictExplanation (utils.js:275, mirroring services/novelty.py) band at 70 and 25. On the novelty screen a concentration of 65% draws a red ring next to a 'review suggested' (amber) verdict.
- **ProgressRing and AnimatedCounter have no reduced-motion gate.** framer's `reducedMotion: 'always'` suppresses transform and layout animations only, so the 1.4 s `strokeDashoffset` sweep and the spring-driven number both still run under the user's reduced-motion preference.
- **Double focus indicator on Buttons.** The base layer applies `outline: 3px solid var(--ring); outline-offset: 3px` to every `:focus-visible` (index.css:322), and Button additionally sets `focus-visible:ring-2 ring-[var(--ring)] ring-offset-2` (Button.jsx:11). Focused buttons paint both.
- **Button asChild forwards invalid attributes.** `disabled={disabled || loading}` is passed through Radix Slot regardless of the child element, so `<Button asChild><a/></Button>` in a loading state emits `disabled` on an anchor — not a valid attribute and not actually disabling anything.
- **Sheet stays mounted and focus-trapped while CSS-hidden.** The default `responsiveClass='md:hidden'` is applied to the overlay and the aside, not to the mount condition, so an open Sheet at >= md width is a `display:none` Radix Dialog.Content that still traps focus and locks body scroll.
- **useDeterministicDismiss re-subscribes its keydown listener on every render.** `{ canClose = () => true }` (Modal.jsx:27) creates a new function identity per call and `canClose` is in the effect's dep array; ConfirmDialog likewise builds `canClose` inline each render (Modal.jsx:136). Functionally harmless, but the effect tears down and re-adds on every render of every open dialog.
- **Modal's select-click swallow is a one-frame heuristic.** A pointerdown on `select, [role="combobox"]` inside the content sets a ref that eats the *next* overlay click (Modal.jsx:46-59). It is cleared by the swallow itself or by a native select's change event — so a user who opens a Radix select and then clicks the overlay twice in a row still dismisses on the second click, and a stale flag can eat one legitimate overlay dismissal.
- **Two independent sources of truth for dark mode.** UI primitives read `usePreferences().isDark`; the scenes read `useIsDark()`, a MutationObserver on `documentElement.classList` (chosen because the scenes live outside the nav's state tree). They agree only because PreferencesContext toggles the class — a future theme toggle that skipped the class would desync the scenes.
- **No SSR guards in three places that read the DOM during render/initialization.** `TiltCard` calls `window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)')` in a useState initializer (Motion.jsx:162) and never re-reads it, so rotating a tablet keeps the pre-rotation verdict; `useSceneRuntime` reads `document.hidden`; `useSceneCapability` reads `document.visibilityState`. useMediaQuery, by contrast, correctly returns false under SSR.
- **SpotlightCard has no reduced-motion or effects gate** — it writes --spot-x/--spot-y on every pointermove and always renders the `.spotlight-overlay`, which is only an opacity transition (clamped by the CSS override) but is not in the `.effects-decorative` set either.
- **MarqueeRow relies entirely on CSS for reduced motion.** Under `data-motion="reduced"` the marquee animation runs once in 0.01 ms and, with no fill-mode, snaps back to translateX(0); the duplicated `aria-hidden` half remains in the DOM either way.
- **TypewriterText's effect deps include the `phrases` array identity** (Motion.jsx:110), so a call site passing an inline literal restarts the typing timer on every render while `state.current` (phrase/char/deleting) persists — mid-word restarts.
- **PageSkeleton is aria-hidden="true" with no live-region announcement**, so screen-reader users get silence during a route chunk load. RouteSkeleton also matches on the *first* path segment only, so nested admin routes all get the admin shape and anything unmapped falls to 'generic'.
- **Toaster sets duration twice** — `duration={4000}` on the root and `toastOptions.duration = reducedMotion ? 6000 : 4000`; the toastOptions value wins, making the root prop misleading. The entire toast visual identity is ~190 lines of `!important` overrides in index.css:427-658 keyed on sonner's `[data-sonner-*]` attributes, which will break on any sonner DOM change; `[data-sonner-toaster] { z-index: 999999 }` sits above every other layer in the app (Select 140, Tooltip 100, ConfirmDialog 91, Modal 81, Sheet 51, mobile nav 40).
- **`.prose-chat table` forces `display: block`** (index.css:773-781) so a model-emitted table scrolls rather than widening the thread — but that also drops the table's semantic layout box, and the generation prompt forbids tables in the first place, so this is defensive dead-ish styling.
- **contrast.js's rgbFromHex rejects 8-digit hex.** materialSemanticTokens emits `--card` as `#RRGGBBAA` and `--border`/`--input` with alpha suffixes, so any future test that tries to measure those tokens through `blend`/`contrastRatio` will throw TypeError rather than fail an assertion.
- **PHASE_CONFIGS copy hardcodes frozen RAG constants in prose** — '800-TOKEN SEMANTIC SLICING', '100-TOKEN OVERLAP', '768D', 'COSINE SIMILARITY MATRIX 85%', 'GEMINI EMBEDDING 001' (IngestScene3D.jsx:23-66). None of it reads from config; changing a frozen contract in config.py leaves this HUD lying, and no repository-fact test covers it.
- **UploadBatch fabricates a stage from a progress percentage** (UploadBatch.jsx:668: `progress < 20 ? 'download' : progress < 40 ? 'malware_scan' : …`), so the batch HUD claims pipeline stages the worker never reported.
- **StageExtractClean reaches into `groupRef.current.children[0].children`** to animate slabs (IngestScene3D.jsx:304-309), as does StageChunking (:374-381) — positional child indexing that breaks the moment a mesh is inserted above the slab group. StageExtractClean also calls `Math.random()` inside useFrame (:317-318), the only unseeded randomness in the scenes, defeating the StrictMode-determinism property everything else maintains.
- **ConstellationOrb's pulses re-target from a mutable ref during render-adjacent frame work** (`randRef.current()` at ConstellationOrb.jsx:172) and mutate the memoized `pulses` objects in place (`pulse.curve`, `pulse.lastU`), so the useMemo result is not actually immutable state.
- **buildArcs can silently return fewer arcs than requested** — it gives up after `maxArcs * 60` attempts within the [0.4, 1.15] distance window (constellation.js:54-55) — and `pulses` picks `Math.floor(rand() * curves.length)`, so an empty `curves` array would make `curves[pulse.curve].getPointAt` throw. Not currently reachable at 140 nodes / radius 2, but unguarded.
- **HeroScene owns a second, redundant viewport listener.** It re-implements a `(max-width: 1023px)` matchMedia subscription (HeroScene.jsx:22-29) to compute `simple`, duplicating what useMediaQuery/useSceneCapability already do at a different breakpoint (768px for the mount decision, 1023px for the quality decision).
- **ThesisCards renders DOM through drei's <Html>**, so three hardcoded demo thesis titles ('CNN-Based Rice Leaf Disease Detection', etc.) become real, non-aria-hidden DOM inside the decorative hero — mitigated only by the wrapper's aria-hidden and `pointerEvents: 'none'`.
- **`useIdleReady` latches and never resets**, so once a scene has been allowed to mount it stays allowed for the document's lifetime even if `enabled` goes false.
- **useSceneCapability returns a fresh object every render** (it calls the pure evaluator inline), so any consumer memoizing on the verdict object identity will thrash.
- **avatarPublicUrl's allowlist regex forbids characters Supabase user ids do not contain but paths might** — `/^[a-zA-Z0-9-]+\/[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+$/` permits exactly one path segment pair, so any nested avatar path returns null; extractOwnedAvatarPath (utils.js:281) accepts deeper paths, so the two helpers disagree on what a valid avatar path is.
- **`html { scroll-behavior: smooth }` is global** (index.css:302) and only neutralized by the reduced-motion blocks, so every in-page anchor and programmatic scroll animates for full-motion users, including the Pagination scroll-restoration hack it fights with.

### Open questions

- Is the dead [data-palette] CSS block intentional pre-hydration insurance, or a leftover from a pre-Material-generator design? As written it can never paint, and it silently loses the only attempt to keep --secondary distinct from --primary under the gold palette.
- Should IngestScene3D go behind useSceneCapability? It currently ignores every hard constraint the policy declares non-overridable (reduced motion, effects:'low', Data Saver, low memory, no WebGL), which contradicts sceneCapability.js's own stated design and leaves no fallback when WebGL is absent rather than merely lost.
- Was PipelineTimeline's 3D branch meant to be the single ingestion-scene call site, with Upload's IngestingPanel duplicating it by accident? Today the only caller passes hide3D, so one of the two is dead.
- Should the bundle guard assert its own chunk names still resolve? 'useSceneRuntime' as the three.js chunk name is an emergent property of the import graph; a rename would disarm the check without failing anything.
- Should --tertiary/--tertiary-foreground/--secondary/--secondary-foreground be dropped from materialSemanticTokens, or are they reserved for planned surfaces? Four properties are recomputed and written on every preference change with no consumers.
- ProgressRing's 30/60 colour bands vs the 25/70 verdict bands from services/novelty.py — which is authoritative for what the user sees on the novelty screen?
- Under effects:'low', surface-glass keeps --card's ~90% alpha with no blur. Should the low-effects override force an opaque surface instead, given that this is the exact configuration the axe matrix measures?
- Does anything enforce that PHASE_CONFIGS' hardcoded '800-TOKEN', '100-TOKEN OVERLAP', '768D', '85%' and 'GEMINI EMBEDDING 001' copy tracks config.py's frozen constants? No repository-fact test appears to cover the frontend HUD.
- Should index.html carry a pre-hydration inline script to apply the stored theme, given it hardcodes class="dark" and every light-mode user sees a dark flash?

**Depends on:** @material/material-color-utilities 0.4.0 (themeFromSourceColor, argbFromHex, hexFromArgb, CorePalette neutral tones) — legacy Scheme contract only, tailwindcss 4.3.3 + @tailwindcss/vite (@theme, @custom-variant, @layer, @apply), framer-motion 12.42.2 (motion, AnimatePresence, MotionConfig, MotionGlobalConfig, useSpring, useTransform, useInView, layoutId), @radix-ui/react-dialog 1.1.22, react-alert-dialog 1.1.22, react-select 2.3.6, react-slot 1.3.2, react-tooltip 1.2.15, class-variance-authority 0.7.1, clsx 2.1.1, tailwind-merge 3.6.0 (cn), lucide-react 1.26.0 (every icon), sonner 2.0.7 (toast DOM restyled wholesale via [data-sonner-*] !important rules), three 0.182.0, @react-three/fiber 9.6.1, @react-three/drei 10.7.7 (Float, Html) — lazy only, react 19.2.8 (useId, useSyncExternalStore), react-router 8.3.0 (useLocation in RouteSkeleton, useNavigate in CommandPalette), src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx — the single driver of the colour/motion/effects/contrast systems, src/supabaseClient (lib/avatar.js storage public URL), nginx/default.conf.template + index.html (font delivery / CSP contract)

**Used by:** src/main.jsx — PreferencesProvider, PreferenceMotion, Toaster, TooltipProvider, ErrorBoundary, index.css, src/components/AppShell.jsx — skip-link, surface-glass, safe-area-bottom, --primary-container nav pills, --muted kbd, Sheet, CommandPalette, src/pages/landing/* (Hero, LandingNav, BentoFeatures, Audiences, TracksMarquee, HowItWorks, StatsStrip, FinalCTA, AskDemo) — Aurora, Magnetic, TiltCard, SpotlightCard, MarqueeRow, TypewriterText, AnimatedCounter, ProgressRing, text-gradient-isu, font-accent, HeroScene, src/pages/Login.jsx + pages/auth/* — AuthScene, OtpInput, gradient-border, bg-grid, mask-fade-b, AuthFx variants, src/pages/Upload.jsx, UploadBatch.jsx, components/upload/* (Dropzone, WizardStepper, WizardStep, WizardRail, MetadataForm, ReviewPanel, PipelineTimeline, IngestOutcome) — IngestScene3D, motionTokens, ProgressRing, animate-dash-march, animate-stage-sheen, src/pages/Chat.jsx — prose-chat, state-layer, --surface-3, --ring, src/pages/Archive.jsx — GooglePagination, TableScroller, --surface-0, src/pages/admin/* (Admin, AdminOverview, OverviewCharts, SystemManagementTab, OperationsTab, UploadHistoryTab) — Badge/RoleBadge (tone literals asserted by badgeTones.test.js), TableStateRow, TableScroller, PaginationFooter, staggerContainer/Item, src/pages/Settings.jsx + pages/settings/SectionCard.jsx — AppearanceControls, staggerItem, src/pages/Novelty.jsx — ProgressRing, verdict helpers from lib/utils.js, --ring, src/components/MfaEnrollDialog.jsx, components/security/SecurityCheck.jsx — glass-subtle, state-layer, staggerContainer/Item, src/App.jsx — RouteSkeleton / PageSkeleton per lazy route Suspense boundary, paper / docs evidence: evaluation/iso25010_evidence.md consumes the axe report the a11y matrix emits

**Additional notes returned by the reader** (fields outside the schema, preserved verbatim)

- `purposeNote`: n/a

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 15 correction(s), 7 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** materialSemanticTokens returns a 28-entry map; '28 semantic custom properties emitted'; '28 inline custom properties' written on <html>.
  **Actual:** It returns 32 keys. Counted directly: the object literal has 32 '--…' entries (--surface-0..3 are four separate keys, which the map's own dataModel list collapses into one line). applyMaterialTheme therefore writes 32 inline properties, not 28. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/materialTheme.js:44-77`)
- **Wrong:** 'four decorative WebGL scenes behind a capability gate'; 'Dead return value across all four scenes'.
  **Actual:** There are three Canvas roots and exactly three useSceneRuntime consumers: HeroScene, AuthScene, IngestScene3D. ConstellationOrb/ParticleField/ThesisCards are sub-components inside HeroScene's and AuthScene's canvases, not scenes with their own runtime. Also only two of the three are behind the capability gate at all (the map says so elsewhere, so 'four … behind a capability gate' double-counts). (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/HeroScene.jsx:21, AuthScene.jsx:19, IngestScene3D.jsx:714`)
- **Wrong:** 'Low-effects mode makes glass *more* likely to fail contrast … --card is `${surfaces.low}E6`, i.e. still ~90% translucent. So `.surface-glass` under effects=low is semi-transparent with no blur.'
  **Actual:** Inverted. E6 = 0.902 alpha, i.e. ~90% OPAQUE / ~10% translucent. Worse for the argument: in the balanced state .surface-glass paints `color-mix(in srgb, var(--card), transparent 8%)` (~83% opaque) plus a blur; the effects=low override replaces that with `background: var(--card) !important`, which is MORE opaque than the default. Low effects makes the glass surface less transparent, not less legible — the blur removal is the only regression. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/index.css:398-405 and 787-795; rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/materialTheme.js:47`)
- **Wrong:** 'Accessibility is enforced by construction rather than by review: de-emphasised text is explicit neutral tones instead of opacity-*' and the invariant 'De-emphasised text uses --color-ink-muted / --color-ink-faint … never opacity-*'.
  **Actual:** That rule is enforced only inside BADGE_TONE_CLASSES strings (badgeTones.test.js:63 greps the exported tone classes and nothing else). The design system itself ships opacity-blended de-emphasised body text in three places, none covered by any test: ConfirmDialog's description (`opacity-75`), the AppearanceControls explainer paragraph (`opacity-75`), and `.prose-chat blockquote` (`opacity-90`). The invariant is aspirational outside badges. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Modal.jsx:165, src/components/ui/AppearanceControls.jsx:114, src/index.css:769-771`)
- **Wrong:** index.css `@theme` block contains '13 keyframes'.
  **Actual:** @theme contains 11 @keyframes (aurora, float, shimmer, marquee, pulse-glow, gradient-x, shine, sweep, caret-blink, dash-march, stage-sheen) against 14 `--animate-*` tokens. The file has 13 total: `m3-menu-in` and `m3-menu-out` sit outside @theme and outside any @layer, driving the Radix Select popover, with `html[data-motion="reduced"] .m3-select-content { animation: none !important }` as a separate preference-keyed kill switch the map's four-mechanism motion model omits. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/index.css:105-157 vs 815-840`)
- **Wrong:** sceneCapability.test.js is '29 cases'.
  **Actual:** 28 `it(` literals; 36 cases at runtime, because two of them are parameterized over the 5-entry HARD table (one 'blocks on X' and one "still blocks … even when effects is 'full'" per hard signal). The file uses describe/it, not the flat `test()` form the map's other test descriptions imply. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/sceneCapability.test.js:39-59`)
- **Wrong:** 'evaluateSceneCapability then applies five hard constraints in order — no WebGL, reduced motion, effects === low, Data Saver, deviceMemory < 4, hardwareConcurrency < 4'.
  **Actual:** That sentence names six. The code has six unconditional early returns before the soft block. The 'five' figure comes from the module's own prose, which excludes low-effects from its hard list (sceneCapability.js:15-17) even though the code treats it identically; the test's HARD table likewise has five entries and tests effects:'low' separately. Worth flagging because the doc-vs-code count is itself a small inconsistency in the source. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/sceneCapability.js:65-71`)
- **Wrong:** Invariant: "useSceneRuntime's static signals (webgl, deviceMemory, logicalCores) are read exactly once per document — repeated WebGL probing allocates throwaway GL contexts."
  **Actual:** Wrong hook and wrong scope. useSceneRuntime reads none of those signals; the once-only `useState(() => ({ webgl: detectWebgl(), ...readBrowserSignals() }))` is in useSceneCapability. And it is once per hook *instance*, not per document: Hero and Login each mount their own, so a session that visits both probes WebGL twice. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/useSceneCapability.js:27-30 vs useSceneRuntime.js:5-14`)
- **Wrong:** Pagination's public surface is `<Pagination page setPage total limit showJump hideOnSinglePage className/>`, with the page list and ellipsis logic implied to live in the component.
  **Actual:** Incomplete in a load-bearing way: all page maths is delegated to src/lib/pagination.js (`totalPageCount`, `clampPage`, `getPaginationPages` with its 7-slot Google sliding window and `maxVisible` default), a module the map never lists in keyFiles or dependsOn and which has its own test file. Also `showJump` is tri-state, not boolean: when undefined it auto-enables at totalPages > 7, and the jump form additionally requires totalPages > 1. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Pagination.jsx:4,32-37,154 and rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/pagination.js:9-60`)
- **Wrong:** PHASE_CONFIGS is the place where frozen RAG constants are hardcoded in prose, and 'no repository-fact test covers it'.
  **Actual:** The second half is right but the leak is wider than PHASE_CONFIGS: Upload.jsx's live-telemetry chips also hardcode '768d' and '800 tok' as plain literals next to the scene, in the same panel, with the same absence of any test. IngestScene3D is not the only frontend surface that will lie if config.py's Literal[800]/Literal[768] move. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:88-101`)
- **Wrong:** 'Input, Textarea, Select … Shared FloatingLabel + FieldErrorHint.'
  **Actual:** Select does not use FloatingLabel. It has its own SelectFloatingLabel with a different state model (permanently raised, `group-focus-visible:` / `group-data-[state=open]:` colour transitions instead of the peer-placeholder-shown trick). Only FieldErrorHint is genuinely shared across all four. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Input.jsx:31-52 vs 283-303`)
- **Wrong:** Non-labelled Input/Textarea show 'a red border and no message at all — including to screen readers'.
  **Actual:** Nearly right, but the bare path still sets `aria-invalid="true"`, so the field is announced as invalid — what is missing is the describedby/message, not the error state. The discarded `containerClassName` and the absent FieldErrorHint are both confirmed. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Input.jsx:124-143 and 210-229`)
- **Wrong:** ConstellationOrb builds arcs with maxArcs 64.
  **Actual:** 64 is buildArcs' default, but the call site passes `maxArcs: degraded ? 40 : 64`, so the degraded orb has 40 arcs and re-runs buildArcs (the memo depends on `degraded`), regenerating the merged LineSegments buffer on the first FPS degrade. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/ConstellationOrb.jsx:89-92`)
- **Wrong:** UploadBatch 'fabricates a stage from a progress percentage (UploadBatch.jsx:668: progress < 20 ? download : progress < 40 ? malware_scan : …)'.
  **Actual:** Correct as far as it goes, but the material consequence is unstated: the chain is 20/40/60/80 → download / malware_scan / extract / embed / index, so two of the seven canonical stages ('chunk' and 'screen') are unreachable from the batch HUD, and StageChunking/StageNoveltyScreen never play there — while HolographicDocument's `activeStageKey === 'chunk'` branch can never fire either. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/UploadBatch.jsx:665-670 and src/components/three/IngestScene3D.jsx:87`)
- **Wrong:** Anchors: 'asserted both ways (materialTheme.test.js:22-23)'; 'e2e/accessibility.spec.js:52-58 runs the entire matrix with effects: low'; 'the a11y suite … because sampling mid-fade once reported 1.24:1'.
  **Actual:** Three anchor/number slips. The neutral.tone assertion is line 19 and the surfaceContainer negative is line 22 (23 is the Number.isInteger regex). `effects: 'low'` is set at accessibility.spec.js:474 (52-58 is the baseProfile fixture). 1.24:1 is main.jsx:18's figure; the a11y spec's own comment records 1.04:1 for the upload-history pipeline labels, the same number tokens.js cites. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/design/materialTheme.test.js:19-23; rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js:446,474; rag-thesis-frontend/src/main.jsx:18`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- **ChoiceGrid is a fake radiogroup.** AppearanceControls — described only as 'the only editor for the 5 preferences' — implements theme and palette selection as `role="radiogroup"` containing plain `<button role="radio" aria-checked>` with no roving tabindex and no arrow-key handler, so every option is a separate tab stop and arrow keys do nothing. This is the control surface for the entire colour/motion/contrast system and it is exercised by the axe matrix, which does not test keyboard semantics. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/AppearanceControls.jsx:18-49)
- **The dark FOUC is worse than stated.** Beyond index.html hardcoding class="dark" with no pre-hydration script, `systemPrefersDark()` returns `true` when `window.matchMedia` is absent (`?? true`), so the 'system' default also fails toward dark. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx:26-28)
- **The scenes violate the lib/keys.js invariant the map states as universal.** Four `key={idx}` / `key={i}` index keys inside IngestScene3D (document text lines, extract slabs, chunk cards, pgvector tiers) and one in LogoActivityDots. Harmless (fixed-length static lists) but the map asserts 'List keys for fixed-length placeholder rows come from slotKeys()' as an invariant, and these are exactly that case and do not. (IngestScene3D.jsx:113,334,396,603; LogoActivityDots.jsx:18)
- **Skip-link shares Tooltip's z-index.** `.skip-link { z-index: 100 }` equals TooltipContent's `z-[100]`, so an open tooltip and a focused skip link are in the same stacking band — the map's z-index ladder (Select 140, Tooltip 100, ConfirmDialog 91 …) omits the skip link entirely, and it is the one element that must paint above everything when focused. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/index.css:407-422 vs src/components/ui/Tooltip.jsx:27)
- **A fourth reduced-motion CSS mechanism exists, keyed to the app preference, not the media query.** `html[data-motion="reduced"] .m3-select-content { animation: none !important }` disables the Select popover's m3-menu-in/out specifically; it sits outside every @layer alongside the effects=low block, and it is the only place a Tailwind/CSS animation is killed by the *app* preference rather than by the global 0.01ms clamp. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/index.css:825-840)
- **Toaster's duration precedence is confirmed, not merely asserted.** sonner resolves `duration: toastOptions?.duration ?? duration`, so the root `duration={4000}` is genuinely dead whenever toastOptions.duration is set — worth stating as verified rather than inferred. (node_modules/sonner/dist/index.mjs, `_toastOptions_duration != null ? _toastOptions_duration : duration`)
- **Everything else in the map that I could check, checked out.** Including the fiddly ones: animate-spin 9 / animate-pulse 8 / animate-ping 4 / animate-bounce 1 real call sites after excluding spin-slow and pulse-glow; animate-float-slow 0 sites; the eleven-entry effects=low kill list; Hero.jsx:71 `allowed || reason === 'page-hidden'` with rootMargin '600px 0px'; vite.config.js:113-125 manualChunks prose and `resolve.dedupe: ['three']`; the full bundle-budget contract (330/250/115/150 + MUST_STAY_LAZY + the zero-eager-assets guard); the 9 BLOCK_REASONS and their exact ordering; `isBelow` requiring a finite positive; detectWebgl's `{antialias:false, powerPreference:'default'}`; the dead [data-palette] block (33 lines, 260-292) and the inline-style precedence argument; the dead --glass-opacity/--radius/--tertiary*/--secondary(-foreground) tokens (grepped: 0 consumers each); GlassCard's no-op `strong`; the hardcoded `jump-to-page-input` id and double scrollTo; Pagination's `motion-reduce:` at 87/119/147; Field's unreferenced labelId/descriptionId; Select's `<option>`-only filter and synthesized event; useDeterministicDismiss's per-render canClose identity; Sheet's focus trap under `md:hidden`; the unreachable context-loss retry (all three consumers early-return on `lost`); IngestScene3D bypassing the capability gate at all three call sites; PipelineTimeline's dead 3D branch (sole caller passes hide3D); ProgressRing 60/30 vs verdict 70/25 with Novelty.jsx:168 passing concentration and no `color`; StageExtractClean's `Math.random()` in useFrame as the only unseeded randomness; the avatarPublicUrl/extractOwnedAvatarPath disagreement on nested paths; and the full CSP/fontDelivery pin.

## 6. `fe-quality` - Frontend test/E2E harness and quality gates

Three independent gate layers guard the React client: (1) Node's built-in test runner over 22 colocated `*.test.js` files covering only pure, importable JS helpers — no JSX, no DOM, no React renderer, no vitest/jest; (2) a Playwright/Chromium suite of 26 tests in 3 spec files that runs against a real production-shaped build served on 127.0.0.1:4173, with all backend and Supabase traffic redirected to synthetic origins (`/__e2e_api`, `/__e2e_supabase`) so no live credential can ever be reached; (3) a gzip bundle budget that reads the built `index.html` to determine the eager payload and asserts the WebGL and charting chunks stay behind `lazy()`. A fourth, non-CI script validates production security headers against a deployed URL. All of the first three run in `.github/workflows/quality.yml`; their numbers feed the thesis's ISO/IEC 25010 evidence and SonarQube.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-frontend/playwright.config.js` (38 lines) - Playwright config: testDir ./e2e, globalSetup, fullyParallel:false, workers CI?2:1, retries CI?2:0, forbidOnly on CI, baseURL 127.0.0.1:4173, single chromium project, webServer = `node e2e/vite-server.mjs` with reuseExistingServer gated on PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER==="1"
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/vite-server.mjs` (45 lines) - Builds `--mode e2e` into dist-e2e via the programmatic vite `build()`, then `preview()` on 127.0.0.1:4173 strictPort. Shuts down on SIGINT/SIGTERM/SIGHUP or the literal line `close` on stdin
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/run-playwright.mjs` (56 lines) - `npm run test:e2e` entrypoint. Spawns vite-server.mjs with a piped stdin, polls http://127.0.0.1:4173 for up to 120 s, then spawns node_modules/@playwright/test/cli.js with PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER=1 and forwards argv.slice(2); stops the server through stdin in a finally block
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/global-setup.js` (18 lines) - rm -rf test-results/axe-shards/ before workers start. Dead in practice — see gotchas
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/critical-flows.spec.js` (690 lines) - 13 end-to-end journey tests with a per-test fail-closed `mockApi` that records unmocked keys and fulfills them 501
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/accessibility.spec.js` (573 lines) - 12 axe-core tests (one per surface), each scanning 4 desktop theme/contrast states at 1280px plus dark-standard at 360px = 60 axe runs total; writes per-surface JSON shards and merges them into axe-report.json in afterAll
- `rag-thesis-frontend/e2e/visual-quality.spec.js` (114 lines) - 1 test: structural DOM audit of /, /login, /chat at 360/768/1280/1536 px; optional screenshot evidence capture
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/testing/e2eSession.js` (21 lines) - The e2e-mode switch: `isE2ETestMode = import.meta.env.MODE === 'e2e'` plus read/clear of the localStorage auth fixture
- `rag-thesis-frontend/vite.config.js` (130 lines) - Declares the e2e-mode defines (VITE_API_URL, VITE_SUPABASE_URL, VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY='') and the `e2eApiGuard` plugin installed on both dev and preview servers
- `rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` (153 lines) - Gzip-level-9 bundle budget: 330 kB eager, useSceneRuntime 250 kB, OverviewCharts 115 kB, 150 kB default per chunk, plus the must-stay-lazy name check
- `rag-thesis-frontend/scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs` (28 lines) - Fetches a deployed URL and regex-checks CSP/X-Frame-Options/X-Content-Type-Options/Referrer-Policy/Permissions-Policy (+HSTS on https). Not wired into CI
- `rag-thesis-frontend/package.json` (65 lines) - Defines `test` (bare `node --test`), `test:coverage` (85/85/80 + lcov), `test:e2e`, `bundle:budget`, `security:headers`; devDeps pin @playwright/test 1.61.1 and @axe-core/playwright 4.12.1
- `.github/workflows/quality.yml` (~330 lines) - The `frontend` job runs lint → test:coverage → build → bundle:budget → playwright install chromium → test:e2e, in that order, on node 24.18.0
- `rag-thesis-frontend/eslint.config.js` (61 lines) - Flat config; globalIgnores dist, dist-e2e, node_modules, playwright-report, test-results; `files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}']` with globals.browser only
- `sonar-project.properties` (77 lines) - Consumes rag-thesis-frontend/coverage/lcov.info; excludes src/**/*.test.js from source analysis rather than using sonar.test.inclusions
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_readme_accuracy.py` (~270 lines) - Pins four frontend README facts from Python: npm version from packageManager, the lazy admin-tab count from Admin.jsx, and that `test:coverage` and `bundle:budget` are documented (lines 147-195)

### Control flow

**`--mode e2e` fixture swap.** `npm run test:e2e` → `e2e/run-playwright.mjs` spawns `e2e/vite-server.mjs` (piped stdin, inherited stdout/stderr), which calls Vite's programmatic `build({mode:'e2e', build:{outDir:'dist-e2e'}})` then `preview({mode:'e2e', build:{outDir:'dist-e2e'}, preview:{host:'127.0.0.1', port:4173, strictPort:true}})`. Both resolve `vite.config.js`'s `defineConfig(({mode}) => …)` with mode `'e2e'`, so two things fire: the `e2eApiGuard()` plugin enters the plugin array (vite.config.js:79), and the `define` block (vite.config.js:80-89) replaces `import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL`, `VITE_SUPABASE_URL`, `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` and `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` with literals. This override is load-bearing and it does win: Vite's define plugin builds its map as `{...processEnv, ...importMetaKeys, ...userDefine, ...importMetaFallbackKeys}` (node_modules/vite/dist/node/chunks/node.js:24723-24728) and merges `userDefineEnv` last into the serialized `import.meta.env` object, so the real `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` sitting in `rag-thesis-frontend/.env` cannot leak into an e2e build by either access form. `src/api.js:9` then sets the axios `baseURL` to `/__e2e_api`, and `src/supabaseClient.js:14` builds a real supabase-js client pointed at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/__e2e_supabase`. Inside the app, `src/testing/e2eSession.js:3` computes `isE2ETestMode` from `import.meta.env.MODE`, and six modules branch on it: `api.js:74-82` skips `supabase.auth.getSession()` and attaches a literal `Bearer deterministic-e2e-token` when a fixture with a `user` is present (or the `X-Guest-ID` header when not); `api.js:133,141` disables the silent-refresh retry and the `signOut()` on 401 (it still hard-navigates to `loginPathWithNext(...)`); `AuthContext.jsx:24-28,162-176,179,203,269-276` seeds `user`/`profile`/`features`/`needsMfa` synchronously from the fixture, starts with `loading=false`, skips `getSession()`/`onAuthStateChange` and the realtime broadcast channel entirely, and makes `signOut()` a local `clearE2EAuthFixture()`; `Admin.jsx:52,119` disables the MFA-factor query and forces `adminSecurityState` to `'ready'`; `IdleSessionGuard.jsx:75` disarms the idle-logout timer; `main.jsx:26-27` sets `MotionGlobalConfig.skipAnimations` only when e2e mode *and* `localStorage.isu_e2e_skip_animations === '1'`; `turnstileConfig.js:1-11` substitutes a fake site key only when e2e mode *and* `localStorage.isu_e2e_turnstile === '1'`. The auth fixture itself is a JSON blob written to `localStorage['isu_e2e_auth_fixture']` by `page.addInitScript`, read once at `AuthProvider` construction with a shape guard (`e2eSession.js:10`: a fixture carrying a `user` is rejected wholesale unless it has `user.id` and `profile.role`).

**The fail-closed 501 guard.** Two layers. Server side, `e2eApiGuard` mounts a connect middleware at the `/__e2e_api` path prefix on both the dev and preview servers (vite.config.js:55-61). Because the `configurePreviewServer` hook returns `undefined` rather than a post-hook function, the middleware is installed *ahead of* Vite's internal middlewares, so it pre-empts the SPA history fallback — the failure the comment at vite.config.js:51-54 describes is that without it an unmocked XHR gets `200 text/html` and surfaces as a JSON parse error instead of a clean refusal. Test side, each spec installs `page.route('**/__e2e_api/**', …)`. `critical-flows.spec.js:39-62` normalises a request to `"<METHOD> <pathname minus /__e2e_api>"`, looks it up in the per-test `handlers` then in two defaults (`GET /health`, `GET /settings/public`), and on a miss pushes the key into an `unexpected` array and fulfills 501; every test that uses it ends with `expect(unexpected).toEqual([])`. A handler is either a literal body, a function of the request, or an envelope `{__status, __body}` for non-200s. `accessibility.spec.js:297-318` does the same with a path-only (method-insensitive) `API_FIXTURES` table and an `unmocked` array asserted per surface. `visual-quality.spec.js:32-47` deliberately does **not** fail closed: unknown paths get `200 []`.

**Why dist-e2e.** `vite-server.mjs:12` pins `OUT_DIR='dist-e2e'` and its header comment gives both reasons: the suite runs against a built bundle because the dev server's on-demand transform, not the browser, was the single-threaded bottleneck that capped Playwright parallelism; and it must never land in `dist/` because `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs:33` hardcodes `dist/` as what it measures, so an e2e-mode build (different defines, different tree-shaking) standing in for the production build would silently invalidate the payload gate. Both directories are gitignored (.gitignore:11-12) and both are in eslint's `globalIgnores`.

**Bundle budget.** `check-bundle-budget.mjs:62-73` derives the eager set from the built `dist/index.html` by three regexes — `<script type=module src>`, `<link rel=modulepreload href>`, `<link rel=stylesheet href>` — i.e. the browser's own definition of "needed before first interaction" rather than a guess. It hard-fails if the parse yields zero hrefs (a silently-changed build format would otherwise report 0 kB and pass). Every eager asset is gzipped at level 9, summed, and compared against 330 kB; `chunkPrefix()` strips a `-<hash≥6>.js|css` suffix to recover the logical name; any eager prefix in `MUST_STAY_LAZY` is a violation with a message naming the likely cause (a static `import` replacing `lazy(() => import(...))`); then every `.js`/`.css` file directly under `dist/assets` is checked against its named cap or the 150 kB default. Measured on today's build: eager payload **324.7 kB across 12 files against the 330 kB budget — 5.3 kB of headroom**, `useSceneRuntime-*.js` 232.08 kB against its 250 kB cap, `OverviewCharts-*.js` 107.08 kB against 115 kB. The lazy split is enforced upstream in `vite.config.js:113-125`, whose `manualChunks` deliberately creates only `motion` and `vendor` groups and explicitly refuses to group three.js/recharts because rolldown's group capture drags shared deps (scheduler, clsx) in and makes the entry eagerly preload the whole subtree; `scheduler` is pinned into `vendor` for exactly that reason.

**Accessibility matrix.** For each of 12 surfaces, `accessibility.spec.js:485` marks `test.slow()`, installs the API/Supabase/Google-Fonts routes, `addInitScript`s the skip-animations flag (so `main.jsx` sees it on first module evaluation), optionally `addInitScript`s the role fixture, then navigates to `/` purely to create the origin so `localStorage` can be written. `scan()` (465-482) then, per state: `setViewportSize`, `emulateMedia({colorScheme, reducedMotion:'reduce'})`, write the preferences blob, `goto(surface.path)`, await the surface's `ready()` locator, run its optional `prepare()` (the three `/admin` tab surfaces click into their tab), `settleAnimations()`, and `new AxeBuilder({page}).analyze()` with no rule filtering. `settleAnimations` (450-463) polls, via two nested rAFs, until the joined computed `opacity` of every `[style*=\"opacity\"]` element stops changing — the comment records that sampling mid-fade measured the upload-history pipeline labels at 1.04:1, a ratio their static classes cannot produce. Findings are flattened (first 4 nodes, html truncated to 240 chars), written to `test-results/axe-shards/<surface>.json` **before** the assertions so a failing surface still contributes, and only then asserted: no unmocked calls, and no `serious`/`critical` violations. A file-scoped `afterAll` re-reads every shard from disk, splits blocking from advisory, and writes `test-results/axe-report.json` (plus a dated `docs/evidence/accessibility/<date>/` copy under `CAPTURE_A11Y_EVIDENCE=1`) with an explicit `scope_note` that manual screen-reader transcripts and real-GPU 3D profiling remain open PI-05/PI-06 gates. Reading from disk rather than memory is what makes the report correct when Playwright respawns a worker after a failure and runs the hook more than once.

**Visual-quality matrix.** One test, 12 (viewport × route) iterations, light scheme, reduced motion. Per iteration it asserts `main` visible and exactly one `h1`, then runs a single `page.evaluate` DOM audit returning `documentElement.scrollWidth - innerWidth` (≤1 allowed), duplicate `[id]` values, *visible* `button|a|input|select|textarea` with no accessible name from aria-label / aria-labelledby / title / placeholder / `labels` / text content (hidden inputs exempt), visible `img` with no `alt`, and the h1 count. It collects `console` errors across the whole loop and asserts the array is empty at the very end. Screenshot capture is conditional on `CAPTURE_VISUAL_EVIDENCE=1` *and* `route==='landing' || width===360 || width===1536`, which yields exactly the 8 PNGs currently committed under docs/evidence/visual-baselines/.

### Public surface

- npm run test — bare `node --test` from rag-thesis-frontend/. Node's default discovery glob picks up the 22 `**/*.test.js` files and nothing else; verified 209 tests / 176 top-level / 7 suites, all passing, in ~400 ms. `.spec.js` is NOT in Node's default glob, which is the only reason `node --test` does not try to execute the Playwright specs.
- npm run test:coverage — `node -e "mkdirSync('coverage')"` (Node's lcov reporter will not create its destination dir) && `node --test --experimental-test-coverage --test-coverage-exclude="**/*.test.js" --test-coverage-lines=85 --test-coverage-functions=85 --test-coverage-branches=80 --test-reporter=lcov --test-reporter-destination=coverage/lcov.info --test-reporter=spec --test-reporter-destination=stdout`. Measured today: 96.62 lines / 87.90 branches / 97.00 functions over 21 files.
- npm run test:e2e [-- <playwright args>] — node e2e/run-playwright.mjs; extra argv passes straight through to the Playwright CLI (e.g. `-- e2e/critical-flows.spec.js -g "guest"`).
- npm run test:e2e:install — `playwright install chromium` (CI uses `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium` instead).
- npm run bundle:budget — node scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs; requires a prior `npm run build` and exits 1 listing every violation.
- npm run security:headers -- <url> — node scripts/validate-security-headers.mjs; exit 2 with no argument, 1 on any missing/invalid header. Never invoked by CI.
- src/testing/e2eSession.js exports: `isE2ETestMode` (const, `import.meta.env.MODE === 'e2e'`), `readE2EAuthFixture()` → fixture|null, `clearE2EAuthFixture()`, `E2E_AUTH_STORAGE_KEY` (= 'isu_e2e_auth_fixture', exported but imported by nothing).
- vite.config.js `e2eApiGuard()` plugin — exposes `configureServer` and `configurePreviewServer`, both `server.middlewares.use('/__e2e_api', …)` returning 501 `{detail:'Unmocked E2E API request'}` for every method. Only in the plugin array when `mode === 'e2e'`.
- vite.config.js `backendReadiness()` plugin — `configureServer` only; serves `/__backend-ready` → `{ready:boolean}` by probing http://localhost:8000/health with a 1 s AbortSignal. Absent from the preview server, so e2e never has it.
- e2e-mode defines (vite.config.js:84-88): `import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL='/__e2e_api'`, `VITE_SUPABASE_URL='http://127.0.0.1:4173/__e2e_supabase'`, `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY='deterministic-e2e-anon-key'`; plus `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY=''` for e2e at vite.config.js:81-83.
- scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs constants: EAGER_BUDGET_KB=330, LAZY_CHUNK_BUDGETS_KB={useSceneRuntime:250, OverviewCharts:115}, DEFAULT_CHUNK_BUDGET_KB=150, MUST_STAY_LAZY=['useSceneRuntime','OverviewCharts'].
- accessibility.spec.js knobs: THEME_STATES = light-standard / light-high-contrast / dark-standard / dark-high-contrast; DESKTOP_WIDTH=1280; MOBILE_WIDTH=360; BLOCKING_IMPACTS=['serious','critical']; env `CAPTURE_A11Y_EVIDENCE=1` additionally writes docs/evidence/accessibility/<YYYY-MM-DD>/axe-report.json.
- visual-quality.spec.js knobs: VIEWPORTS=[360,768,1280,1536]; ROUTES=[/ , /login, /chat]; env `CAPTURE_VISUAL_EVIDENCE=1` writes docs/evidence/visual-baselines/<route>-<width>.png.
- E2E localStorage contract (the whole fixture API, all raw string literals): 'isu_e2e_auth_fixture' (JSON {user,profile,features,needsMfa?}), 'isu_e2e_skip_animations' ('1' → MotionGlobalConfig.skipAnimations), 'isu_e2e_turnstile' ('1' → fake Turnstile site key), 'isu-thesis-preferences-v2' (JSON {theme,palette,motion,effects,contrast}).
- PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER=1 — set by run-playwright.mjs; without it `npx playwright test` starts its own vite-server.mjs, which will fail on strictPort 4173 if one is already up.

### Invariants

- `import.meta.env.MODE === 'e2e'` is the single switch. Every fixture path in the app is gated on the `isE2ETestMode` constant, so a production build tree-shakes all of it (e.g. main.jsx:26 collapses to `skipAnimations = false`). Nothing reads a runtime env var to enable fixtures.
- Vite `define` beats `.env`. The e2e Supabase/API literals override any real value in rag-thesis-frontend/.env because userDefine is spread after importMetaKeys. Do not move these to `.env.e2e` — that would reverse the precedence.
- The e2e build output directory must stay `dist-e2e`. `scripts/check-bundle-budget.mjs` measures `dist/` and only `dist/`.
- The eager payload must stay ≤330 kB gzip and `useSceneRuntime` / `OverviewCharts` must never appear in the set of assets that dist/index.html preloads.
- `Admin.jsx` must keep lazy-importing exactly four tab modules with the literal `lazy(() => import('./admin/` — rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_readme_accuracy.py:180-188 counts those occurrences and requires the frontend README to spell the count in words ("four Admin tabs").
- `test:coverage` and `bundle:budget` must both remain in package.json scripts AND be named in rag-thesis-frontend/README.md (test_readme_accuracy.py:190-195), and `packageManager`'s npm version must appear in that README (174-178).
- Coverage thresholds are lines 85 / functions 85 / branches 80, encoded only in the package.json script string — there is no config file. The README states them as "85/80/85 lines/branches/functions", which is the same numbers in a different order.
- The four E2E localStorage keys are a hard-coded string contract duplicated between app and specs: 'isu_e2e_auth_fixture', 'isu_e2e_skip_animations', 'isu_e2e_turnstile', 'isu-thesis-preferences-v2'. No spec imports the exported constant.
- `skipAnimations` is opt-in per spec, not per run. main.jsx:22-25 records why: collapsing AnimatePresence enter/exit closes the appearance dialog mid-flow and breaks critical-flows. Only accessibility.spec.js sets the flag.
- The accessibility gate blocks on `serious`/`critical` impact only; `moderate`/`minor` are recorded as `advisory` in axe-report.json and never fail the build.
- Every accessibility surface must also make zero unmocked API calls — `expect(unmocked).toEqual([])` runs alongside the axe assertion.
- `stepInteraction` must return 'done' only for steps already passed (wizardSteps.test.js:75-83). This is an explicit guard against a Playwright strict-mode collision: accessible names match as substrings, so a focusable rail node named "Review" beside the footer "Review" button would make `getByRole` ambiguous.
- `DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES` must stay deep-frozen (permissions.test.js:45-48 asserts both a top-level and a nested write throw TypeError) and must mirror rag-thesis-backend routers/settings.py::DEFAULT_FEATURES.
- privilegedMfa.test.js:15 hard-codes the backend refusal sentence 'Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.' — it is pinned on the Python side too; rewording it breaks both suites.
- The batch idempotency keys submitted for two manuscripts must be distinct 36-char UUIDs and travel per row (critical-flows.spec.js:637-644 also asserts the multipart body contains no top-level `name="program_id"` part).
- Playwright `fullyParallel: false` — only whole spec files run side by side; within a file order is guaranteed. The accessibility shard/merge design depends on the afterAll being file-scoped.
- `PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER=1` must be set whenever a server is already listening on 4173, otherwise webServer starts a second vite-server.mjs which dies on `strictPort: true`.

### Data model

- E2E auth fixture (localStorage `isu_e2e_auth_fixture`): `{ user: {id, email, user_metadata:{full_name}}, profile: {role, full_name, email, department, status, avatar_url}, features: {<role>: {chat,archive,novelty,upload}}, needsMfa?: boolean }`. Validated by e2eSession.js:8-11; a fixture carrying `user` without `user.id` or `profile.role` is discarded entirely.
- Appearance preferences (localStorage `isu-thesis-preferences-v2`, owned by src/context/PreferencesContext.jsx:5): `{theme:'light'|'dark', palette:'isu', motion:'reduced'|…, effects:'low'|'balanced'|'full', contrast:'standard'|'high'}`.
- critical-flows mock handler map: `Record<"<METHOD> <path>", body | ((request) => body) | {__status, __body}>`, seeded with `GET /health` and `GET /settings/public`.
- accessibility API_FIXTURES: a flat path→body map (method-insensitive) covering /health, /settings/public, /settings/features, /analytics/{summary,overview,activity,users,logs/system,me}, /papers, /upload/tracks, /catalog/departments/legacy, /sessions, /duplication/history, /maintenance/{operations/summary,workers,upload-jobs,alerts,retention/report}.
- Axe finding shard (`test-results/axe-shards/<surface>.json`): array of `{surface, path, state, width, id, impact, help, helpUrl, tags, nodeCount, nodes[≤4]{target, html(≤240), failureSummary}}`.
- Merged axe report (`test-results/axe-report.json`, optionally `docs/evidence/accessibility/<date>/`): `{generated_at, axe_core, engine, matrix:{surfaces, theme_states, widths, configuration}, scope_note, totals:{blocking, advisory}, blocking[], advisory[]}`.
- visual-quality DOM audit result: `{horizontalOverflow:number, duplicateIds:string[], unnamedControls:string[], imagesWithoutAlt:string[], h1Count:number}`.
- Bundle-budget internal shape: eagerHrefs from index.html → per-asset gzip bytes → `eagerPrefixes:Set<string>` (post `chunkPrefix`) checked against MUST_STAY_LAZY, plus a violations string array printed in full before exit 1.

### Config knobs

- CI — enables `forbidOnly`, 2 Playwright workers and 2 retries (playwright.config.js:14-16).
- PLAYWRIGHT_REUSE_SERVER=1 — makes Playwright attach to an already-running 4173 instead of spawning its own webServer; set by run-playwright.mjs.
- CAPTURE_A11Y_EVIDENCE=1 — additionally writes docs/evidence/accessibility/<YYYY-MM-DD>/axe-report.json.
- CAPTURE_VISUAL_EVIDENCE=1 — creates docs/evidence/visual-baselines/ and writes the 8 landing/360/1536 screenshots with `animations:'disabled'`.
- VITE_API_URL / VITE_SUPABASE_URL / VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY / VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY — build-time; overridden by literals in e2e mode, supplied as Docker build ARGs in production (rag-thesis-frontend/Dockerfile:9-16).
- SUPABASE_ORIGIN / SUPABASE_WS_ORIGIN / API_ORIGIN / DOLLAR — envsubst variables in nginx/default.conf.template, which src/design/fontDelivery.test.js parses statically.
- localStorage flags read at module-evaluation time and therefore requiring `page.addInitScript`: isu_e2e_skip_animations, isu_e2e_turnstile, isu_e2e_auth_fixture.
- Node 24.18.0 / npm 11.16.0 — pinned by `.nvmrc`, `engines` and `packageManager`; CI uses actions/setup-node with 24.18.0 and caches node_modules keyed on package-lock.json.

### Error handling

- Unmocked backend call in e2e: HTTP 501 JSON `{detail:'Unmocked E2E API request'}` from the Vite preview middleware, or `{detail:'Unmocked E2E request: <key>'}` / `{detail:'Unmocked: <path>'}` from the spec's own route handler, which also records the key for an end-of-test assertion.
- Unmocked Supabase call in e2e: no guard — the SPA fallback answers 200 with index.html and supabase-js fails on the parse.
- axe serious/critical finding: the shard is written first, then `expect(blocking, '<surface> has serious/critical axe violations').toEqual([])` fails with a list of `impact/rule @ state/width (N nodes)` strings.
- Bundle budget violation: every violation is collected and printed under `Bundle budget exceeded:` with a closing instruction to raise the budget in the same commit; exit code 1. A build whose index.html parses to zero eager assets is a separate hard failure (exit 1) rather than a 0 kB pass.
- Missing `dist/index.html`: explicit `Bundle budget: no build found at … Run \`npm run build\` first.` and exit 1. Missing `dist/assets/`: unhandled `readdirSync` throw.
- E2E web server never becomes ready: run-playwright.mjs throws `Timed out waiting for the E2E web server` after 120 s, or `The E2E web server stopped before becoming ready` if the child exits first; the `finally` still attempts a graceful stdin `close` then a 5 s race then `kill()`, and the process exits 1.
- Playwright per-test artifacts: `trace: 'retain-on-failure'`, `screenshot: 'only-on-failure'`, `video: 'retain-on-failure'`; HTML report to playwright-report/ with `open:'never'`; CI retries each test twice.
- Coverage below any threshold: `node --test` exits non-zero after printing the per-file coverage table; no separate reporter is needed.
- security:headers: exit 2 for a missing argument, exit 1 listing each `"<header>: missing or invalid"` plus a non-2xx status line.

Hard blocks that `effects:'full'` cannot override (NO_WEBGL — the default when nothing is known, REDUCED_MOTION, SAVE_DATA, LOW_MEMORY <4 GB, LOW_CPU <4 cores); soft blocks it can (COARSE_POINTER, SMALL_VIEWPORT); unknown readings (null/undefined/0/'lots'/NaN) never block; exact boundary behaviour at MIN_DEVICE_MEMORY_GB=4 / MIN_LOGICAL_CORES=4; PAGE_HIDDEN reported separately and ranked below a real capability failure; an exhaustiveness test proving every BLOCK_REASONS value is reachable. readBrowserSignals: navigator.connection plus moz/webkit prefixes, absent APIs report null not 0, a string '4' is not a number, `null` navigator survives. detectWebgl: webgl2 with exact options `{antialias:false, powerPreference:'default'}`, webgl1 fallback, false on no context, false instead of throwing when createElement throws.
